    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useCallback } from "react";
    4: 
    5: import {
    6:   type FormEvent,
    7:   useEffect,
    8:   useMemo,
    9:   useState,
   10: } from "react";
   11: import Link from "next/link";
   12: import toast from "react-hot-toast";
   13: 
   14: import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
   15: import Skeleton from "@/components/ui/Skeleton";
   16: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
   17: 
   18: import {
   19:   createTeacherClass,
   20:   getTeacherClasses,
   21:   type TeacherClass,
   22: } from "@/services/classService";
   23: 
   24: import type {
   25:   ExamBoard,
   26:   Qualification,
   27: } from "@/types/user";
   28: 
   29: import {
   30:   getUnifiedTeacherAssignments,
   31:   type UnifiedTeacherAssignment,
   32: } from "@/services/unifiedTeacherAssignmentService";
   33: 
   34: type StatusFilter =
   35:   | "active"
   36:   | "archived"
   37:   | "all";
   38: 
   39: function defaultAcademicYear(): string {
   40:   const now = new Date();
   41: 
   42:   const year =
   43:     now.getFullYear();
   44: 
   45:   /*
   46:    * Treat August onward as preparation for the new UK academic year.
   47:    */
   48:   const startYear =
   49:     now.getMonth() >= 7
   50:       ? year
   51:       : year - 1;
   52: 
   53:   return `${startYear}/${startYear + 1}`;
   54: }
   55: 
   56: function qualificationLabel(
   57:   qualification:
   58:     Qualification | "",
   59: ): string {
   60:   if (
   61:     qualification ===
   62:     "A_LEVEL"
   63:   ) {
   64:     return "A Level";
   65:   }
   66: 
   67:   return qualification || "Not set";
   68: }
   69: 
   70: export default function TeacherClassesPage() {
   71:   const {
   72:     user,
   73:     profile,
   74:     loading: authLoading,
   75:     profileReady,
   76:   } = useAuth();
   77: 
   78:   const [classes, setClasses] =
   79:     useState<
   80:       TeacherClass[]
   81:     >([]);
   82: 
   83:   const [
   84:     unifiedAssignments,
   85:     setUnifiedAssignments,
   86:   ] = useState<
   87:     UnifiedTeacherAssignment[]
   88:   >([]);
   89: 
   90:   const [
   91:     loadingClasses,
   92:     setLoadingClasses,
   93:   ] = useState(true);
   94: 
   95:   const [
   96:     showForm,
   97:     setShowForm,
   98:   ] = useState(false);
   99: 
  100:   const [
  101:     className,
  102:     setClassName,
  103:   ] = useState("");
  104: 
  105:   const [
  106:     yearGroup,
  107:     setYearGroup,
  108:   ] = useState("");
  109: 
  110:   const [subject, setSubject] =
  111:     useState(
  112:       "Computer Science",
  113:     );
  114: 
  115:   const [
  116:     academicYear,
  117:     setAcademicYear,
  118:   ] = useState(
  119:     defaultAcademicYear(),
  120:   );
  121: 
  122:   const [
  123:     qualification,
  124:     setQualification,
  125:   ] =
  126:     useState<
  127:       Qualification | ""
  128:     >("GCSE");
  129: 
  130:   const [
  131:     examBoard,
  132:     setExamBoard,
  133:   ] =
  134:     useState<
  135:       ExamBoard | ""
  136:     >("OCR");
  137: 
  138:   const [
  139:     submitting,
  140:     setSubmitting,
  141:   ] = useState(false);
  142: 
  143:   const [
  144:     searchTerm,
  145:     setSearchTerm,
  146:   ] = useState("");
  147: 
  148:   const [
  149:     statusFilter,
  150:     setStatusFilter,
  151:   ] =
  152:     useState<StatusFilter>(
  153:       "active",
  154:     );
  155: 
  156:   const loadClasses = useCallback(() => {
  157:     if (!user?.uid) {
  158:       return Promise.resolve().then(() => {
  159:         setClasses([]);
  160:         setUnifiedAssignments([]);
  161:         setLoadingClasses(false);
  162:       });
  163:     }
  164: 
  165:     const teacherId = user.uid;
  166: 
  167:     return Promise.resolve()
  168:       .then(() => {
  169:         setLoadingClasses(true);
  170: 
  171:         return Promise.all([
  172:           getTeacherClasses(
  173:             teacherId,
  174:           ),
  175:           getUnifiedTeacherAssignments(
  176:             teacherId,
  177:           ),
  178:         ]);
  179:       })
  180:       .then(([
  181:         loaded,
  182:         assignmentSummary,
  183:       ]) => {
  184:         setClasses(
  185:           loaded,
  186:         );
  187: 
  188:         setUnifiedAssignments(
  189:           assignmentSummary.assignments,
  190:         );
  191:       })
  192:       .catch((error) => {
  193:         console.error(
  194:           "Failed to load classes:",
  195:           error,
  196:         );
  197: 
  198:         toast.error(
  199:           "Could not load your classes.",
  200:         );
  201:       })
  202:       .finally(() => {
  203:         setLoadingClasses(
  204:           false,
  205:         );
  206:       });
  207:   }, [user]);
  208: 
  209:   useEffect(() => {
  210:     if (
  211:       authLoading ||
  212:       !profileReady
  213:     ) {
  214:       return;
  215:     }
  216: 
  217:     void loadClasses();
  218:   }, [
  219:     authLoading,
  220:     profileReady,
  221:     loadClasses,
  222:   ]);
  223: 
  224:   const visibleClasses =
  225:     useMemo(() => {
  226:       const search =
  227:         searchTerm
  228:           .trim()
  229:           .toLowerCase();
  230: 
  231:       return classes.filter(
  232:         (item) => {
  233:           const matchesStatus =
  234:             statusFilter ===
  235:               "all" ||
  236:             item.status ===
  237:               statusFilter;
  238: 
  239:           if (
  240:             !matchesStatus
  241:           ) {
  242:             return false;
  243:           }
  244: 
  245:           if (!search) {
  246:             return true;
  247:           }
  248: 
  249:           return [
  250:             item.name,
  251:             item.subject,
  252:             item.yearGroup,
  253:             item.academicYear,
  254:             item.qualification,
  255:             item.examBoard,
  256:           ].some(
  257:             (value) =>
  258:               value
  259:                 .toLowerCase()
  260:                 .includes(
  261:                   search,
  262:                 ),
  263:           );
  264:         },
  265:       );
  266:     }, [
  267:       classes,
  268:       searchTerm,
  269:       statusFilter,
  270:     ]);
  271: 
  272:   const activeClasses =
  273:     classes.filter(
  274:       (item) =>
  275:         item.status ===
  276:         "active",
  277:     );
  278: 
  279:   const totalStudents =
  280:     activeClasses.reduce(
  281:       (
  282:         total,
  283:         item,
  284:       ) =>
  285:         total +
  286:         item.studentIds.length,
  287:       0,
  288:     );
  289: 
  290:   const activeClassIds =
  291:     new Set(
  292:       activeClasses.map(
  293:         (item) => item.id,
  294:       ),
  295:     );
  296: 
  297:   const totalAssignments =
  298:     unifiedAssignments.filter(
  299:       (assignment) =>
  300:         activeClassIds.has(
  301:           assignment.classId,
  302:         ),
  303:     ).length;
  304: 
  305:   const assignmentCountByClass =
  306:     useMemo(() => {
  307:       const counts =
  308:         new Map<string, number>();
  309: 
  310:       unifiedAssignments.forEach(
  311:         (assignment) => {
  312:           counts.set(
  313:             assignment.classId,
  314:             (counts.get(
  315:               assignment.classId,
  316:             ) || 0) + 1,
  317:           );
  318:         },
  319:       );
  320: 
  321:       return counts;
  322:     }, [
  323:       unifiedAssignments,
  324:     ]);
  325: 
  326:   async function handleCreateClass(
  327:     event:
  328:       FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>,
  329:   ) {
  330:     event.preventDefault();
  331: 
  332:     if (!user?.uid) {
  333:       toast.error(
  334:         "You must be logged in as a teacher.",
  335:       );
  336:       return;
  337:     }
  338: 
  339:     if (
  340:       !profile?.schoolId
  341:     ) {
  342:       toast.error(
  343:         "Create or join your school organisation before creating classes.",
  344:       );
  345:       return;
  346:     }
  347: 
  348:     if (
  349:       !className.trim() ||
  350:       !yearGroup.trim() ||
  351:       !academicYear.trim() ||
  352:       !qualification ||
  353:       !examBoard
  354:     ) {
  355:       toast.error(
  356:         "Complete every required class field.",
  357:       );
  358:       return;
  359:     }
  360: 
  361:     try {
  362:       setSubmitting(true);
  363: 
  364:       await createTeacherClass({
  365:         name:
  366:           className,
  367: 
  368:         subject,
  369: 
  370:         yearGroup,
  371: 
  372:         academicYear,
  373: 
  374:         qualification,
  375: 
  376:         examBoard,
  377: 
  378:         teacherId:
  379:           user.uid,
  380: 
  381:         teacherName:
  382:           profile?.name ||
  383:           user.displayName ||
  384:           user.email ||
  385:           "Teacher",
  386: 
  387:         schoolId:
  388:           profile.schoolId,
  389:       });
  390: 
  391:       toast.success(
  392:         "Class created successfully.",
  393:       );
  394: 
  395:       setClassName("");
  396:       setYearGroup("");
  397:       setSubject(
  398:         "Computer Science",
  399:       );
  400:       setAcademicYear(
  401:         defaultAcademicYear(),
  402:       );
  403:       setQualification(

  405:       );
  406:       setExamBoard(
  407:         "OCR",
  408:       );
  409:       setShowForm(false);
  410:

  412:     } catch (error) {
  413:       console.error(
  414:         "Create class error:",
  415:         error,

  417: 
  418:       toast.error(
  419:         error instanceof Error
  420:           ? error.message
  421:           : "Could not create the class.",
  422:       );
  423:     } finally {
  424:       setSubmitting(false);
  425:     }
  426:   }
  427: 
  428:   if (
  429:     authLoading ||
  430:     !profileReady ||
  431:     loadingClasses

  433:     return (

  435:         <Skeleton className="h-52 w-full" />

  438:           <Skeleton className="h-28" />

  440:           <Skeleton className="h-28" />
  441:         </div>
  442: 
  443:         <Skeleton className="h-96 w-full" />
  444:       </div>
  445:     );
  446:   }
  447: 
  448:   return (
  449:     <div className="space-y-8">
  450:       <Card className="border-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-700 via-teal-700 to-cyan-700 text-white">
  451:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
  452:           <div>

  454:               Teacher Portal

  458:               Manage Classes
  459:             </h1>

  462:               Create school-scoped teaching groups, manage curriculum settings,

  464:             </p>
  465:           </div>

  471:             >
  472:               School

  475:             <button
  476:               type="button"
  477:               onClick={() =>
  478:                 setShowForm(
  479:                   (current) =>
  480:                     !current,

  484:             >
  485:               {showForm

  492:

  609:               >

  770:                         </span>
  771:

  773:                           "archived" && (

  777:                         )}

  784:                       <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
  785:                         {classItem.subject ||
  786:                           "Computer Science"}{" "}

  788:                         {classItem.academicYear ||
  789:                           "Academic year not set"}
  790:                       </p>
  791:                     </div>
  792: 
  793:                     <Link
  794:                       href={`/teacher/classes/${classItem.id}`}
  795:                       className="shrink-0 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-blue-700"
  796:                     >
  797:                       Open class
  798:                     </Link>
  799:                   </div>
  800: 
  801:                   <div className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
  802:                     <MiniMetric
  803:                       label="Students"
  804:                       value={
  805:                         classItem
  806:                           .studentIds
  807:                           .length
  808:                       }
  809:                     />
  810: 
  811:                     <MiniMetric
  812:                       label="Assignments"
  813:                       value={
  814:                         assignmentCountByClass.get(
  815:                           classItem.id,
  816:                         ) || 0
  817:                       }
  818:                     />
  819:                   </div>
  820:                 </article>
  821:               ),
  822:             )}
  823:           </div>
  824:         )}
  825:       </Card>
  826:     </div>
  827:   );
  828: }
  829: 
  830: function Metric({
  831:   label,
  832:   value,
  833: }: {
  834:   label: string;
  835:   value: number;
  836: }) {
  837:   return (
  838:     <Card>
  839:       <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500">
  840:         {label}
  841:       </p>
  842: 
  843:       <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black text-slate-950">
  844:         {value}
  845:       </p>
  846:     </Card>
  847:   );
  848: }
  849: 
  850: function MiniMetric({
  851:   label,
  852:   value,
  853: }: {
  854:   label: string;
  855:   value: number;
  856: }) {
  857:   return (
  858:     <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-4">
  859:       <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
  860:         {label}
  861:       </p>
  862: 
  863:       <p className="mt-1 text-xl font-black text-slate-900">
  864:         {value}
  865:       </p>
  866:     </div>
  867:   );
  868: }
  869: 
  870: function Field({
  871:   label,
  872:   value,
  873:   onChange,
  874:   placeholder,
  875: }: {
  876:   label: string;
  877:   value: string;
  878:   onChange: (
  879:     value: string,
  880:   ) => void;
  881:   placeholder: string;
  882: }) {
  883:   return (
  884:     <label>
  885:       <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700">
  886:         {label}
  887:       </span>
  888: 
  889:       <input
  890:         type="text"
  891:         value={value}
  892:         onChange={(
  893:           event,
  894:         ) =>
  895:           onChange(
  896:             event.target.value,
  897:           )
  898:         }
  899:         placeholder={
  900:           placeholder
  901:         }
  902:         className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3"
  903:       />
  904:     </label>
  905:   );
  906: }

   20: function formatDate(date: Date | null): string {
   21:   if (!date) {
   22:     return "No date";
   23:   }
   24: 
   25:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
   26:     day: "numeric",
   27:     month: "short",
   28:     year: "numeric",
   29:   }).format(date);
   30: }
   31: 
   32: 
   33: function qualificationLabel(value: string): string {
   34:   if (value === "A_LEVEL") {
   35:     return "A Level";
   36:   }
   37: 
   38:   return value || "Not set";
   39: }
   40: 
   41: function getDueDateStatus(dueDate: Date | null): {
   42:   label: string;
   43:   className: string;
   44: } {
   45:   if (!dueDate) {
   46:     return {
   47:       label: "No due date",
   48:       className: "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700",
   49:     };
   50:   }
   51: 
   52:   const today = new Date();
   53:   const due = new Date(dueDate);
   54: 
   55:   today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
   56:   due.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
   57: 
   58:   const differenceInMilliseconds = due.getTime() - today.getTime();
   59: 
   60:   const differenceInDays = Math.ceil(
   61:     differenceInMilliseconds / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24),
   62:   );
   63: 
   64:   if (differenceInDays < 0) {
   65:     return {
   66:       label: "Overdue",
   67:       className: "bg-red-100 text-red-700",
   68:     };
   69:   }
   70: 
   71:   if (differenceInDays === 0) {
   72:     return {
   73:       label: "Due today",
   74:       className: "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700",
   75:     };
   76:   }
   77: 
   78:   if (differenceInDays === 1) {
   79:     return {
   80:       label: "Due tomorrow",
   81:       className: "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700",
   82:     };
   83:   }
   84: 
   85:   return {
   86:     label: `Due in ${differenceInDays} days`,
   87:     className: "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700",
   88:   };
   89: }
   90: 
   91: function calculateCompletionPercentage(
   92:   assignment: UnifiedTeacherAssignment,
   93: ): number {
   94:   return assignment.completionPercentage;
   95: }

   97: export default function ClassDetailsPage() {
   98:   const params = useParams<{
   99:     classId: string;
  100:   }>();
  101: 
  102:   const router = useRouter();
  103:   const { user } = useAuth();
  104: 
  105:   const classId = params.classId;
  106: 
  107:   const [teacherClass, setTeacherClass] = useState<TeacherClass | null>(null);
  108: 
  109:   const [assignments, setAssignments] = useState<UnifiedTeacherAssignment[]>([]);
  110: 
  111:   const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<ClassTab>("overview");
  112: 
  113:   const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  114: 
  115:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
  116: 
  117:   useEffect(() => {
  118:     async function loadClassDetails() {
  119:       if (!user?.uid || !classId) {
  120:         return;
  121:       }
  122: 
  123:       try {
  124:         setIsLoading(true);
  125:         setError("");
  126: 
  127:         const classRecord = await getTeacherClassById(classId);
  128: 
  129:         if (!classRecord) {
  130:           setError("This class could not be found.");
  131:           return;
  132:         }
  133: 
  134:         if (classRecord.teacherId !== user.uid) {
  135:           setError("You do not have permission to view this class.");
  136:           return;
  137:         }
  138: 
  139:         const assignmentSummary =
  140:           await getUnifiedTeacherAssignments(user.uid);
  141: 
  142:         const matchingAssignments =
  143:           assignmentSummary.assignments.filter(
  144:             (assignment) => assignment.classId === classId,
  145:           );
  146: 
  147:         setTeacherClass(classRecord);
  148:         setAssignments(matchingAssignments);
  149:       } catch (loadError: unknown) {
  150:         const firebaseCode =
  151:           typeof loadError === "object" &&
  152:           loadError !== null &&
  153:           "code" in loadError &&
  154:           typeof (loadError as { code?: unknown }).code === "string"
  155:             ? (loadError as { code: string }).code
  156:             : "";
  157: 
  158:         if (
  159:           firebaseCode === "permission-denied" ||
  160:           firebaseCode === "firestore/permission-denied"
  161:         ) {
  162:           setError("You do not have permission to view this class.");
  163:           return;
  164:         }
  165: 
  166:         setError(
  167:           loadError instanceof Error
  168:             ? loadError.message
  169:             : "Unable to load this class.",
  170:         );
  171:       } finally {
  172:         setIsLoading(false);
  173:       }
  174:     }
  175: 
  176:     void loadClassDetails();
  177:   }, [classId, user?.uid]);
  178: 
  179:   const students = useMemo<ClassStudent[]>(() => {
  180:     return teacherClass?.students ?? [];
  181:   }, [teacherClass]);
  182: 
  183:   const activeAssignments = useMemo(() => {
  184:     return assignments.filter((assignment) => assignment.status === "active");
  185:   }, [assignments]);
  186: 
  187:   const totalCompletions = useMemo(() => {
  188:     return assignments.reduce(
  189:       (total, assignment) => total + assignment.completedCount,
  190:       0,
  191:     );
  192:   }, [assignments]);
  193: 
  194:   const totalPossibleCompletions = useMemo(() => {
  195:     return assignments.reduce(
  196:       (total, assignment) => total + assignment.studentCount,
  197:       0,
  198:     );
  199:   }, [assignments]);
  200: 
  201:   const overallCompletionPercentage =
  202:     totalPossibleCompletions > 0
  203:       ? Math.round((totalCompletions / totalPossibleCompletions) * 100)
  204:       : 0;
  205: 
  206:   if (isLoading) {
  207:     return (
  208:       <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 p-6 lg:p-10">
  209:         <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl">
  210:           <div className="animate-pulse space-y-6">
  211:             <div className="h-8 w-48 rounded bg-slate-200" />
  212: 
  213:             <div className="h-48 rounded-3xl bg-slate-200" />
  214: 
  215:             <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-4">
  216:               {Array.from({
  217:                 length: 4,
  218:               }).map((_, index) => (
  219:                 <div key={index} className="h-32 rounded-2xl bg-slate-200" />
  220:               ))}
  221:             </div>
  222:           </div>
  223:         </div>
  224:       </main>
  225:     );
  226:   }
  227: 
  228:   if (error || !teacherClass) {
  229:     return (
  230:       <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 p-6 lg:p-10">
  231:         <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl">
  232:           <button

  635: function AssignmentsTab({

  683: function AnalyticsTab({
  684:   assignments,
  685:   overallCompletionPercentage,
  686:   totalCompletions,
  687:   totalPossibleCompletions,
  688: }: {
  689:   assignments: UnifiedTeacherAssignment[];
  690:   overallCompletionPercentage: number;
  691:   totalCompletions: number;
  692:   totalPossibleCompletions: number;
  693: }) {
  694:   return (
  695:     <div className="space-y-6">
  696:       <section className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
  697:         <StatCard
  698:           label="Overall completion"
  699:           value={`${overallCompletionPercentage}%`}
  700:           description="Across every assignment"
  701:         />
  702: 
  703:         <StatCard
  704:           label="Student completions"
  705:           value={totalCompletions}
  706:           description={`Out of ${totalPossibleCompletions} expected`}
  707:         />
  708: 
  709:         <StatCard
  710:           label="Resources assigned"
  711:           value={assignments.length}
  712:           description="Total class assignments"
  713:         />
  714:       </section>
  715: 
  716:       <section className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
  717:         <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
  718:           Assignment performance
  719:         </h2>
  720: 
  721:         <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
  722:           Completion rates across lessons/resources, programming, quizzes and exams assigned to this class.
  723:         </p>
  724: 
  725:         {assignments.length === 0 ? (
  726:           <EmptyState
  727:             title="No analytics available"
  728:             description="Analytics will appear after assignments have been created for this class."
  729:           />
  730:         ) : (
  731:           <div className="mt-6 space-y-5">
  732:             {assignments.map((assignment) => {
  733:               const percentage = calculateCompletionPercentage(assignment);
  734: 
  735:               return (
  736:                 <div key={assignment.id}>
  737:                   <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
  738:                     <div>
  739:                       <p className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
  740:                         {assignment.title}
  741:                       </p>
  742: 
  743:                       <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
  744:                         {assignment.completedCount} of {assignment.studentCount}{" "}
  745:                         completed
  746:                       </p>
  747:                     </div>
  748: 
  749:                     <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">
  750:                       {percentage}%
  751:                     </span>
  752:                   </div>
  753: 
  754:                   <div className="h-3 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-200">
  755:                     <div
  756:                       className="h-full rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-sky-400 via-blue-500 to-indigo-500 transition-all"
  757:                       style={{
  758:                         width: `${percentage}%`,
  759:                       }}
  760:                     />
  761:                   </div>
  762:                 </div>
  763:               );
  764:             })}
  765:           </div>
  766:         )}
  767:       </section>
  768:     </div>
  769:   );
  770: }
  771: 
  772: function AssignmentSummaryCard({
  773:   assignment,
  774: }: {
  775:   assignment: UnifiedTeacherAssignment;
  776: }) {
  777:   const dueDateStatus = getDueDateStatus(assignment.dueDate);
  778: 
  779:   const completionPercentage = calculateCompletionPercentage(assignment);
  780: 
  781:   return (
  782:     <article className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
  783:       <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-start">
  784:         <div>
  785:           <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
  786:             {assignment.title}
  787:           </h3>
  788: 
  789:           <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
  790:             {assignment.topic ||
  791:               assignment.kind ||
  792:               "Teaching resource"}
  793:           </p>
  794:         </div>
  795: 
  796:         <span
  797:           className={`w-fit rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${dueDateStatus.className}`}
  798:         >
  799:           {dueDateStatus.label}
  800:         </span>
  801:       </div>
  802: 
  803:       <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
  804:         <span className="text-slate-500">
  805:           {assignment.completedCount} of {assignment.studentCount} completed
  806:         </span>
  807: 
  808:         <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">
  809:           {completionPercentage}%
  810:         </span>
  811:       </div>
  812:     </article>
  813:   );
  814: }

  816: function AssignmentDetailedCard({
  817:   assignment,
  818: }: {
  819:   assignment: UnifiedTeacherAssignment;
  820: }) {
  821:   const dueDateStatus = getDueDateStatus(assignment.dueDate);

  835:               {assignment.status}
  836:             </span>
  837:           </div>
  838: 
  839:           <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
  840:             {assignment.topic ||
  841:               assignment.kind ||
  842:               "Teaching resource"}
  843:           </p>

  846:             <p className="mt-3 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
  847:               {assignment.description}
  848:             </p>

  851:

  856:             {dueDateStatus.label}
  857:           </span>

  861:           </span>
  862:         </div>
  863:       </div>
  864: 
  865:       <div className="mt-5">
  866:         <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
  867:           <span className="text-slate-500">
  868:             {assignment.completedCount} of {assignment.studentCount} students
  869:             completed
  870:           </span>
  871: 
  872:           <span className="font-bold text-slate-800">
  873:             {completionPercentage}%
  874:           </span>
  875:         </div>
  876: 
  877:         <div className="h-3 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-200">
  878:           <div
  879:             className="h-full rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-sky-400 via-blue-500 to-indigo-500"
  880:             style={{
  881:               width: `${completionPercentage}%`,
  882:             }}
  883:           />
  884:         </div>
  885:       </div>
  886:     </article>
  887:   );
  888: }
  889: 
  890: function StudentCard({ student }: { student: ClassStudent }) {
  891:   return (
  892:     <article className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
  893:       <StudentAvatar name={student.displayName} />
  894: 
  895:       <div className="min-w-0">
  896:         <p className="truncate font-semibold text-slate-900">
  897:           {student.displayName || "Student"}
  898:         </p>
  899: 
  900:         <p className="truncate text-sm text-slate-500">
  901:           {student.email || "No email"}
  902:         </p>
  903:       </div>
  904:     </article>
  905:   );
  906: }
  907: 
  908: function StudentAvatar({ name }: { name: string }) {
  909:   const initials =
  910:     name
  911:       ?.split(" ")
  912:       .filter(Boolean)
  913:       .slice(0, 2)
  914:       .map((part) => part.charAt(0).toUpperCase())
  915:       .join("") || "S";
  916: 
  917:   return (
  918:     <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-blue-100 text-sm font-bold text-blue-700">
  919:       {initials}
  920:     </div>
  921:   );
  922: }
  923: 
  924: function InformationRow({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  925:   return (
  926:     <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-100 pb-3 last:border-0 last:pb-0">
  927:       <dt className="text-sm text-slate-500">{label}</dt>
  928: 
  929:       <dd className="text-right text-sm font-semibold capitalize text-slate-900">
  930:         {value}
  931:       </dd>
  932:     </div>
  933:   );
  934: }
  935: 
  936: function EmptyState({
  937:   title,
  938:   description,
  939: }: {
  940:   title: string;
  941:   description: string;
  942: }) {
  943:   return (
  944:     <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-6 py-10 text-center">
  945:       <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{title}</h3>
  946: 
  947:       <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-md text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
  948:         {description}
  949:       </p>
  950:     </div>
  951:   );
  952: }

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useState } from "react";
    4: import toast from "react-hot-toast";
    5: 
    6: import {
    7:   archiveTeacherClass,
    8:   deleteTeacherClass,
    9:   restoreTeacherClass,
   10:   updateTeacherClass,
   11:   type TeacherClass,
   12: } from "@/services/classService";
   13: 
   14: import type {
   15:   ExamBoard,
   16:   Qualification,
   17: } from "@/types/user";
   18: 
   19: type Props = {
   20:   teacherClass: TeacherClass;
   21: 
   22:   onUpdated: (
   23:     updatedClass:
   24:       TeacherClass,
   25:   ) => void;
   26: 
   27:   onDeleted: () => void;
   28: };
   29: 
   30: export default function ClassSettingsPanel({
   31:   teacherClass,
   32:   onUpdated,
   33:   onDeleted,
   34: }: Props) {
   35:   const [name, setName] =
   36:     useState(
   37:       teacherClass.name,
   38:     );
   39: 
   40:   const [subject, setSubject] =
   41:     useState(
   42:       teacherClass.subject,
   43:     );
   44: 
   45:   const [
   46:     yearGroup,
   47:     setYearGroup,
   48:   ] = useState(
   49:     teacherClass.yearGroup,
   50:   );
   51: 
   52:   const [
   53:     academicYear,
   54:     setAcademicYear,
   55:   ] = useState(
   56:     teacherClass.academicYear,
   57:   );
   58: 
   59:   const [
   60:     qualification,
   61:     setQualification,
   62:   ] =
   63:     useState<
   64:       Qualification | ""
   65:     >(
   66:       teacherClass.qualification,
   67:     );
   68: 
   69:   const [
   70:     examBoard,
   71:     setExamBoard,
   72:   ] =
   73:     useState<
   74:       ExamBoard | ""
   75:     >(
   76:       teacherClass.examBoard,
   77:     );
   78: 
   79:   const [saving, setSaving] =
   80:     useState(false);
   81: 
   82:   const [
   83:     changingStatus,
   84:     setChangingStatus,
   85:   ] = useState(false);
   86: 
   87:   const [
   88:     deleting,
   89:     setDeleting,
   90:   ] = useState(false);
   91: 
   92:   async function save() {
   93:     if (
   94:       !name.trim() ||
   95:       !yearGroup.trim() ||
   96:       !academicYear.trim() ||
   97:       !qualification ||
   98:       !examBoard
   99:     ) {
  100:       toast.error(
  101:         "Complete the class name, year group, academic year, qualification and exam board.",
  102:       );
  103: 
  104:       return;
  105:     }
  106: 
  107:     try {
  108:       setSaving(true);
  109: 
  110:       await updateTeacherClass(
  111:         teacherClass.id,
  112:         {
  113:           name,
  114:           subject,
  115:           yearGroup,
  116:           academicYear,
  117:           qualification,
  118:           examBoard,
  119:         },
  120:       );
  121: 
  122:       onUpdated({
  123:         ...teacherClass,
  124: 
  125:         name:
  126:           name
  127:             .trim()
  128:             .replace(
  129:               /\s+/g,
  130:               " ",
  131:             ),
  132: 
  133:         subject:
  134:           subject.trim() ||
  135:           "Computer Science",
  136: 
  137:         yearGroup:
  138:           yearGroup.trim(),
  139: 
  140:         academicYear:
  141:           academicYear.trim(),
  142: 
  143:         qualification,
  144: 
  145:         examBoard,
  146:       });
  147: 
  148:       toast.success(
  149:         "Class details updated.",
  150:       );
  151:     } catch (error) {
  152:       console.error(
  153:         "Unable to update class:",
  154:         error,
  155:       );
  156: 
  157:       toast.error(
  158:         error instanceof Error
  159:           ? error.message
  160:           : "The class could not be updated.",
  161:       );
  162:     } finally {
  163:       setSaving(false);
  164:     }
  165:   }
  166: 
  167:   async function toggleArchive() {
  168:     try {
  169:       setChangingStatus(
  170:         true,
  171:       );
  172: 
  173:       if (
  174:         teacherClass.status ===
  175:         "active"
  176:       ) {
  177:         await archiveTeacherClass(
  178:           teacherClass.id,
  179:         );
  180: 
  181:         onUpdated({
  182:           ...teacherClass,
  183:           status:
  184:             "archived",
  185:         });
  186: 
  187:         toast.success(
  188:           "Class archived.",
  189:         );
  190:       } else {
  191:         await restoreTeacherClass(
  192:           teacherClass.id,
  193:         );
  194: 
  195:         onUpdated({
  196:           ...teacherClass,
  197:           status:
  198:             "active",
  199:         });
  200: 
  201:         toast.success(
  202:           "Class restored.",
  203:         );
  204:       }
  205:     } catch (error) {
  206:       console.error(
  207:         "Unable to change class status:",
  208:         error,
  209:       );
  210: 
  211:       toast.error(
  212:         error instanceof Error
  213:           ? error.message
  214:           : "The class status could not be changed.",
  215:       );
  216:     } finally {
  217:       setChangingStatus(
  218:         false,
  219:       );
  220:     }
  221:   }
  222: 
  223:   async function removePermanently() {
  224:     const confirmed =
  225:       window.confirm(
  226:         "Permanently delete this empty class? This cannot be undone. Classes with students or assignment history cannot be deleted.",
  227:       );
  228: 
  229:     if (!confirmed) {
  230:       return;
  231:     }
  232: 
  233:     try {
  234:       setDeleting(true);
  235: 
  236:       await deleteTeacherClass(
  237:         teacherClass.id,
  238:       );
  239: 
  240:       toast.success(
  241:         "Class deleted.",
  242:       );
  243: 
  244:       onDeleted();
  245:     } catch (error) {
  246:       console.error(
  247:         "Unable to delete class:",
  248:         error,
  249:       );
  250: 
  251:       toast.error(
  252:         error instanceof Error
  253:           ? error.message
  254:           : "The class could not be deleted.",
  255:       );
  256:     } finally {
  257:       setDeleting(false);
  258:     }
  259:   }
  260: 
  261:   return (
  262:     <div className="space-y-6">
  263:       <section className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
  264:         <div>
  265:           <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-teal-600">
  266:             Class settings
  267:           </p>
  268: 
  269:           <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
  270:             Teaching group details
  271:           </h2>
  272: 
  273:           <p className="mt-2 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
  274:             Keep the class identity, curriculum and academic-year information accurate.
  275:             Existing student progress and assignments are not changed when these details
  276:             are edited.
  277:           </p>
  278:         </div>
  279: 
  280:         <div className="mt-6 grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
  281:           <Field
  282:             label="Class name"
  283:             value={name}
  284:             onChange={
  285:               setName
  286:             }
  287:             placeholder="e.g. Year 11 Computer Science"
  288:           />
  289: 
  290:           <Field
  291:             label="Subject"
  292:             value={subject}
  293:             onChange={
  294:               setSubject
  295:             }
  296:             placeholder="Computer Science"
  297:           />
  298: 
  299:           <Field
  300:             label="Year group"
  301:             value={
  302:               yearGroup
  303:             }
  304:             onChange={
  305:               setYearGroup
  306:             }
  307:             placeholder="e.g. Year 11"
  308:           />
  309: 
  310:           <Field
  311:             label="Academic year"
  312:             value={
  313:               academicYear
  314:             }
  315:             onChange={
  316:               setAcademicYear
  317:             }
  318:             placeholder="e.g. 2026/2027"
  319:           />
  320: 
  321:           <label>
  322:             <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700">
  323:               Qualification
  324:             </span>
  325: 
  326:             <select
  327:               value={
  328:                 qualification
  329:               }
  330:               onChange={(
  331:                 event,
  332:               ) =>
  333:                 setQualification(
  334:                   event.target
  335:                     .value as
  336:                     | Qualification
  337:                     | "",
  338:                 )
  339:               }
  340:               className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-teal-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-teal-100"
  341:             >

  361:             <select
  362:               value={
  363:                 examBoard
  364:               }
  365:               onChange={(
  366:                 event,
  367:               ) =>
  368:                 setExamBoard(

  370:                     .value as

  373:                 )

  376:             >
  377:               <option value="">
  378:                 Select exam board
  379:               </option>
  380: 
  381:               <option value="AQA">
  382:                 AQA
  383:               </option>
  384: 
  385:               <option value="OCR">
  386:                 OCR
  387:               </option>
  388: 
  389:               <option value="EDEXCEL">
  390:                 Pearson Edexcel
  391:               </option>
  392:             </select>
  393:           </label>
  394:         </div>
  395: 
  396:         <button
  397:           type="button"
  398:           onClick={() =>
  399:             void save()
  400:           }
  401:           disabled={
  402:             saving
  403:           }
  404:           className="mt-6 rounded-xl bg-teal-600 px-6 py-3 font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-teal-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
  405:         >
  406:           {saving
  407:             ? "Saving..."
  408:             : "Save class details"}
  409:         </button>
  410:       </section>
  411: 
  412:       <section className="rounded-3xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-6">
  413:         <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-amber-700">
  414:           Class lifecycle
  415:         </p>
  416: 
  417:         <h2 className="mt-2 text-xl font-black text-amber-950">
  418:           {teacherClass.status ===
  419:           "active"
  420:             ? "Archive this class"
  421:             : "Restore this class"}
  422:         </h2>
  423: 
  424:         <p className="mt-2 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-6 text-amber-800">
  425:           Archiving keeps student membership, assignment history and analytics intact
  426:           while removing the class from the normal active-class workflow.
  427:         </p>
  428: 
  429:         <button
  430:           type="button"
  431:           onClick={() =>
  432:             void toggleArchive()
  433:           }
  434:           disabled={
  435:             changingStatus
  436:           }
  437:           className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-amber-300 bg-white px-5 py-3 font-bold text-amber-800 transition hover:bg-amber-100 disabled:opacity-60"
  438:         >
  439:           {changingStatus
  440:             ? "Updating..."
  441:             : teacherClass.status ===
  442:                 "active"
  443:               ? "Archive class"
  444:               : "Restore class"}
  445:         </button>
  446:       </section>
  447: 
  448:       <section className="rounded-3xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-6">
  449:         <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-red-700">
  450:           Danger zone
  451:         </p>
  452: 
  453:         <h2 className="mt-2 text-xl font-black text-red-950">
  454:           Permanently delete an empty class
  455:         </h2>
  456: 
  457:         <p className="mt-2 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-6 text-red-800">
  458:           Permanent deletion is intentionally restricted. A class containing students
  459:           or assignment history must be archived instead.
  460:         </p>
  461: 
  462:         <button
  463:           type="button"
  464:           onClick={() =>
  465:             void removePermanently()
  466:           }
  467:           disabled={
  468:             deleting
  469:           }
  470:           className="mt-5 rounded-xl bg-red-600 px-5 py-3 font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-red-700 disabled:opacity-60"
  471:         >
  472:           {deleting
  473:             ? "Deleting..."
  474:             : "Delete empty class"}
  475:         </button>
  476:       </section>
  477:     </div>
  478:   );
  479: }
  480: 
  481: function Field({
  482:   label,
  483:   value,
  484:   onChange,
  485:   placeholder,
  486: }: {
  487:   label: string;
  488:   value: string;
  489:   onChange: (
  490:     value: string,
  491:   ) => void;
  492:   placeholder: string;
  493: }) {
  494:   return (
  495:     <label>
  496:       <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700">
  497:         {label}
  498:       </span>
  499: 
  500:       <input
  501:         type="text"
  502:         value={value}
  503:         onChange={(
  504:           event,
  505:         ) =>
  506:           onChange(
  507:             event.target.value,
  508:           )
  509:         }
  510:         placeholder={
  511:           placeholder
  512:         }
  513:         className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-teal-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-teal-100"
  514:       />
  515:     </label>
  516:   );
  517: }

   26: function getInitials(name: string): string {
   27:   return (
   28:     name
   29:       .split(" ")
   30:       .filter(Boolean)
   31:       .slice(0, 2)
   32:       .map((part) => part.charAt(0).toUpperCase())
   33:       .join("") || "S"
   34:   );
   35: }
   36: 
   37: function formatCourse(
   38:   student: StudentDirectoryRecord,
   39: ): string {
   40:   const qualification =
   41:     student.qualification
   42:       .trim()
   43:       .toUpperCase();
   44: 
   45:   const examBoard =
   46:     student.examBoard
   47:       .trim()
   48:       .toUpperCase();
   49: 
   50:   if (qualification && examBoard) {
   51:     return `${qualification} Â· ${examBoard}`;
   52:   }
   53: 
   54:   return (
   55:     qualification ||
   56:     examBoard ||
   57:     "Course not selected"
   58:   );
   59: }

   61: export default function ClassStudentsManager({

   67:

   72:     useState(false);

   78:     StudentDirectoryRecord[]

  148:

  155:         ),
  156:       [availableStudents],

  158:

  164:           studentId,
  165:         ),
  166:     );
  167: 
  168:   useEffect(() => {
  169:     if (!isModalOpen) {
  170:       return;
  171:     }
  172:

  186: 
  187:         setDirectoryStudents(
  188:           loadedStudents,
  189:         );

  193:           error,
  194:         );

  197:           "Unable to load your school's student directory.",
  198:         );
  199:       } finally {
  200:         setIsLoadingDirectory(false);
  201:       }
  202:     }
  203: 
  204:     void loadDirectory();
  205:   }, [
  206:     isModalOpen,
  207:     schoolId,
  208:   ]);
  209: 
  210:   function openModal() {

  213:       new Set(),

  217:

  230:   function toggleStudent(
  231:     studentId: string,
  232:   ) {
  233:     setSelectedStudentIds(
  234:       (current) => {
  235:         const next =
  236:           new Set(current);
  237: 
  238:         if (
  239:           next.has(studentId)

  246:             studentId,
  247:           );
  248:         }
  249: 
  250:         return next;
  251:       },
  252:     );
  253:   }
  254: 
  255:   function toggleAllVisible() {

  257:       (current) => {

  259:           new Set(current);
  260: 
  261:         if (
  262:           allVisibleSelected

  264:           visibleStudentIds.forEach(
  265:             (studentId) =>
  266:               next.delete(
  267:                 studentId,
  268:               ),
  269:           );
  270:         } else {
  271:           visibleStudentIds.forEach(
  272:             (studentId) =>
  273:               next.add(
  274:                 studentId,
  275:               ),
  276:           );
  277:         }
  278: 
  279:         return next;
  280:       },
  281:     );

  284:   async function enrolSelectedStudents() {

  350:       );

  376:     }

  107: function convertTimestamp(
  108:   value?: Timestamp,
  109: ): Date | null {
  110:   return value?.toDate
  111:     ? value.toDate()
  112:     : null;
  113: }
  114: 
  115: function normaliseClassName(
  116:   value: string,
  117: ): string {
  118:   return value
  119:     .trim()
  120:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  121: }
  122: 
  123: function normaliseString(
  124:   value: unknown,
  125: ): string {
  126:   return typeof value ===
  127:     "string"
  128:     ? value.trim()
  129:     : "";
  130: }
  131: 
  132: function normaliseEmail(
  133:   value: unknown,
  134: ): string {
  135:   return normaliseString(
  136:     value,
  137:   ).toLowerCase();
  138: }
  139: 
  140: function normaliseQualification(
  141:   value: unknown,
  142: ): Qualification | "" {
  143:   return value === "GCSE" ||
  144:     value === "A_LEVEL"
  145:     ? value
  146:     : "";
  147: }
  148: 
  149: function normaliseExamBoard(
  150:   value: unknown,
  151: ): ExamBoard | "" {
  152:   return value === "AQA" ||
  153:     value === "OCR" ||
  154:     value === "EDEXCEL"
  155:     ? value
  156:     : "";
  157: }
  158: 
  159: function normaliseStringArray(
  160:   value: unknown,
  161: ): string[] {
  162:   return Array.isArray(value)
  163:     ? value.filter(
  164:         (
  165:           item,
  166:         ): item is string =>
  167:           typeof item === "string",
  168:       )
  169:     : [];
  170: }
  171: 
  172: function normaliseStudent(
  173:   student: ClassStudent,
  174: ): ClassStudent {
  175:   return {
  176:     studentId:
  177:       student.studentId.trim(),
  178: 
  179:     displayName:
  180:       student.displayName.trim() ||
  181:       "Unnamed Student",
  182: 
  183:     email:
  184:       student.email
  185:         .trim()
  186:         .toLowerCase(),
  187:   };
  188: }

  190: function convertClassDocument(
  191:   documentId: string,
  192:   data:
  193:     Partial<FirestoreTeacherClass>,
  194: ): TeacherClass {
  195:   return {
  196:     id:
  197:       documentId,
  198: 
  199:     name:
  200:       normaliseString(
  201:         data.name,
  202:       ) ||
  203:       "Untitled Class",
  204: 
  205:     subject:
  206:       normaliseString(
  207:         data.subject,
  208:       ) ||
  209:       "Computer Science",
  210: 
  211:     yearGroup:
  212:       normaliseString(
  213:         data.yearGroup,
  214:       ),
  215: 
  216:     academicYear:
  217:       normaliseString(
  218:         data.academicYear,
  219:       ),
  220: 
  221:     qualification:
  222:       normaliseQualification(
  223:         data.qualification,
  224:       ),
  225: 
  226:     examBoard:
  227:       normaliseExamBoard(
  228:         data.examBoard,
  229:       ),
  230: 
  231:     teacherId:
  232:       normaliseString(
  233:         data.teacherId,
  234:       ),
  235: 
  236:     teacherName:
  237:       normaliseString(
  238:         data.teacherName,
  239:       ),
  240: 
  241:     schoolId:
  242:       normaliseString(
  243:         data.schoolId,
  244:       ),
  245: 
  246:     studentIds:
  247:       normaliseStringArray(
  248:         data.studentIds,
  249:       ),
  250: 
  251:     students:
  252:       Array.isArray(
  253:         data.students,
  254:       )
  255:         ? data.students
  256:         : [],
  257: 
  258:     assignmentIds:
  259:       normaliseStringArray(
  260:         data.assignmentIds,
  261:       ),
  262: 
  263:     status:
  264:       data.status ===
  265:       "archived"
  266:         ? "archived"
  267:         : "active",
  268: 
  269:     createdAt:
  270:       convertTimestamp(
  271:         data.createdAt,
  272:       ),
  273: 
  274:     updatedAt:
  275:       convertTimestamp(
  276:         data.updatedAt,
  277:       ),
  278:   };
  279: }
  280: 
  281: /*
  282:  * Loads the freshest name/email directly from users/{studentId}.
  283:  * Embedded class student details remain a fallback.
  284:  */
  285: async function hydrateClassStudents(
  286:   teacherClass:
  287:     TeacherClass,
  288: ): Promise<TeacherClass> {
  289:   const embeddedById =
  290:     new Map(
  291:       (
  292:         teacherClass.students ??
  293:         []
  294:       ).map(
  295:         (student) => [
  296:           student.studentId,
  297:           student,
  298:         ],
  299:       ),
  300:     );
  301: 
  302:   const studentIds =
  303:     Array.from(
  304:       new Set(
  305:         [
  306:           ...teacherClass.studentIds,
  307: 
  308:           ...(
  309:             teacherClass.students ??
  310:             []
  311:           ).map(
  312:             (student) =>
  313:               student.studentId,
  314:           ),
  315:         ]
  316:           .map(
  317:             (studentId) =>
  318:               studentId.trim(),
  319:           )
  320:           .filter(Boolean),
  321:       ),
  322:     );
  323: 
  324:   if (
  325:     studentIds.length === 0
  326:   ) {
  327:     return {
  328:       ...teacherClass,
  329:       studentIds: [],
  330:       students: [],
  331:     };
  332:   }
  333: 
  334:   const hydrated =
  335:     await Promise.all(
  336:       studentIds.map(
  337:         async (
  338:           studentId,
  339:         ): Promise<ClassStudent> => {
  340:           const embedded =
  341:             embeddedById.get(
  342:               studentId,
  343:             );
  344: 
  345:           try {
  346:             const snapshot =
  347:               await getDoc(
  348:                 doc(
  349:                   db,
  350:                   "users",
  351:                   studentId,
  352:                 ),
  353:               );
  354: 
  355:             if (
  356:               !snapshot.exists()
  357:             ) {
  358:               return {
  359:                 studentId,
  360:                 displayName:
  361:                   embedded
  362:                     ?.displayName ||
  363:                   "Unnamed Student",
  364:                 email:
  365:                   embedded
  366:                     ?.email ||
  367:                   "",
  368:               };
  369:             }
  370: 
  371:             const data =
  372:               snapshot.data() as
  373:                 FirestoreStudentProfile;
  374: 
  375:             return {
  376:               studentId,
  377: 
  378:               displayName:
  379:                 normaliseString(
  380:                   data.name,
  381:                 ) ||
  382:                 embedded
  383:                   ?.displayName ||
  384:                 "Unnamed Student",
  385: 
  386:               email:
  387:                 normaliseEmail(
  388:                   data.email,
  389:                 ) ||
  390:                 embedded
  391:                   ?.email ||
  392:                 "",
  393:             };
  394:           } catch (
  395:             error
  396:           ) {
  397:             console.warn(
  398:               `Unable to hydrate student profile ${studentId}:`,
  399:               error,
  400:             );
  401: 
  402:             return {
  403:               studentId,
  404:               displayName:
  405:                 embedded
  406:                   ?.displayName ||
  407:                 "Unnamed Student",
  408:               email:
  409:                 embedded?.email ||
  410:                 "",
  411:             };
  412:           }
  413:         },
  414:       ),
  415:     );
  416: 
  417:   return {
  418:     ...teacherClass,
  419:     studentIds,
  420:     students:
  421:       hydrated,
  422:   };
  423: }
  424: 
  425: export async function createTeacherClass(
  426:   input:
  427:     CreateClassInput,
  428: ): Promise<string> {
  429:   const className =
  430:     normaliseClassName(
  431:       input.name,
  432:     );
  433: 
  434:   if (!className) {
  435:     throw new Error(
  436:       "Enter a valid class name.",
  437:     );
  438:   }
  439: 
  440:   const teacherId =
  441:     input.teacherId.trim();
  442: 
  443:   if (!teacherId) {
  444:     throw new Error(
  445:       "A teacher account is required.",
  446:     );
  447:   }
  448: 
  449:   if (
  450:     !input.yearGroup.trim()
  451:   ) {
  452:     throw new Error(
  453:       "Select or enter a year group.",
  454:     );
  455:   }
  456: 
  457:   if (
  458:     !input.academicYear.trim()
  459:   ) {
  460:     throw new Error(
  461:       "Enter an academic year.",
  462:     );
  463:   }
  464: 
  465:   if (
  466:     !input.qualification
  467:   ) {
  468:     throw new Error(
  469:       "Select a qualification.",
  470:     );
  471:   }
  472: 
  473:   if (
  474:     !input.examBoard
  475:   ) {
  476:     throw new Error(
  477:       "Select an exam board.",
  478:     );
  479:   }
  480: 
  481:   const teacherReference =
  482:     doc(
  483:       db,
  484:       "users",
  485:       teacherId,
  486:     );
  487: 
  488:   const teacherSnapshot =
  489:     await getDoc(
  490:       teacherReference,
  491:     );
  492: 
  493:   if (
  494:     !teacherSnapshot.exists()
  495:   ) {
  496:     throw new Error(
  497:       "The teacher account could not be found.",
  498:     );
  499:   }
  500: 
  501:   const teacherData =
  502:     teacherSnapshot.data();
  503: 
  504:   const schoolId =
  505:     input.schoolId
  506:       ?.trim() ||
  507:     normaliseString(
  508:       teacherData.schoolId,
  509:     );
  510: 
  511:   if (!schoolId) {
  512:     throw new Error(
  513:       "Your teacher account must be linked to a school before creating classes.",
  514:     );
  515:   }
  516: 
  517:   const classReference =
  518:     await addDoc(
  519:       collection(
  520:         db,
  521:         "classes",
  522:       ),
  523:       {
  524:         name:
  525:           className,
  526: 
  527:         subject:
  528:           input.subject.trim() ||
  529:           "Computer Science",
  530: 
  531:         yearGroup:
  532:           input.yearGroup.trim(),
  533: 
  534:         academicYear:
  535:           input.academicYear.trim(),
  536: 
  537:         qualification:
  538:           input.qualification,
  539: 
  540:         examBoard:
  541:           input.examBoard,
  542: 
  543:         teacherId,
  544: 
  545:         teacherName:
  546:           input.teacherName
  547:             ?.trim() ||
  548:           normaliseString(
  549:             teacherData.name,
  550:           ) ||
  551:           "Teacher",
  552: 
  553:         schoolId,
  554: 
  555:         studentIds: [],
  556:         students: [],
  557:         assignmentIds: [],
  558: 
  559:         status:
  560:           "active",
  561: 
  562:         createdAt:
  563:           serverTimestamp(),
  564: 
  565:         updatedAt:
  566:           serverTimestamp(),
  567:       },
  568:     );
  569: 
  570:   return classReference.id;
  571: }
  572: 
  573: export async function getTeacherClasses(
  574:   teacherId: string,
  575: ): Promise<TeacherClass[]> {
  576:   const cleanedTeacherId =
  577:     teacherId.trim();
  578: 
  579:   if (!cleanedTeacherId) {
  580:     return [];
  581:   }
  582: 
  583:   const classesQuery =
  584:     query(
  585:       collection(
  586:         db,
  587:         "classes",
  588:       ),
  589:       where(
  590:         "teacherId",
  591:         "==",
  592:         cleanedTeacherId,
  593:       ),
  594:       orderBy(
  595:         "createdAt",
  596:         "desc",
  597:       ),
  598:     );
  599: 
  600:   const snapshot =
  601:     await getDocs(
  602:       classesQuery,
  603:     );
  604: 
  605:   return snapshot.docs.map(
  606:     (
  607:       classDocument,
  608:     ) =>
  609:       convertClassDocument(
  610:         classDocument.id,
  611:         classDocument.data() as
  612:           Partial<FirestoreTeacherClass>,
  613:       ),
  614:   );
  615: }
  616: 
  617: export async function getTeacherClassById(
  618:   classId: string,
  619: ): Promise<TeacherClass | null> {
  620:   const cleanedClassId =
  621:     classId.trim();
  622: 
  623:   if (!cleanedClassId) {
  624:     return null;
  625:   }
  626: 
  627:   const snapshot =
  628:     await getDoc(
  629:       doc(
  630:         db,
  631:         "classes",
  632:         cleanedClassId,
  633:       ),
  634:     );
  635: 
  636:   if (
  637:     !snapshot.exists()
  638:   ) {
  639:     return null;
  640:   }
  641: 
  642:   return hydrateClassStudents(
  643:     convertClassDocument(
  644:       snapshot.id,
  645:       snapshot.data() as
  646:         Partial<FirestoreTeacherClass>,
  647:     ),
  648:   );
  649: }
  650: 
  651: export type UpdateTeacherClassInput =
  652:   Partial<
  653:     Pick<
  654:       TeacherClass,
  655:       | "name"
  656:       | "subject"
  657:       | "yearGroup"
  658:       | "academicYear"
  659:       | "qualification"
  660:       | "examBoard"
  661:       | "status"
  662:     >
  663:   >;
  664: 
  665: export async function updateTeacherClass(
  666:   classId: string,
  667:   updates:
  668:     UpdateTeacherClassInput,
  669: ): Promise<void> {
  670:   const cleanedClassId =
  671:     classId.trim();
  672: 
  673:   if (!cleanedClassId) {
  674:     throw new Error(
  675:       "A valid class is required.",
  676:     );
  677:   }
  678: 
  679:   const updateData:
  680:     Record<
  681:       string,
  682:       unknown
  683:     > = {};
  684: 
  685:   if (
  686:     updates.name !==
  687:     undefined
  688:   ) {
  689:     const name =
  690:       normaliseClassName(
  691:         updates.name,
  692:       );
  693: 
  694:     if (!name) {
  695:       throw new Error(
  696:         "Enter a valid class name.",
  697:       );
  698:     }
  699: 
  700:     updateData.name =
  701:       name;
  702:   }
  703: 
  704:   if (
  705:     updates.subject !==
  706:     undefined
  707:   ) {
  708:     updateData.subject =
  709:       updates.subject.trim() ||
  710:       "Computer Science";
  711:   }
  712: 
  713:   if (
  714:     updates.yearGroup !==
  715:     undefined
  716:   ) {
  717:     if (
  718:       !updates.yearGroup.trim()
  719:     ) {
  720:       throw new Error(
  721:         "Enter a year group.",
  722:       );
  723:     }
  724: 
  725:     updateData.yearGroup =
  726:       updates.yearGroup.trim();
  727:   }
  728: 
  729:   if (
  730:     updates.academicYear !==
  731:     undefined
  732:   ) {
  733:     if (
  734:       !updates.academicYear.trim()
  735:     ) {
  736:       throw new Error(
  737:         "Enter an academic year.",
  738:       );
  739:     }
  740: 
  741:     updateData.academicYear =
  742:       updates.academicYear.trim();
  743:   }
  744: 
  745:   if (
  746:     updates.qualification !==
  747:     undefined
  748:   ) {
  749:     if (
  750:       updates.qualification !==
  751:         "GCSE" &&
  752:       updates.qualification !==
  753:         "A_LEVEL"
  754:     ) {
  755:       throw new Error(
  756:         "Select GCSE or A Level.",
  757:       );
  758:     }
  759: 
  760:     updateData.qualification =
  761:       updates.qualification;
  762:   }
  763: 
  764:   if (
  765:     updates.examBoard !==
  766:     undefined
  767:   ) {
  768:     if (
  769:       updates.examBoard !==
  770:         "AQA" &&
  771:       updates.examBoard !==
  772:         "OCR" &&
  773:       updates.examBoard !==
  774:         "EDEXCEL"
  775:     ) {
  776:       throw new Error(
  777:         "Select AQA, OCR or Edexcel.",
  778:       );
  779:     }
  780: 
  781:     updateData.examBoard =
  782:       updates.examBoard;
  783:   }
  784: 
  785:   if (
  786:     updates.status !==
  787:     undefined
  788:   ) {
  789:     updateData.status =
  790:       updates.status;
  791:   }
  792: 
  793:   const classReference =
  794:     doc(
  795:       db,
  796:       "classes",
  797:       cleanedClassId,
  798:     );
  799: 
  800:   const classSnapshot =
  801:     await getDoc(
  802:       classReference,
  803:     );
  804: 
  805:   if (
  806:     !classSnapshot.exists()
  807:   ) {
  808:     throw new Error(
  809:       "The class could not be found.",
  810:     );
  811:   }
  812: 
  813:   const existingClass =
  814:     classSnapshot.data() as
  815:       Partial<FirestoreTeacherClass>;
  816: 
  817:   /*
  818:    * T1A -> T1B compatibility:
  819:    * legacy classes created before school tenancy may still lack schoolId.
  820:    * Adopt the owning teacher's current school during the first settings
  821:    * update so the tenant security rule remains satisfied.
  822:    */
  823:   if (
  824:     !normaliseString(
  825:       existingClass.schoolId,
  826:     )
  827:   ) {
  828:     const ownerId =
  829:       normaliseString(
  830:         existingClass.teacherId,
  831:       );
  832: 
  833:     if (ownerId) {
  834:       const ownerSnapshot =
  835:         await getDoc(
  836:           doc(
  837:             db,
  838:             "users",
  839:             ownerId,
  840:           ),
  841:         );
  842: 
  843:       const ownerSchoolId =
  844:         ownerSnapshot.exists()
  845:           ? normaliseString(
  846:               ownerSnapshot.data()
  847:                 .schoolId,
  848:             )
  849:           : "";
  850: 
  851:       if (ownerSchoolId) {
  852:         updateData.schoolId =
  853:           ownerSchoolId;
  854:       }
  855:     }
  856:   }
  857: 
  858:   await updateDoc(
  859:     classReference,
  860:     {
  861:       ...updateData,
  862:       updatedAt:
  863:         serverTimestamp(),
  864:     },
  865:   );
  866: }
  867: 
  868: /*
  869:  * Enrolment updates both the class and student profile atomically.
  870:  * Same-school membership is verified inside the transaction.
  871:  */
  872: export async function addStudentToClass(
  873:   classId: string,
  874:   student:
  875:     ClassStudent,
  876: ): Promise<void> {
  877:   const cleanedClassId =
  878:     classId.trim();
  879: 
  880:   const cleanedStudent =
  881:     normaliseStudent(
  882:       student,
  883:     );
  884: 
  885:   if (!cleanedClassId) {
  886:     throw new Error(
  887:       "A valid class is required.",
  888:     );
  889:   }
  890: 
  891:   if (
  892:     !cleanedStudent.studentId
  893:   ) {
  894:     throw new Error(
  895:       "A valid student account is required.",
  896:     );
  897:   }
  898: 
  899:   const classReference =
  900:     doc(
  901:       db,
  902:       "classes",
  903:       cleanedClassId,
  904:     );
  905: 
  906:   const studentReference =
  907:     doc(
  908:       db,
  909:       "users",
  910:       cleanedStudent.studentId,
  911:     );
  912: 
  913:   await runTransaction(
  914:     db,
  915:     async (
  916:       transaction,
  917:     ) => {
  918:       const [
  919:         classSnapshot,
  920:         studentSnapshot,
  921:       ] =
  922:         await Promise.all([
  923:           transaction.get(
  924:             classReference,
  925:           ),
  926:           transaction.get(
  927:             studentReference,
  928:           ),
  929:         ]);
  930: 
  931:       if (
  932:         !classSnapshot.exists()
  933:       ) {
  934:         throw new Error(
  935:           "The selected class could not be found.",
  936:         );
  937:       }
  938: 
  939:       if (
  940:         !studentSnapshot.exists()
  941:       ) {
  942:         throw new Error(
  943:           "The selected student account could not be found.",
  944:         );
  945:       }
  946: 
  947:       const classData =
  948:         classSnapshot.data() as
  949:           Partial<FirestoreTeacherClass>;
  950: 
  951:       const studentData =
  952:         studentSnapshot.data() as
  953:           FirestoreStudentProfile;
  954: 
  955:       if (
  956:         studentData.role !==
  957:         "student"
  958:       ) {
  959:         throw new Error(
  960:           "Only student accounts can be enrolled.",
  961:         );
  962:       }
  963: 
  964:       let classSchoolId =
  965:         normaliseString(
  966:           classData.schoolId,
  967:         );
  968: 
  969:       /*
  970:        * Legacy classes created before schoolId existed can adopt their
  971:        * owning teacher's current school on the next enrolment.
  972:        */
  973:       if (!classSchoolId) {
  974:         const ownerId =
  975:           normaliseString(
  976:             classData.teacherId,
  977:           );
  978: 
  979:         if (ownerId) {
  980:           const teacherSnapshot =
  981:             await transaction.get(
  982:               doc(
  983:                 db,
  984:                 "users",
  985:                 ownerId,
  986:               ),
  987:             );
  988: 
  989:           if (
  990:             teacherSnapshot.exists()
  991:           ) {
  992:             classSchoolId =
  993:               normaliseString(
  994:                 teacherSnapshot.data()
  995:                   .schoolId,
  996:               );
  997:           }
  998:         }
  999:       }
 1000: 
 1001:       const studentSchoolId =
 1002:         normaliseString(
 1003:           studentData.schoolId,
 1004:         );
 1005: 
 1006:       if (!classSchoolId) {
 1007:         throw new Error(
 1008:           "This class is not linked to a school yet.",
 1009:         );
 1010:       }
 1011: 
 1012:       if (!studentSchoolId) {
 1013:         throw new Error(
 1014:           "This student is not linked to your school.",
 1015:         );
 1016:       }
 1017: 
 1018:       if (
 1019:         studentSchoolId !==
 1020:         classSchoolId
 1021:       ) {
 1022:         throw new Error(
 1023:           "This student belongs to a different school and cannot be enrolled in this class.",
 1024:         );
 1025:       }
 1026: 
 1027:       const verifiedStudent:
 1028:         ClassStudent = {
 1029:         studentId:
 1030:           cleanedStudent.studentId,
 1031: 
 1032:         displayName:
 1033:           normaliseString(
 1034:             studentData.name,
 1035:           ) ||
 1036:           cleanedStudent.displayName ||
 1037:           "Unnamed Student",
 1038: 
 1039:         email:
 1040:           normaliseEmail(
 1041:             studentData.email,
 1042:           ) ||
 1043:           cleanedStudent.email ||
 1044:           "",
 1045:       };
 1046: 
 1047:       const existingStudentIds =
 1048:         normaliseStringArray(
 1049:           classData.studentIds,
 1050:         );
 1051: 
 1052:       const existingStudents =
 1053:         Array.isArray(
 1054:           classData.students,
 1055:         )
 1056:           ? classData.students
 1057:           : [];
 1058: 
 1059:       if (
 1060:         existingStudentIds.includes(
 1061:           verifiedStudent.studentId,
 1062:         )
 1063:       ) {
 1064:         throw new Error(
 1065:           "This student is already enrolled in the class.",
 1066:         );
 1067:       }
 1068: 
 1069:       const updatedStudents =
 1070:         [
 1071:           ...existingStudents.filter(
 1072:             (
 1073:               existingStudent,
 1074:             ) =>
 1075:               existingStudent.studentId !==
 1076:               verifiedStudent.studentId,
 1077:           ),
 1078:           verifiedStudent,
 1079:         ];
 1080: 
 1081:       const currentClassIds =
 1082:         normaliseStringArray(
 1083:           studentData.classIds,
 1084:         );
 1085: 
 1086:       transaction.update(
 1087:         classReference,
 1088:         {
 1089:           schoolId:
 1090:             classSchoolId,
 1091: 
 1092:           studentIds: [
 1093:             ...existingStudentIds,
 1094:             verifiedStudent.studentId,
 1095:           ],
 1096: 
 1097:           students:
 1098:             updatedStudents,
 1099: 
 1100:           updatedAt:
 1101:             serverTimestamp(),
 1102:         },
 1103:       );
 1104: 
 1105:       transaction.update(
 1106:         studentReference,
 1107:         {
 1108:           classIds:
 1109:             Array.from(
 1110:               new Set([
 1111:                 ...currentClassIds,
 1112:                 cleanedClassId,
 1113:               ]),
 1114:             ),
 1115: 
 1116:           updatedAt:
 1117:             serverTimestamp(),
 1118:         },
 1119:       );
 1120:     },
 1121:   );
 1122: }
 1123: 
 1124: export async function removeStudentFromClass(
 1125:   classId: string,
 1126:   student:
 1127:     ClassStudent,
 1128: ): Promise<void> {
 1129:   const cleanedClassId =
 1130:     classId.trim();
 1131: 
 1132:   const cleanedStudent =
 1133:     normaliseStudent(
 1134:       student,
 1135:     );
 1136: 
 1137:   if (
 1138:     !cleanedClassId ||
 1139:     !cleanedStudent.studentId
 1140:   ) {
 1141:     throw new Error(
 1142:       "A valid class and student are required.",
 1143:     );
 1144:   }
 1145: 
 1146:   const classReference =
 1147:     doc(
 1148:       db,
 1149:       "classes",
 1150:       cleanedClassId,
 1151:     );
 1152: 
 1153:   const studentReference =
 1154:     doc(
 1155:       db,
 1156:       "users",
 1157:       cleanedStudent.studentId,
 1158:     );
 1159: 
 1160:   await runTransaction(
 1161:     db,
 1162:     async (
 1163:       transaction,
 1164:     ) => {
 1165:       const [
 1166:         classSnapshot,
 1167:         studentSnapshot,
 1168:       ] =
 1169:         await Promise.all([
 1170:           transaction.get(
 1171:             classReference,
 1172:           ),
 1173:           transaction.get(
 1174:             studentReference,
 1175:           ),
 1176:         ]);
 1177: 
 1178:       if (
 1179:         !classSnapshot.exists()
 1180:       ) {
 1181:         throw new Error(
 1182:           "The selected class could not be found.",
 1183:         );
 1184:       }
 1185: 
 1186:       const classData =
 1187:         classSnapshot.data() as
 1188:           Partial<FirestoreTeacherClass>;
 1189: 
 1190:       const updatedStudentIds =
 1191:         normaliseStringArray(
 1192:           classData.studentIds,
 1193:         ).filter(
 1194:           (
 1195:             studentId,
 1196:           ) =>
 1197:             studentId !==
 1198:             cleanedStudent.studentId,
 1199:         );
 1200: 
 1201:       const updatedStudents =
 1202:         (
 1203:           Array.isArray(
 1204:             classData.students,
 1205:           )
 1206:             ? classData.students
 1207:             : []
 1208:         ).filter(
 1209:           (
 1210:             classStudent,
 1211:           ) =>
 1212:             classStudent.studentId !==
 1213:             cleanedStudent.studentId,
 1214:         );
 1215: 
 1216:       transaction.update(
 1217:         classReference,
 1218:         {
 1219:           studentIds:
 1220:             updatedStudentIds,
 1221: 
 1222:           students:
 1223:             updatedStudents,
 1224: 
 1225:           updatedAt:
 1226:             serverTimestamp(),
 1227:         },
 1228:       );
 1229: 
 1230:       if (
 1231:         studentSnapshot.exists()
 1232:       ) {
 1233:         const studentData =
 1234:           studentSnapshot.data() as
 1235:             FirestoreStudentProfile;
 1236: 
 1237:         const currentClassIds =
 1238:           normaliseStringArray(
 1239:             studentData.classIds,
 1240:           );
 1241: 
 1242:         transaction.update(
 1243:           studentReference,
 1244:           {
 1245:             classIds:
 1246:               currentClassIds.filter(
 1247:                 (
 1248:                   studentClassId,
 1249:                 ) =>
 1250:                   studentClassId !==
 1251:                   cleanedClassId,
 1252:               ),
 1253: 
 1254:             updatedAt:
 1255:               serverTimestamp(),
 1256:           },
 1257:         );
 1258:       }
 1259:     },
 1260:   );
 1261: }

 1263: export async function archiveTeacherClass(
 1264:   classId: string,
 1265: ): Promise<void> {
 1266:   await updateTeacherClass(
 1267:     classId,
 1268:     {
 1269:       status:
 1270:         "archived",
 1271:     },
 1272:   );
 1273: }
 1274: 
 1275: export async function restoreTeacherClass(
 1276:   classId: string,
 1277: ): Promise<void> {
 1278:   await updateTeacherClass(
 1279:     classId,
 1280:     {
 1281:       status:
 1282:         "active",
 1283:     },
 1284:   );
 1285: }
 1286: 
 1287: /*
 1288:  * Permanent deletion is intentionally conservative.
 1289:  * Classes with students or assignment references should be archived
 1290:  * instead so school history is not accidentally severed.
 1291:  */
 1292: export async function deleteTeacherClass(
 1293:   classId: string,
 1294: ): Promise<void> {
 1295:   const cleanedClassId =
 1296:     classId.trim();
 1297: 
 1298:   if (!cleanedClassId) {
 1299:     throw new Error(
 1300:       "A valid class is required.",
 1301:     );
 1302:   }
 1303: 
 1304:   const reference =
 1305:     doc(
 1306:       db,
 1307:       "classes",
 1308:       cleanedClassId,
 1309:     );
 1310: 
 1311:   const snapshot =
 1312:     await getDoc(
 1313:       reference,
 1314:     );
 1315: 
 1316:   if (
 1317:     !snapshot.exists()
 1318:   ) {
 1319:     return;
 1320:   }
 1321: 
 1322:   const teacherClass =
 1323:     convertClassDocument(
 1324:       snapshot.id,
 1325:       snapshot.data() as
 1326:         Partial<FirestoreTeacherClass>,
 1327:     );
 1328: 
 1329:   if (
 1330:     teacherClass.studentIds
 1331:       .length > 0
 1332:   ) {
 1333:     throw new Error(
 1334:       "Remove students before permanently deleting this class. Archive it instead if you need to preserve history.",
 1335:     );
 1336:   }
 1337: 
 1338:   if (
 1339:     teacherClass.assignmentIds
 1340:       .length > 0
 1341:   ) {
 1342:     throw new Error(
 1343:       "This class has assignment history and cannot be permanently deleted. Archive it instead.",
 1344:     );
 1345:   }
 1346: 
 1347:   await deleteDoc(
 1348:     reference,
 1349:   );
 1350: }

==============================================================================================================
FILE: services\classStudentService.ts
==============================================================================================================

    1: import { arrayUnion, doc, getDoc, updateDoc } from "firebase/firestore";
    2: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
    3: 
    4: export async function addStudentToClass(classId: string, studentId: string) {
    5:   const classRef = doc(db, "classes", classId);
    6:   const studentRef = doc(db, "users", studentId);
    7: 
    8:   const [classSnapshot, studentSnapshot] = await Promise.all([
    9:     getDoc(classRef),
   10:     getDoc(studentRef),
   11:   ]);
   12: 
   13:   if (!classSnapshot.exists()) {
   14:     throw new Error("Class not found.");
   15:   }
   16: 
   17:   if (!studentSnapshot.exists()) {
   18:     throw new Error("Student not found.");
   19:   }
   20: 
   21:   const studentData = studentSnapshot.data();
   22: 
   23:   if (studentData.role !== "student") {
   24:     throw new Error("Only student accounts can be added to a class.");
   25:   }
   26: 
   27:   await Promise.all([
   28:     updateDoc(classRef, {
   29:       studentIds: arrayUnion(studentId),
   30:     }),
   31: 
   32:     updateDoc(studentRef, {
   33:       classIds: arrayUnion(classId),
   34:     }),
   35:   ]);
   36: }

  414:   async function submitAssignments() {

  483:
