  485:             if (!classSnapshot.exists()) throw new Error("A selected class could not be found.");
  486:             const classData = classSnapshot.data();
  487:             const studentIds = Array.isArray(classData.studentIds)
  488:               ? classData.studentIds.filter(
  489:                   (value): value is string =>
  490:                     typeof value === "string" && Boolean(value.trim()),
  491:                 )
  492:               : [];
  493: 
  494:             await createResourceAssignment({
  495:               resourceId: wizardData.resource!.resourceId,

  498:               resourceType: "teacher-resource",

  501:               classId: selectedClassId,

  503:               instructions: wizardData.instructions.trim(),
  504:               dueDate: new Date(`${wizardData.dueDate}T23:59:59`),
  505:               studentIds,
  506:             });
  507:           }),
  508:         );
  509:       } else if (
  510:         wizardData.resource.resourceType ===
  511:         "lesson"
  512:       ) {
  513:         await Promise.all(
  514:           wizardData.selectedClassIds.map(
  515:             async (
  516:               selectedClassId,
  517:             ) => {
  518:               const selectedClass =
  519:                 classes.find(
  520:                   (item) =>
  521:                     item.id ===
  522:                     selectedClassId,
  523:                 );
  524: 
  525:               if (!selectedClass) {
  526:                 throw new Error(
  527:                   "A selected class could not be found.",
  528:                 );
  529:               }
  530: 
  531:               const classSnapshot =
  532:                 await getDoc(
  533:                   doc(
  534:                     db,
  535:                     "classes",
  536:                     selectedClassId,
  537:                   ),
  538:                 );
  539: 
  540:               if (
  541:                 !classSnapshot.exists()
  542:               ) {
  543:                 throw new Error(
  544:                   "A selected class could not be found.",
  545:                 );
  546:               }
  547: 
  548:               const classData =
  549:                 classSnapshot.data();
  550: 
  551:               const studentIds =
  552:                 Array.isArray(
  553:                   classData.studentIds,
  554:                 )
  555:                   ? classData.studentIds.filter(
  556:                       (
  557:                         value,
  558:                       ): value is string =>
  559:                         typeof value ===
  560:                           "string" &&
  561:                         Boolean(
  562:                           value.trim(),
  563:                         ),
  564:                     )
  565:                   : [];
  566: 
  567:               await createResourceAssignment(
  568:                 {
  569:                   resourceId:
  570:                     wizardData.resource!
  571:                       .resourceId,
  572:                   resourceTitle:
  573:                     wizardData.resource!
  574:                       .title,
  575:                   resourceTopic:
  576:                     wizardData.resource!
  577:                       .topicTitle ||
  578:                     "Interactive lesson",
  579:                   resourceType:
  580:                     "lesson",
  581:                   teacherId:
  582:                     user.uid,
  583:                   teacherName:
  584:                     user.displayName ||
  585:                     "Teacher",

  587:                     selectedClassId,
  588:                   className:
  589:                     selectedClass.name,
  590:                   instructions:
  591:                     wizardData.instructions.trim(),
  592:                   dueDate:

  594:                       `${wizardData.dueDate}T23:59:59`,
  595:                     ),
  596:                   studentIds,
  597:                 },
  598:               );
  599:             },
  600:           ),
  601:         );
  602:       } else if (

  610:

  612:           throw new Error(

  614:           );
  615:         }
  616: 
  617:         await Promise.all(
  618:           wizardData.selectedClassIds.map(
  619:             async (
  620:               selectedClassId,
  621:             ) => {

  623:                 classes.find(
  624:                   (item) =>
  625:                     item.id ===
  626:                     selectedClassId,

  654:

  664:                           "string" &&

  668:                     )
  669:                   : [];
  670: 
  671:               await createExamAssignment({

  684:                 questionSetTitle:
  685:                   questionSet.title,
  686:                 questionSetSnapshot:
  687:                   questionSet.content,
  688:                 title:
  689:                   wizardData.resource!
  690:                     .title,
  691:                 instructions:
  692:                   wizardData.instructions.trim(),
  693:                 dueDate:
  694:                   new Date(
  695:                     `${wizardData.dueDate}T23:59:59`,
  696:                   ),
  697:               });
  698:             },
  699:           ),
  700:         );
  701:       } else if (
  702:         wizardData.resource.resourceType === "quiz" ||
  703:         wizardData.resource.resourceType === "ai-quiz"
  704:       ) {
  705:         const isAIQuiz =
  706:           wizardData.resource.resourceType === "ai-quiz";
  707: 
  708:         await Promise.all(
  709:           wizardData.selectedClassIds.map(
  710:             (selectedClassId) =>
  711:               createAssignment({
  712:                 teacherId: user.uid,
  713:                 classId: selectedClassId,
  714:                 title: wizardData.resource!.title,
  715:                 description:
  716:                   wizardData.instructions.trim(),
  717:                 type: "quiz",
  718:                 resourceId:
  719:                   wizardData.resource!.resourceId,
  720:                 dueDate: wizardData.dueDate,
  721:                 quizSource: isAIQuiz
  722:                   ? "ai-generated"
  723:                   : "built-in",
  724:                 deliveryMode:
  725:                   wizardData.deliveryMode,
  726:               }),
  727:           ),
  728:         );
  729:       } else {
  730:         throw new Error(
  731:           "This assignment type is not yet supported.",
  732:         );
  733:       }
  734: 
  735:       toast.success(
  736:         `${
  737:           wizardData.resource.resourceType ===
  738:           "programming-challenge"
  739:             ? "Programming assignment"
  740:             : wizardData.resource.resourceType ===
  741:                 "teaching-resource"
  742:               ? "Resource assignment"
  743:               : wizardData.resource.resourceType ===
  744:                   "lesson"
  745:               ? "Lesson assignment"
  746:               : wizardData.resource.resourceType ===
  747:                   "exam-paper"
  748:                 ? "Exam assignment"
  749:                 : wizardData.resource.resourceType ===
  750:                     "ai-quiz"
  751:                   ? "AI quiz assignment"
  752:                   : wizardData.resource.resourceType ===
  753:                       "quiz"
  754:                     ? "Quiz assignment"
  755:                     : "Assignment"
  756:         } created for ${
  757:           wizardData.selectedClassIds.length
  758:         } ${
  759:           wizardData.selectedClassIds.length ===
  760:           1
  761:             ? "class"
  762:             : "classes"
  763:         }.`,
  764:       );
  765: 
  766:       setWizardData(
  767:         initialWizardData,
  768:       );
  769:       setStep("resource");
  770:     } catch (error) {
  771:       console.error(
  772:         "Assignment wizard error:",
  773:         error,
  774:       );
  775: 
  776:       toast.error(
  777:         error instanceof Error
  778:           ? error.message
  779:           : "Could not create the assignments.",
  780:       );
  781:     } finally {
  782:       setSubmitting(false);
  783:     }
  784:   }
  785: 
  786:   if (
  787:     authLoading ||
  788:     loadingResource
  789:   ) {
  790:     return (
  791:       <main className="flex min-h-[60vh] items-center justify-center">
  792:         <div className="text-center">
  793:           <div className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 animate-spin rounded-full border-4 border-indigo-200 border-t-indigo-600" />
  794: 
  795:           <p className="mt-4 font-semibold text-slate-600">
  796:             Preparing assignment wizard...
  797:           </p>
  798:         </div>
  799:       </main>
  800:     );
  801:   }
  802: 
  803:   return (
  804:     <div className="space-y-8">
  805:       <Card className="border-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-700 via-teal-700 to-cyan-700 text-white">
  806:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
  807:           <div>
  808:             <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-emerald-100">
  809:               Teacher Portal
  810:             </p>
  811: 
  812:             <h1 className="mt-3 text-4xl font-extrabold">
  813:               Assignment Wizard
  814:             </h1>
  815: 
  816:             <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-emerald-100">
  817:               Choose a resource or programming challenge, select classes,
  818:               add a deadline and create assignments in one guided workflow.
  819:             </p>
  820:           </div>
  821: 
  822:           <Link
  823:             href="/teacher/assignments"
  824:             className="rounded-xl bg-white px-5 py-3 text-center font-bold text-teal-700 transition hover:bg-emerald-50"
  825:           >
  826:             â† Assignments
  827:           </Link>
  828:         </div>
  829:       </Card>
  830: 
  831:       <WizardProgress
  832:         currentStep={step}
  833:       />
  834: 
  835:       {step === "resource" && (
  836:         <AssignmentResourceStep
  837:           selectedResource={
  838:             wizardData.resource
  839:           }
  840:           onSelect={selectResource}
  841:           onNext={() =>
  842:             setStep("classes")
  843:           }
  844:         />
  845:       )}
  846: 
  847:       {step === "classes" && (
  848:         <AssignmentClassStep
  849:           classes={classes}
  850:           selectedClassIds={
  851:             wizardData.selectedClassIds
  852:           }
  853:           loading={loadingClasses}
  854:           onToggleClass={
  855:             toggleClass
  856:           }
  857:           onBack={() =>
  858:             setStep("resource")
  859:           }
  860:           onNext={() =>
  861:             setStep("details")
  862:           }
  863:         />
  864:       )}
  865: 
  866:       {step === "details" && (
  867:         <div className="space-y-6">
  868:           {(wizardData.resource?.resourceType === "quiz" ||
  869:             wizardData.resource?.resourceType === "ai-quiz") && (
  870:             <Card>

  872:                 Quiz delivery mode

  876:                 How should students take this quiz?

  878:

  880:                 Practice keeps the normal quiz experience. Assessment marks the
  881:                 assignment for monitored Exam Mode delivery.
  882:               </p>

  885:                 <button

  887:                   onClick={() =>
  888:                     setWizardData((current) => ({
  889:                       ...current,
  890:                       deliveryMode: "practice",
  891:                     }))
  892:                   }
  893:                   className={`rounded-2xl border-2 p-5 text-left transition ${
  894:                     wizardData.deliveryMode === "practice"
  895:                       ? "border-teal-500 bg-teal-50"
  896:                       : "border-slate-200 bg-white hover:border-teal-300"
  897:                   }`}
  898:                 >
  899:                   <p className="font-black text-slate-950">
  900:                     Practice quiz
  901:                   </p>
  902: 
  903:                   <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
  904:                     Standard quiz player with feedback, XP and normal navigation.
  905:                   </p>
  906:

  909:                   </p>

  911:

  943:                   This assignment will be stored as an assessment quiz. The next
  944:                   wiring step will route assessment quizzes through the existing

  952:             dueDate={wizardData.dueDate}
  953:             instructions={

  989:           }
  990:           onSubmit={
  991:             submitAssignments
  992:           }
  993:         />
  994:       )}
  995:     </div>
  996:   );
  997: }
  998: 
  999: function WizardProgress({
 1000:   currentStep,
 1001: }: {
 1002:   currentStep: AssignmentWizardStep;
 1003: }) {
 1004:   const steps: Array<{
 1005:     id: AssignmentWizardStep;
 1006:     label: string;
 1007:   }> = [
 1008:     {
 1009:       id: "resource",
 1010:       label: "Resource",
 1011:     },
 1012:     {
 1013:       id: "classes",
 1014:       label: "Classes",
 1015:     },
 1016:     {
 1017:       id: "details",
 1018:       label: "Details",
 1019:     },
 1020:     {
 1021:       id: "review",
 1022:       label: "Review",
 1023:     },
 1024:   ];
 1025: 
 1026:   const currentIndex =
 1027:     steps.findIndex(
 1028:       (item) =>
 1029:         item.id === currentStep,
 1030:     );
 1031: 
 1032:   return (
 1033:     <Card>
 1034:       <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 md:grid-cols-4">
 1035:         {steps.map(
 1036:           (item, index) => {
 1037:             const complete =
 1038:               index < currentIndex;
 1039:             const active =
 1040:               item.id ===
 1041:               currentStep;
 1042: 
 1043:             return (
 1044:               <div
 1045:                 key={item.id}
 1046:                 className={`rounded-xl border p-4 text-center ${
 1047:                   active
 1048:                     ? "border-teal-500 bg-teal-50 text-teal-700"
 1049:                     : complete
 1050:                       ? "border-green-300 bg-green-50 text-green-700"
 1051:                       : "border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-500"
 1052:                 }`}
 1053:               >
 1054:                 <p className="text-sm font-bold">
 1055:                   {complete
 1056:                     ? "âœ“"
 1057:                     : index + 1}
 1058:                 </p>
 1059: 
 1060:                 <p className="mt-1 font-semibold">
 1061:                   {item.label}
 1062:                 </p>
 1063:               </div>
 1064:             );
 1065:           },
 1066:         )}
 1067:       </div>
 1068:     </Card>
 1069:   );
 1070: }

   69: 
   70: function formatDate(
   71:   value: Date | null,
   72: ): string {
   73:   if (!value) {
   74:     return "No due date";
   75:   }
   76: 
   77:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(
   78:     "en-GB",
   79:     {
   80:       day: "numeric",
   81:       month: "short",
   82:       year: "numeric",
   83:     },
   84:   ).format(value);
   85: }
   86: 
   87: function dueLabel(
   88:   assignment:
   89:     UnifiedTeacherAssignment,
   90: ): string {
   91:   if (!assignment.dueDate) {
   92:     return assignment.status ===
   93:       "active"
   94:       ? "No deadline"
   95:       : "No deadline was set";
   96:   }
   97: 
   98:   if (
   99:     assignment.status ===
  100:     "closed"
  101:   ) {
  102:     return `Closed Â· due date was ${formatDate(
  103:       assignment.dueDate,
  104:     )}`;
  105:   }
  106: 
  107:   if (
  108:     assignment.status ===
  109:     "cancelled"
  110:   ) {
  111:     return `Cancelled Â· due date was ${formatDate(
  112:       assignment.dueDate,
  113:     )}`;
  114:   }
  115: 
  116:   if (
  117:     isUnifiedTeacherAssignmentOverdue(
  118:       assignment,
  119:     )
  120:   ) {
  121:     return "Overdue";
  122:   }
  123: 
  124:   const today = new Date();
  125:   const due =
  126:     new Date(
  127:       assignment.dueDate,
  128:     );
  129: 
  130:   today.setHours(
  131:     0,
  132:     0,
  133:     0,
  134:     0,
  135:   );
  136: 
  137:   due.setHours(
  138:     0,
  139:     0,
  140:     0,
  141:     0,
  142:   );
  143: 
  144:   const difference =
  145:     Math.ceil(
  146:       (due.getTime() -
  147:         today.getTime()) /
  148:         (1000 *
  149:           60 *
  150:           60 *
  151:           24),
  152:     );
  153: 
  154:   if (difference === 0) {
  155:     return "Due today";
  156:   }
  157: 
  158:   if (difference === 1) {
  159:     return "Due tomorrow";
  160:   }
  161: 
  162:   return `${difference} days remaining`;
  163: }
  164:

  230: function statusLabel(
  231:   status:
  232:     UnifiedTeacherAssignmentStatus,
  233: ): string {
  234:   if (status === "closed") {
  235:     return "Closed";
  236:   }
  237: 
  238:   if (
  239:     status === "cancelled"
  240:   ) {
  241:     return "Cancelled";
  242:   }
  243: 
  244:   return "Active";
  245: }
  246: 
  247: function statusTone(
  248:   status:
  249:     UnifiedTeacherAssignmentStatus,
  250: ): string {
  251:   if (status === "closed") {
  252:     return "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700";
  253:   }
  254: 
  255:   if (
  256:     status === "cancelled"
  257:   ) {
  258:     return "bg-red-50 text-red-700";
  259:   }
  260: 
  261:   return "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700";
  262: }
  263: 
  264: function sortAssignments(
  265:   assignments:
  266:     UnifiedTeacherAssignment[],
  267:   sort:
  268:     SortOption,
  269: ): UnifiedTeacherAssignment[] {
  270:   const copied =
  271:     [...assignments];
  272: 
  273:   if (
  274:     sort ===
  275:     "completion-high"
  276:   ) {
  277:     return copied.sort(
  278:       (
  279:         first,
  280:         second,
  281:       ) =>
  282:         second.completionPercentage -
  283:         first.completionPercentage,
  284:     );
  285:   }
  286: 
  287:   if (
  288:     sort ===
  289:     "completion-low"
  290:   ) {
  291:     return copied.sort(
  292:       (
  293:         first,
  294:         second,
  295:       ) =>
  296:         first.completionPercentage -
  297:         second.completionPercentage,
  298:     );
  299:   }
  300: 
  301:   if (
  302:     sort ===
  303:     "due-soon"
  304:   ) {
  305:     return copied.sort(
  306:       (
  307:         first,
  308:         second,
  309:       ) => {
  310:         if (
  311:           !first.dueDate &&
  312:           !second.dueDate
  313:         ) {
  314:           return 0;
  315:         }
  316: 
  317:         if (!first.dueDate) {
  318:           return 1;
  319:         }
  320: 
  321:         if (!second.dueDate) {
  322:           return -1;
  323:         }
  324: 
  325:         return (
  326:           first.dueDate.getTime() -
  327:           second.dueDate.getTime()
  328:         );
  329:       },
  330:     );
  331:   }
  332: 
  333:   return copied.sort(
  334:     (
  335:       first,
  336:       second,
  337:     ) =>
  338:       (second.createdAt?.getTime() ??
  339:         0) -
  340:       (first.createdAt?.getTime() ??
  341:         0),
  342:   );
  343: }

  384:   ] =
  385:     useState<StatusFilter>(
  386:       "all",
  387:     );
  388: 
  389:   const [
  390:     classFilter,
  391:     setClassFilter,
  392:   ] = useState("all");
  393: 
  394:   const [
  395:     sortOption,
  396:     setSortOption,
  397:   ] =
  398:     useState<SortOption>(
  399:       "newest",
  400:     );
  401: 
  402:   const [
  403:     updatingKey,
  404:     setUpdatingKey,
  405:   ] = useState("");
  406: 
  407:   const loadAssignments =
  408:     useCallback(
  409:       () => {
  410:         if (!user?.uid) {
  411:           return Promise.resolve().then(() => {
  412:             setSummary(
  413:               emptySummary,
  414:             );
  415:             setError("");
  416:             setLoading(false);
  417:           });
  418:         }
  419: 
  420:         const teacherId =
  421:           user.uid;
  422: 
  423:         return Promise.resolve()
  424:           .then(() => {
  425:             setLoading(true);
  426:             setError("");
  427: 
  428:             return getUnifiedTeacherAssignments(
  429:               teacherId,
  430:             );
  431:           })
  432:           .then(
  433:             (loadedSummary) => {
  434:               setSummary(
  435:                 loadedSummary,
  436:               );
  437:             },
  438:           )
  439:           .catch(
  440:             (caughtError) => {
  441:               console.error(
  442:                 "Unable to load unified teacher assignments:",
  443:                 caughtError,
  444:               );
  445: 
  446:               setSummary(
  447:                 emptySummary,
  448:               );
  449: 
  450:               setError(
  451:                 caughtError instanceof
  452:                   Error
  453:                   ? caughtError.message
  454:                   : "Your assignments could not be loaded.",
  455:               );
  456:             },
  457:           )
  458:           .finally(() => {
  459:             setLoading(false);
  460:           });
  461:       },
  462:       [user],
  463:     );
  464: 
  465:   useEffect(() => {
  466:     if (
  467:       authLoading ||
  468:       !profileReady
  469:     ) {
  470:       return;
  471:     }
  472: 
  473:     void loadAssignments();
  474:   }, [
  475:     authLoading,
  476:     profileReady,
  477:     loadAssignments,
  478:   ]);
  479: 
  480:   const classes =
  481:     useMemo(() => {
  482:       const classMap =
  483:         new Map<
  484:           string,
  485:           string
  486:         >();
  487: 
  488:       summary.assignments.forEach(
  489:         (
  490:           assignment,
  491:         ) => {
  492:           if (
  493:             assignment.classId
  494:           ) {
  495:             classMap.set(
  496:               assignment.classId,
  497:               assignment.className ||
  498:                 "Untitled class",
  499:             );
  500:           }
  501:         },
  502:       );
  503: 
  504:       return Array.from(
  505:         classMap.entries(),
  506:       )
  507:         .map(
  508:           ([
  509:             id,
  510:             name,
  511:           ]) => ({
  512:             id,
  513:             name,
  514:           }),
  515:         )
  516:         .sort(
  517:           (
  518:             first,
  519:             second,
  520:           ) =>
  521:             first.name.localeCompare(
  522:               second.name,
  523:               "en-GB",
  524:             ),
  525:         );
  526:     }, [
  527:       summary.assignments,
  528:     ]);
  529: 
  530:   const filteredAssignments =
  531:     useMemo(() => {
  532:       const search =
  533:         searchTerm
  534:           .trim()
  535:           .toLowerCase();
  536: 
  537:       const filtered =
  538:         summary.assignments.filter(
  539:           (
  540:             assignment,
  541:           ) => {
  542:             const matchesType =
  543:               typeFilter ===
  544:                 "all" ||
  545:               assignment.kind ===
  546:                 typeFilter;
  547: 
  548:             const matchesStatus =
  549:               statusFilter ===
  550:                 "all" ||
  551:               (statusFilter ===
  552:               "overdue"
  553:                 ? isUnifiedTeacherAssignmentOverdue(
  554:                     assignment,
  555:                   )
  556:                 : assignment.status ===
  557:                   statusFilter);
  558: 
  559:             const matchesClass =
  560:               classFilter ===
  561:                 "all" ||
  562:               assignment.classId ===
  563:                 classFilter;
  564: 
  565:             const matchesSearch =
  566:               !search ||
  567:               [
  568:                 assignment.title,
  569:                 assignment.topic,
  570:                 assignment.className,
  571:                 assignment.description,
  572:                 kindLabel(
  573:                   assignment.kind,
  574:                 ),
  575:               ].some(
  576:                 (value) =>
  577:                   value
  578:                     .toLowerCase()
  579:                     .includes(
  580:                       search,
  581:                     ),
  582:               );
  583: 
  584:             return (
  585:               matchesType &&
  586:               matchesStatus &&
  587:               matchesClass &&
  588:               matchesSearch
  589:             );
  590:           },
  591:         );
  592: 
  593:       return sortAssignments(
  594:         filtered,
  595:         sortOption,
  596:       );
  597:     }, [
  598:       summary.assignments,
  599:       searchTerm,
  600:       typeFilter,
  601:       statusFilter,
  602:       classFilter,
  603:       sortOption,
  604:     ]);
  605: 
  606:   const completionPercentage =
  607:     summary.totalStudentCount >
  608:     0
  609:       ? Math.round(
  610:           (summary.completedStudentCount /
  611:             summary.totalStudentCount) *
  612:             100,
  613:         )
  614:       : 0;
  615: 
  616:   const filtersActive =
  617:     Boolean(
  618:       searchTerm.trim(),
  619:     ) ||
  620:     typeFilter !== "all" ||
  621:     statusFilter !== "all" ||
  622:     classFilter !== "all" ||
  623:     sortOption !== "newest";
  624: 
  625:   function clearFilters() {
  626:     setSearchTerm("");
  627:     setTypeFilter("all");
  628:     setStatusFilter("all");
  629:     setClassFilter("all");
  630:     setSortOption(
  631:       "newest",
  632:     );
  633:   }
  634: 
  635:   async function changeStatus(
  636:     assignment:
  637:       UnifiedTeacherAssignment,
  638:     status:
  639:       UnifiedTeacherAssignmentStatus,
  640:   ) {
  641:     try {
  642:       setUpdatingKey(
  643:         assignment.key,
  644:       );
  645: 
  646:       await updateUnifiedTeacherAssignmentStatus(
  647:         assignment,
  648:         status,
  649:       );
  650: 
  651:       toast.success(
  652:         status === "active"
  653:           ? "Assignment reopened."
  654:           : status ===
  655:               "closed"
  656:             ? "Assignment closed."
  657:             : "Assignment cancelled.",
  658:       );
  659: 
  660:       await loadAssignments();
  661:     } catch (
  662:       caughtError
  663:     ) {
  664:       console.error(
  665:         "Unable to update assignment status:",
  666:         caughtError,
  667:       );
  668: 
  669:       toast.error(
  670:         caughtError instanceof
  671:           Error
  672:           ? caughtError.message
  673:           : "The assignment status could not be changed.",
  674:       );
  675:     } finally {
  676:       setUpdatingKey("");
  677:     }
  678:   }
  679: 
  680:   if (
  681:     authLoading ||
  682:     !profileReady ||
  683:     loading
  684:   ) {
  685:     return (
  686:       <div className="space-y-8">
  687:         <Skeleton className="h-56 w-full rounded-3xl" />
  688: 
  689:         <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
  690:           <Skeleton className="h-28 rounded-3xl" />
  691:           <Skeleton className="h-28 rounded-3xl" />
  692:           <Skeleton className="h-28 rounded-3xl" />
  693:           <Skeleton className="h-28 rounded-3xl" />
  694:         </div>
  695: 
  696:         <Skeleton className="h-96 w-full rounded-3xl" />
  697:       </div>
  698:     );
  699:   }
  700: 
  701:   if (
  702:     profile?.role !==
  703:       "teacher" &&
  704:     profile?.role !==
  705:       "admin"
  706:   ) {
  707:     return (
  708:       <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-7">
  709:         <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-red-950">
  710:           Teacher access required
  711:         </h1>
  712:       </Card>
  713:     );
  714:   }
  715: 
  716:   return (
  717:     <div className="space-y-8">
  718:       <section className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-700 via-teal-700 to-cyan-700 p-7 text-white shadow-xl sm:p-9">
  719:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 xl:flex-row xl:items-center xl:justify-between">
  720:           <div>
  721:             <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-emerald-100">
  722:               T1C Â· Assignment management
  723:             </p>
  724: 
  725:             <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-black sm:text-4xl">
  726:               Assignment Centre
  727:             </h1>
  728: 
  729:             <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-7 text-emerald-100">
  730:               Manage lessons, programming challenges, quizzes and written exams
  731:               from one teacher workspace.
  732:             </p>
  733:           </div>
  734: 
  735:           <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  736:             <Link
  737:               href="/teacher/classes"
  738:               className="rounded-xl border border-white/25 bg-white/10 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-white/20"
  739:             >
  740:               Classes
  741:             </Link>
  742: 
  743:             <Link
  744:               href="/teacher/assignment-wizard"
  745:               className="rounded-xl bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-black text-teal-800 transition hover:bg-emerald-50"
  746:             >
  747:               + Create assignment
  748:             </Link>
  749:           </div>
  750:         </div>
  751:       </section>

  753:       <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">

  762:           }
  763:         />
  764: 
  765:         <SummaryCard
  766:           label="Active"
  767:           value={
  768:             summary.activeAssignments
  769:           }
  770:           description={`${summary.overdueAssignments} overdue`}
  771:           icon={
  772:             <Clock3 className="h-5 w-5" />
  773:           }
  774:         />
  775: 
  776:         <SummaryCard
  777:           label="Completion"

  898:             }
  899:             onChange={(

  904:                   .value as

  909:           >
  910:             <option value="all">
  911:               All statuses
  912:             </option>
  913: 
  914:             <option value="active">

  919:               Overdue
  920:             </option>
  921:

  923:               Closed
  924:             </option>
  925: 
  926:             <option value="cancelled">
  927:               Cancelled
  928:             </option>
  929:           </select>
  930: 
  931:           <select
  932:             aria-label="Class"
  933:             value={

  935:             }
  936:             onChange={(
  937:               event,
  938:             ) =>
  939:               setClassFilter(

  941:                   .value,

  943:             }
  944:             className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm"
  945:           >
  946:             <option value="all">
  947:               All classes
  948:             </option>
  949: 
  950:             {classes.map(
  951:               (item) => (
  952:                 <option
  953:                   key={
  954:                     item.id
  955:                   }
  956:                   value={
  957:                     item.id
  958:                   }
  959:                 >
  960:                   {item.name}
  961:                 </option>
  962:               ),
  963:             )}
  964:           </select>
  965: 
  966:           <label className="relative">
  967:             <span className="sr-only">
  968:               Sort assignments
  969:             </span>
  970: 
  971:             <ArrowUpDown className="pointer-events-none absolute left-4 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
  972: 
  973:             <select
  974:               value={
  975:                 sortOption
  976:               }
  977:               onChange={(
  978:                 event,
  979:               ) =>
  980:                 setSortOption(
  981:                   event.target
  982:                     .value as
  983:                     SortOption,
  984:                 )
  985:               }
  986:               className="w-full appearance-none rounded-xl border border-slate-300 py-3 pl-11 pr-4 text-sm"
  987:             >
  988:               <option value="newest">
  989:                 Newest first
  990:               </option>
  991: 
  992:               <option value="due-soon">
  993:                 Due date
  994:               </option>
  995: 
  996:               <option value="completion-high">
  997:                 Highest completion
  998:               </option>
  999: 
 1000:               <option value="completion-low">
 1001:                 Lowest completion
 1002:               </option>
 1003:             </select>
 1004:           </label>
 1005:         </div>
 1006: 
 1007:         <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
 1008:           <QuickFilter
 1009:             active={
 1010:               statusFilter ===
 1011:               "all"
 1012:             }
 1013:             onClick={() =>
 1014:               setStatusFilter(
 1015:                 "all",
 1016:               )
 1017:             }
 1018:           >
 1019:             All
 1020:           </QuickFilter>
 1021: 
 1022:           <QuickFilter
 1023:             active={
 1024:               statusFilter ===
 1025:               "active"
 1026:             }
 1027:             onClick={() =>
 1028:               setStatusFilter(
 1029:                 "active",
 1030:               )
 1031:             }
 1032:           >
 1033:             Active
 1034:           </QuickFilter>
 1035: 
 1036:           <QuickFilter
 1037:             active={
 1038:               statusFilter ===
 1039:               "overdue"
 1040:             }
 1041:             onClick={() =>
 1042:               setStatusFilter(
 1043:                 "overdue",
 1044:               )
 1045:             }
 1046:           >
 1047:             Overdue (
 1048:             {
 1049:               summary.overdueAssignments
 1050:             }

 1053: 
 1054:           <QuickFilter
 1055:             active={
 1056:               statusFilter ===
 1057:               "closed"

 1060:               setStatusFilter(
 1061:                 "closed",
 1062:               )
 1063:             }
 1064:           >
 1065:             Closed
 1066:           </QuickFilter>
 1067: 
 1068:           <QuickFilter
 1069:             active={
 1070:               statusFilter ===
 1071:               "cancelled"
 1072:             }
 1073:             onClick={() =>
 1074:               setStatusFilter(
 1075:                 "cancelled",
 1076:               )
 1077:             }
 1078:           >
 1079:             Cancelled
 1080:           </QuickFilter>
 1081:         </div>
 1082:       </Card>
 1083: 
 1084:       {error ? (
 1085:         <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-7">
 1086:           <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
 1087:             <AlertCircle className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-red-600" />
 1088:

 1090:               <p className="font-black text-red-950">
 1091:                 Assignments unavailable
 1092:               </p>
 1093:

 1095:                 {error}
 1096:               </p>
 1097: 
 1098:               <button
 1099:                 type="button"
 1100:                 onClick={() =>
 1101:                   void loadAssignments()
 1102:                 }
 1103:                 className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-red-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-bold text-white"
 1104:               >
 1105:                 <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4" />
 1106:                 Try again
 1107:               </button>
 1108:             </div>
 1109:           </div>
 1110:         </Card>
 1111:       ) : filteredAssignments.length ===
 1112:         0 ? (
 1113:         <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 p-12 text-center">
 1114:           <BookOpen className="mx-auto h-10 w-10 text-slate-300" />
 1115: 
 1116:           <h2 className="mt-4 text-xl font-black text-slate-950">
 1117:             No assignments match
 1118:           </h2>
 1119: 
 1120:           <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
 1121:             Create an assignment or adjust your filters.
 1122:           </p>
 1123: 
 1124:           {filtersActive && (
 1125:             <button
 1126:               type="button"
 1127:               onClick={
 1128:                 clearFilters
 1129:               }
 1130:               className="mt-5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white"
 1131:             >
 1132:               Clear filters
 1133:             </button>
 1134:           )}
 1135:         </Card>
 1136:       ) : (
 1137:         <section className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-2">
 1138:           {filteredAssignments.map(
 1139:             (
 1140:               assignment,
 1141:             ) => {
 1142:               const overdue =
 1143:                 isUnifiedTeacherAssignmentOverdue(
 1144:                   assignment,
 1145:                 );
 1146: 
 1147:               const updating =
 1148:                 updatingKey ===
 1149:                 assignment.key;
 1150: 
 1151:               return (
 1152:                 <article
 1153:                   key={
 1154:                     assignment.key
 1155:                   }
 1156:                   className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:shadow-md"
 1157:                 >
 1158:                   <div className="min-w-0">
 1159:                     <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">

   54: function formatDate(value: Date | null, includeTime = false): string {
   55:   if (!value) {
   56:     return "â€”";
   57:   }
   58: 
   59:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(
   60:     "en-GB",
   61:     includeTime
   62:       ? {
   63:           day: "2-digit",
   64:           month: "short",
   65:           year: "numeric",
   66:           hour: "2-digit",
   67:           minute: "2-digit",
   68:         }
   69:       : {
   70:           day: "2-digit",
   71:           month: "long",
   72:           year: "numeric",
   73:         },
   74:   ).format(value);
   75: }
   76: 
   77: function formatResourceType(value: string): string {
   78:   return value
   79:     .replace(/[-_]/g, " ")
   80:     .replace(/\b\w/g, (character) => character.toUpperCase());
   81: }

   83: function formatStatus(status: StudentAssignmentStatus): string {
   84:   if (status === "in_progress") {
   85:     return "In Progress";
   86:   }
   87: 
   88:   if (status === "completed") {
   89:     return "Completed";
   90:   }
   91: 
   92:   return "Not Started";
   93: }
   94: 
   95: function escapeCsvValue(value: string): string {
   96:   return `"${value.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
   97: }
   98: 
   99: function getDueDateInformation(dueDate: Date | null): {
  100:   label: string;
  101:   overdue: boolean;
  102: } {
  103:   if (!dueDate) {
  104:     return {
  105:       label: "No due date",
  106:       overdue: false,
  107:     };
  108:   }
  109: 
  110:   const today = new Date();
  111:   const due = new Date(dueDate);
  112: 
  113:   today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  114:   due.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  115: 
  116:   const differenceInDays = Math.ceil(
  117:     (due.getTime() - today.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24),
  118:   );
  119: 
  120:   if (differenceInDays < 0) {
  121:     const overdueDays = Math.abs(differenceInDays);
  122: 
  123:     return {
  124:       label: `${overdueDays} ${overdueDays === 1 ? "day" : "days"} overdue`,
  125:       overdue: true,
  126:     };
  127:   }
  128: 
  129:   if (differenceInDays === 0) {
  130:     return {
  131:       label: "Due today",
  132:       overdue: false,
  133:     };
  134:   }
  135: 
  136:   if (differenceInDays === 1) {
  137:     return {
  138:       label: "Due tomorrow",
  139:       overdue: false,
  140:     };
  141:   }
  142: 
  143:   return {
  144:     label: `${differenceInDays} days remaining`,
  145:     overdue: false,
  146:   };
  147: }

  149: export default function TeacherResourceAssignmentPage() {

  152:   }>();

  154:   const assignmentId = params.assignmentId;

  157:   const [assignment, setAssignment] = useState<ResourceAssignment | null>(null);

  159:   const [rows, setRows] = useState<StudentProgressRow[]>([]);

  166:

  168: 
  169:   const loadAssignment = useCallback(() => {
  170:     if (!assignmentId || !user?.uid) {
  171:       return Promise.resolve();
  172:     }
  173: 
  174:     const teacherId = user.uid;
  175: 
  176:     return Promise.resolve()
  177:       .then(() => {
  178:         setLoading(true);
  179:         setError("");
  180:         return getAssignmentById(assignmentId);
  181:       })
  182:       .then(async (loadedAssignment) => {
  183:         if (!loadedAssignment) {
  184:           setAssignment(null);
  185:           setRows([]);
  186:           setError("This resource assignment could not be found.");
  187:           return;
  188:         }
  189: 
  190:         if (loadedAssignment.teacherId !== teacherId) {
  191:           setAssignment(null);
  192:           setRows([]);
  193:           setError("You do not have permission to view this assignment.");
  194:           return;
  195:         }
  196: 
  197:         const loadedRows = await Promise.all(
  198:           loadedAssignment.studentIds.map(async (studentId) => {
  199:             const [profileSnapshot, progress] = await Promise.all([
  200:               getDoc(doc(db, "users", studentId)),
  201:               getStudentAssignmentProgress(loadedAssignment.id, studentId),
  202:             ]);
  203: 
  204:             const profileData = profileSnapshot.exists()
  205:               ? profileSnapshot.data()
  206:               : null;
  207: 
  208:             return {
  209:               student: {
  210:                 id: studentId,
  211:                 name: profileData?.name || "Student",
  212:                 email: profileData?.email || "No email available",
  213:               },
  214:               progress,
  215:             } satisfies StudentProgressRow;
  216:           }),
  217:         );
  218: 
  219:         loadedRows.sort((first, second) =>
  220:           first.student.name.localeCompare(second.student.name),
  221:         );
  222: 
  223:         setAssignment(loadedAssignment);
  224:         setRows(loadedRows);
  225:       })
  226:       .catch((caughtError) => {
  227:         console.error("Failed to load assignment progress:", caughtError);
  228: 
  229:         setAssignment(null);
  230:         setRows([]);
  231: 
  232:         setError(
  233:           caughtError instanceof Error
  234:             ? caughtError.message
  235:             : "The assignment dashboard could not be loaded.",
  236:         );
  237:       })
  238:       .finally(() => {
  239:         setLoading(false);
  240:       });
  241:   }, [assignmentId, user]);
  242: 
  243:   useEffect(() => {
  244:     void loadAssignment();
  245:   }, [loadAssignment]);

  292:   function exportCsv() {
  293:     if (!assignment) {
  294:       return;
  295:     }
  296: 
  297:     try {
  298:       setExporting(true);
  299: 
  300:       const headings = [
  301:         "Student Name",

  341:

  346:       URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

  352:   if (loading) {

  366:     );
  367:   }

  383:           <button
  384:             type="button"

  387:             }}

  666: 
  667: function SummaryCard({
  668:   label,
  669:   value,
  670:   description,
  671:   icon,
  672:   iconClassName,
  673: }: {
  674:   label: string;
  675:   value: number;
  676:   description: string;
  677:   icon: ReactNode;
  678:   iconClassName: string;
  679: }) {
  680:   return (
  681:     <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6">
  682:       <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
  683:         <div>
  684:           <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-500">{label}</p>
  685: 
  686:           <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black text-slate-950">{value}</p>
  687: 
  688:           <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{description}</p>
  689:         </div>
  690: 
  691:         <div
  692:           className={`flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl ${iconClassName}`}
  693:         >
  694:           {icon}
  695:         </div>
  696:       </div>
  697:     </Card>
  698:   );
  699: }
  700: 
  701: function StatusBadge({ status }: { status: StudentAssignmentStatus }) {
  702:   if (status === "completed") {
  703:     return (
  704:       <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-emerald-700">
  705:         <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
  706:         Completed
  707:       </span>
  708:     );
  709:   }
  710: 
  711:   if (status === "in_progress") {
  712:     return (
  713:       <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-100 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-amber-700">
  714:         <Clock3 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
  715:         In Progress
  716:       </span>
  717:     );
  718:   }
  719: 
  720:   return (
  721:     <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-600">
  722:       <XCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
  723:       Not Started
  724:     </span>
  725:   );
  726: }
  727: 
  728: function StudentAvatar({ name }: { name: string }) {
  729:   const initials = name
  730:     .split(" ")
  731:     .filter(Boolean)
  732:     .slice(0, 2)
  733:     .map((part) => part[0])
  734:     .join("")
  735:     .toUpperCase();
  736: 
  737:   return (
  738:     <div className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-teal-100 text-sm font-black text-teal-700">
  739:       {initials || "ST"}
  740:     </div>
  741:   );
  742: }
  743: 
  744: function EmptyState({
  745:   title,
  746:   description,
  747: }: {
  748:   title: string;
  749:   description: string;
  750: }) {
  751:   return (
  752:     <div className="p-10 text-center">
  753:       <Users className="mx-auto h-10 w-10 text-slate-400" />
  754: 
  755:       <h3 className="mt-4 text-xl font-black text-slate-950">{title}</h3>
  756: 
  757:       <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-lg text-sm text-slate-500">
  758:         {description}
  759:       </p>
  760:     </div>
  761:   );
  762: }

  106:     useState<AssignmentResourceType | null>(

  111:     useState<SavedQuizOption[]>([]);

  113:   const [quizLoadError, setQuizLoadError] = useState("");
  114: 
  115:   useEffect(() => {
  116:     void Promise.resolve().then(() => {
  117:       setSelectedType(selectedResource?.resourceType || null);
  118:     });

  120: 
  121:   useEffect(() => {
  122:     if (authLoading) return;

  126:         setSavedQuizzes([]);

  137:     const unsubscribe = onSnapshot(
  138:       quizzesQuery,

  141:           (quizDocument) => {

  143: 
  144:             return {
  145:               id: quizDocument.id,

  157:                     : 0,

  172:       },
  173:     );

  176:   }, [authLoading, user]);

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
    4: import type {

    9: type AssignmentReviewStepProps = {
   10:   data: AssignmentWizardData;
   11:   classes: AssignmentWizardClass[];
   12:   submitting: boolean;
   13:   onBack: () => void;
   14:   onSubmit: () => void;
   15: };
   16: 
   17: function formatResourceType(
   18:   value: string,
   19: ): string {
   20:   return value
   21:     .replace(/[-_]/g, " ")
   22:     .replace(/\b\w/g, (character) =>
   23:       character.toUpperCase(),
   24:     );
   25: }
   26: 
   27: export default function AssignmentReviewStep({
   28:   data,
   29:   classes,
   30:   submitting,
   31:   onBack,
   32:   onSubmit,
   33: }: AssignmentReviewStepProps) {
   34:   const selectedClasses =
   35:     classes.filter((classItem) =>
   36:       data.selectedClassIds.includes(
   37:         classItem.id,
   38:       ),
   39:     );
   40: 
   41:   const resourceLabel =
   42:     data.resource?.resourceType ===
   43:     "programming-challenge"
   44:       ? "Programming challenge"
   45:       : data.resource?.resourceType ===
   46:           "lesson"
   47:         ? "Assigned lesson"
   48:         : data.resource?.resourceType === "exam-paper"
   49:           ? "Assigned exam paper"
   50:           : data.resource?.resourceType === "quiz" ||
   51:               data.resource?.resourceType === "ai-quiz"
   52:             ? "Assigned quiz"
   53:             : "Resource";
   54: 
   55:   return (
   56:     <Card>
   57:       <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-teal-600">
   58:         Step 4 of 4
   59:       </p>
   60: 
   61:       <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
   62:         Review Assignment
   63:       </h2>
   64: 
   65:       <p className="mt-2 text-slate-600">
   66:         Check the details before assigning this work.
   67:       </p>
   68: 
   69:       <div className="mt-6 space-y-5">
   70:         <ReviewSection
   71:           label={resourceLabel}
   72:           value={
   73:             data.resource?.title ||
   74:             "No resource selected"
   75:           }
   76:         />
   77: 
   78:         {data.resource?.resourceType ===
   79:           "lesson" && (
   80:           <>
   81:             <ReviewSection
   82:               label="Topic"
   83:               value={
   84:                 data.resource.topicTitle ||
   85:                 "Not specified"
   86:               }
   87:             />
   88: 
   89:             <ReviewSection
   90:               label="Curriculum"
   91:               value={`${data.resource.examBoard || "Not specified"} ${
   92:                 data.resource.qualification === "A_LEVEL"
   93:                   ? "A-Level"
   94:                   : "GCSE"
   95:               }`}
   96:             />
   97:           </>
   98:         )}
   99: 
  100:         {data.resource?.resourceType === "exam-paper" && (
  101:           <>
  102:             <ReviewSection
  103:               label="Topic"
  104:               value={data.resource.examTopic || "Not specified"}
  105:             />
  106: 
  107:             <ReviewSection
  108:               label="Paper"
  109:               value={`${data.resource.questionCount ?? 0} questions Â· ${
  110:                 data.resource.totalMarks ?? 0
  111:               } marks`}
  112:             />
  113: 
  114:             <ReviewSection
  115:               label="Curriculum"
  116:               value={`${data.resource.examBoard || "Not specified"} Â· ${
  117:                 data.resource.examQualification || "Not specified"
  118:               }`}
  119:             />
  120:           </>
  121:         )}
  122: 
  123:         <ReviewSection

  125:           value={
  126:             data.resource
  127:               ? formatResourceType(
  128:                   data.resource.resourceType,
  129:                 )
  130:               : "Not selected"
  131:           }
  132:         />
  133: 
  134:         <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-5">
  135:           <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
  136:             Classes
  137:           </p>
  138: 
  139:           <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
  140:             {selectedClasses.length === 0 ? (
  141:               <p className="text-slate-600">
  142:                 No classes selected.
  143:               </p>
  144:             ) : (
  145:               selectedClasses.map(
  146:                 (classItem) => (
  147:                   <span
  148:                     key={classItem.id}
  149:                     className="rounded-full bg-teal-100 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-teal-700"
  150:                   >
  151:                     {classItem.name} Â·{" "}
  152:                     {classItem.yearGroup}
  153:                   </span>
  154:                 ),
  155:               )
  156:             )}
  157:           </div>
  158:         </div>
  159: 
  160:         <ReviewSection
  161:           label="Due date"
  162:           value={data.dueDate}
  163:         />
  164: 
  165:         <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-5">
  166:           <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
  167:             Instructions
  168:           </p>
  169: 
  170:           <p className="mt-3 leading-7 text-slate-700">
  171:             {data.instructions}
  172:           </p>
  173:         </div>
  174: 
  175:         {data.resource?.resourceType ===
  176:           "lesson" && (
  177:           <div className="rounded-2xl border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 p-5">
  178:             <p className="font-black text-blue-950">
  179:               Automatic lesson completion
  180:             </p>
  181: 
  182:             <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-blue-800">
  183:               The assignment will be completed only when the student
  184:               completes this exact interactive lesson in CS Master.
  185:             </p>
  186:           </div>
  187:         )}
  188: 
  189:         {data.resource?.resourceType === "exam-paper" && (
  190:           <div className="rounded-2xl border border-indigo-200 bg-indigo-50 p-5">
  191:             <p className="font-black text-indigo-950">
  192:               Written assessment workflow
  193:             </p>
  194: 
  195:             <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-indigo-800">
  196:               Student answers are autosaved. Submission locks the paper.
  197:               AI marking suggestions remain provisional until the teacher
  198:               reviews the marks and finalises the result.
  199:             </p>
  200:           </div>
  201:         )}
  202:       </div>
  203: 
  204:       <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:justify-between">
  205:         <button
  206:           type="button"
  207:           onClick={onBack}
  208:           disabled={submitting}
  209:           className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-6 py-3 font-bold text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
  210:         >
  211:           â† Back
  212:         </button>
  213: 
  214:         <button
  215:           type="button"
  216:           onClick={onSubmit}
  217:           disabled={
  218:             submitting ||
  219:             !data.resource ||
  220:             data.selectedClassIds.length === 0 ||
  221:             !data.dueDate ||
  222:             !data.instructions.trim()
  223:           }
  224:           className="rounded-xl bg-teal-600 px-6 py-3 font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-teal-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
  225:         >
  226:           {submitting
  227:             ? "Creating Assignments..."
  228:             : `Assign to ${data.selectedClassIds.length} ${
  229:                 data.selectedClassIds.length === 1
  230:                   ? "Class"
  231:                   : "Classes"
  232:               }`}
  233:         </button>
  234:       </div>
  235:     </Card>
  236:   );
  237: }
  238: 
  239: function ReviewSection({
  240:   label,
  241:   value,
  242: }: {
  243:   label: string;
  244:   value: string;
  245: }) {
  246:   return (
  247:     <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-5">
  248:       <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
  249:         {label}
  250:       </p>
  251: 
  252:       <p className="mt-3 font-bold text-slate-900">
  253:         {value}
  254:       </p>
  255:     </div>
  256:   );
  257: }

==============================================================================================================
FILE: services\assignmentService.ts
==============================================================================================================

    1: import {
    2:   addDoc,
    3:   arrayUnion,
    4:   collection,
    5:   doc,
    6:   serverTimestamp,
    7:   updateDoc,
    8: } from "firebase/firestore";
    9: 
   10: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
   11: 
   12: export type QuizAssignmentSource =
   13:   | "built-in"
   14:   | "ai-generated";
   15: 
   16: export type QuizDeliveryMode =
   17:   | "practice"
   18:   | "assessment";
   19: 
   20: export type CreateAssignmentInput = {
   21:   teacherId: string;
   22:   classId: string;
   23:   title: string;
   24:   description: string;
   25:   type: "lesson" | "quiz";
   26:   resourceId: string;
   27:   dueDate: string;
   28: 
   29:   quizSource?: QuizAssignmentSource;
   30:   deliveryMode?: QuizDeliveryMode;
   31: };
   32: 
   33: export async function createAssignment({
   34:   teacherId,
   35:   classId,
   36:   title,
   37:   description,
   38:   type,
   39:   resourceId,
   40:   dueDate,
   41:   quizSource,
   42:   deliveryMode,
   43: }: CreateAssignmentInput) {
   44:   const assignmentRef = await addDoc(
   45:     collection(db, "assignments"),
   46:     {
   47:       teacherId,
   48:       classId,
   49:       title,
   50:       description,
   51:       type,
   52:       resourceId,
   53:       dueDate,
   54: 
   55:       ...(type === "quiz"
   56:         ? {
   57:             quizSource:
   58:               quizSource || "built-in",
   59: 
   60:             deliveryMode:
   61:               deliveryMode || "practice",
   62:           }
   63:         : {}),
   64: 
   65:       status: "active",
   66:       createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
   67:     },
   68:   );
   69: 
   70:   await updateDoc(
   71:     doc(db, "classes", classId),
   72:     {
   73:       assignmentIds: arrayUnion(
   74:         assignmentRef.id,
   75:       ),
   76:     },
   77:   );
   78: 
   79:   return assignmentRef.id;
   80: }

    1: import {

   30: export type UnifiedAssignment = {

   54: 
   55: function convertResource(
   56:   assignment: StudentAssignmentWithProgress,
   57: ): UnifiedAssignment {
   58:   const isProgramming =
   59:     assignment.resourceType === "programming-challenge";
   60: 
   61:   return {
   62:     kind: isProgramming ? "programming" : "resource",
   63:     id: assignment.id,
   64:     title: assignment.resourceTitle,
   65:     topic: assignment.resourceTopic,
   66:     description: assignment.instructions || "",
   67:     resourceType: assignment.resourceType,
   68:     resourceId: assignment.resourceId,
   69:     className: assignment.className,
   70:     teacherName: assignment.teacherName,
   71:     dueDate: assignment.dueDate,
   72:     createdAt: assignment.createdAt,
   73:     status: assignment.studentProgress.status,
   74:     completedAt: assignment.studentProgress.completedAt,
   75:     percentage: null,
   76:     score: null,
   77:     totalQuestions: null,
   78:     earnedXP: null,
   79:     questionCount: null,
   80:     totalMarks: null,
   81:     submittedAt: null,
   82:     markedAt: null,
   83:     overallFeedback: "",
   84:   };
   85: }
   86: 
   87: function convertQuiz(
   88:   assignment: StudentQuizAssignment,
   89: ): UnifiedAssignment {
   90:   const isAIQuiz = assignment.quizSource === "ai-generated";
   91: 
   92:   return {
   93:     kind: "quiz",
   94:     id: assignment.id,
   95:     title: assignment.title,
   96:     topic: isAIQuiz ? "Assigned AI quiz" : "Assigned quiz",
   97:     description: assignment.description,
   98:     resourceType: isAIQuiz ? "AI Quiz" : "Quiz",
   99:     resourceId: assignment.resourceId,
  100:     className: assignment.className,
  101:     teacherName: assignment.teacherName,
  102:     dueDate: assignment.dueDate,
  103:     createdAt: assignment.createdAt,
  104:     status: assignment.resultStatus,
  105:     completedAt: assignment.completedAt,
  106:     percentage: assignment.percentage,
  107:     score: assignment.score,
  108:     totalQuestions: assignment.totalQuestions,
  109:     earnedXP: assignment.earnedXP,
  110:     questionCount: null,
  111:     totalMarks: null,
  112:     submittedAt: null,
  113:     markedAt: null,
  114:     overallFeedback: "",
  115:   };
  116: }
  117: 
  118: function convertExam(
  119:   assignment: ExamAssignment,
  120:   submission: ExamSubmission | null,
  121: ): UnifiedAssignment {
  122:   return {
  123:     kind: "exam",
  124:     id: assignment.id,
  125:     title: assignment.title,
  126:     topic:
  127:       assignment.questionSetSnapshot.topic ||
  128:       assignment.questionSetTitle,
  129:     description: assignment.instructions || "",
  130:     resourceType: "Written Exam",
  131:     resourceId: assignment.questionSetId,
  132:     className: assignment.className,
  133:     teacherName: assignment.teacherName,
  134:     dueDate: assignment.dueDate,
  135:     createdAt: assignment.createdAt,
  136:     status: submission?.status || "not_started",
  137:     completedAt:
  138:       submission?.markedAt ||
  139:       submission?.submittedAt ||
  140:       null,
  141:     percentage:
  142:       submission?.status === "marked"
  143:         ? submission.percentage
  144:         : null,
  145:     score:
  146:       submission?.status === "marked"
  147:         ? submission.totalAwardedMarks
  148:         : null,
  149:     totalQuestions: null,
  150:     earnedXP: null,
  151:     questionCount: assignment.questionCount,
  152:     totalMarks: assignment.totalMarks,
  153:     submittedAt: submission?.submittedAt || null,
  154:     markedAt: submission?.markedAt || null,
  155:     overallFeedback: submission?.overallFeedback || "",
  156:   };
  157: }
  158: 
  159: export function isUnifiedAssignmentComplete(
  160:   assignment: UnifiedAssignment,
  161: ): boolean {
  162:   return assignment.kind === "exam"
  163:     ? assignment.status === "marked"
  164:     : assignment.status === "completed";
  165: }
  166: 
  167: export function isUnifiedAssignmentOverdue(
  168:   assignment: UnifiedAssignment,
  169: ): boolean {
  170:   if (!assignment.dueDate || isUnifiedAssignmentComplete(assignment)) {
  171:     return false;
  172:   }
  173: 
  174:   const dueDate = new Date(assignment.dueDate);
  175:   dueDate.setHours(23, 59, 59, 999);
  176: 
  177:   return dueDate.getTime() < Date.now();
  178: }
  179: 
  180: export async function getUnifiedStudentAssignments(
  181:   studentId: string,
  182: ): Promise<UnifiedAssignment[]> {
  183:   const [resources, quizzes, exams] = await Promise.all([
  184:     getStudentAssignments(studentId),
  185:     getStudentQuizAssignments(studentId),
  186:     getStudentExamAssignments(studentId),
  187:   ]);
  188: 
  189:   const examItems = await Promise.all(
  190:     exams.map(async (assignment) =>
  191:       convertExam(
  192:         assignment,
  193:         await getExamSubmission(
  194:           assignment.id,
  195:           studentId,
  196:         ),
  197:       ),
  198:     ),
  199:   );
  200: 
  201:   return [
  202:     ...resources.map(convertResource),
  203:     ...quizzes.map(convertQuiz),
  204:     ...examItems,
  205:   ].sort((a, b) => {
  206:     const aComplete = isUnifiedAssignmentComplete(a);
  207:     const bComplete = isUnifiedAssignmentComplete(b);
  208: 
  209:     if (aComplete !== bComplete) return aComplete ? 1 : -1;
  210: 
  211:     const aDue = a.dueDate?.getTime() ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
  212:     const bDue = b.dueDate?.getTime() ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
  213: 
  214:     if (aDue !== bDue) return aDue - bDue;
  215: 
  216:     return (
  217:       (b.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0) -
  218:       (a.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0)
  219:     );
  220:   });
  221: }

    1: import {
    2:   doc,
    3:   serverTimestamp,
    4:   updateDoc,
    5: } from "firebase/firestore";
    6: 
    7: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
    8: 
    9: import {
   10:   getTeacherAssignments,
   11:   type AssignmentStatus,
   12:   type ResourceAssignment,
   13: } from "@/services/resourceAssignmentService";
   14: 
   15: import {
   16:   getTeacherQuizAssignments,
   17:   type QuizAssignmentStatus,
   18:   type TeacherQuizAssignmentSummary,
   19: } from "@/services/teacherQuizAssignmentService";
   20: 
   21: import { getTeacherExamAssignments } from "@/services/examAssignmentService";
   22: import { getTeacherClasses } from "@/services/classService";
   23: import { getAssignmentSubmissions } from "@/services/examSubmissionService";
   24: 
   25: import type {
   26:   ExamAssignment,
   27:   ExamAssignmentStatus,
   28:   ExamSubmission,
   29: } from "@/types/examAssignment";
   30: 
   31: export type UnifiedTeacherAssignmentKind =
   32:   | "resource"
   33:   | "programming"
   34:   | "quiz"
   35:   | "exam";
   36: 
   37: export type UnifiedTeacherAssignmentStatus =
   38:   | "active"
   39:   | "closed"
   40:   | "cancelled";
   41: 
   42: export type UnifiedTeacherAssignment = {
   43:   key: string;
   44:   kind: UnifiedTeacherAssignmentKind;
   45:   id: string;
   46:   title: string;
   47:   topic: string;
   48:   description: string;
   49:   classId: string;
   50:   className: string;
   51:   dueDate: Date | null;
   52:   createdAt: Date | null;
   53:   status: UnifiedTeacherAssignmentStatus;
   54:   studentCount: number;
   55:   completedCount: number;
   56:   completionPercentage: number;
   57:   awaitingMarkingCount: number;
   58:   averagePercentage: number | null;
   59:   detailHref: string;
   60: };
   61: 
   62: export type UnifiedTeacherAssignmentSummary = {
   63:   assignments: UnifiedTeacherAssignment[];
   64:   totalAssignments: number;
   65:   activeAssignments: number;
   66:   overdueAssignments: number;
   67:   completedStudentCount: number;
   68:   totalStudentCount: number;
   69:   awaitingMarkingCount: number;
   70: };
   71: 
   72: function safePercentage(completed: number, total: number): number {
   73:   if (total <= 0) return 0;
   74:   return Math.round((completed / total) * 100);
   75: }
   76: 
   77: function normaliseStatus(
   78:   value: AssignmentStatus | QuizAssignmentStatus | ExamAssignmentStatus,
   79: ): UnifiedTeacherAssignmentStatus {
   80:   if (value === "closed") return "closed";
   81:   if (value === "cancelled") return "cancelled";
   82:   return "active";
   83: }
   84: 
   85: function isOverdue(
   86:   assignment: Pick<UnifiedTeacherAssignment, "dueDate" | "status">,
   87: ): boolean {
   88:   if (assignment.status !== "active" || !assignment.dueDate) return false;
   89: 
   90:   const due = new Date(assignment.dueDate);
   91:   due.setHours(23, 59, 59, 999);
   92:   return due.getTime() < Date.now();
   93: }
   94: 
   95: function convertResourceAssignment(
   96:   assignment: ResourceAssignment,
   97: ): UnifiedTeacherAssignment {
   98:   const programming = assignment.resourceType === "programming-challenge";
   99: 
  100:   return {
  101:     key: `${programming ? "programming" : "resource"}-${assignment.id}`,
  102:     kind: programming ? "programming" : "resource",
  103:     id: assignment.id,
  104:     title: assignment.resourceTitle,
  105:     topic: assignment.resourceTopic,
  106:     description: assignment.instructions,
  107:     classId: assignment.classId,
  108:     className: assignment.className,
  109:     dueDate: assignment.dueDate,
  110:     createdAt: assignment.createdAt,
  111:     status: normaliseStatus(assignment.status),
  112:     studentCount: assignment.studentCount,
  113:     completedCount: assignment.completedCount,
  114:     completionPercentage: safePercentage(
  115:       assignment.completedCount,
  116:       assignment.studentCount,
  117:     ),
  118:     awaitingMarkingCount: 0,
  119:     averagePercentage: null,
  120:     detailHref: programming
  121:       ? `/teacher/programming-assignments/${assignment.id}`
  122:       : `/teacher/assignments/${assignment.id}`,
  123:   };
  124: }
  125: 
  126: function convertQuizAssignment(
  127:   assignment: TeacherQuizAssignmentSummary,
  128: ): UnifiedTeacherAssignment {
  129:   return {
  130:     key: `quiz-${assignment.id}`,
  131:     kind: "quiz",
  132:     id: assignment.id,
  133:     title: assignment.title,
  134:     topic:
  135:       assignment.deliveryMode === "assessment"
  136:         ? "Assessment quiz"
  137:         : "Practice quiz",
  138:     description: assignment.description,
  139:     classId: assignment.classId,
  140:     className: assignment.className,
  141:     dueDate: assignment.dueDate,
  142:     createdAt: assignment.createdAt,
  143:     status: normaliseStatus(assignment.status),
  144:     studentCount: assignment.studentCount,
  145:     completedCount: assignment.completedCount,
  146:     completionPercentage: assignment.completionPercentage,
  147:     awaitingMarkingCount: 0,
  148:     averagePercentage:
  149:       assignment.completedCount > 0 ? assignment.averagePercentage : null,
  150:     detailHref: `/teacher/quiz-assignments/${assignment.id}`,
  151:   };
  152: }
  153: 
  154: function summariseExam(
  155:   assignment: ExamAssignment,
  156:   submissions: ExamSubmission[],
  157: ): UnifiedTeacherAssignment {
  158:   const marked = submissions.filter((item) => item.status === "marked");
  159:   const awaitingMarking = submissions.filter(
  160:     (item) => item.status === "submitted" || item.status === "marking",
  161:   );
  162: 
  163:   const averagePercentage =
  164:     marked.length > 0
  165:       ? Math.round(
  166:           marked.reduce((total, item) => total + item.percentage, 0) /
  167:             marked.length,
  168:         )
  169:       : null;
  170: 
  171:   const studentCount = assignment.studentIds.length;
  172: 
  173:   return {
  174:     key: `exam-${assignment.id}`,
  175:     kind: "exam",
  176:     id: assignment.id,
  177:     title: assignment.title,
  178:     topic:
  179:       assignment.questionSetSnapshot.topic || assignment.questionSetTitle,
  180:     description: assignment.instructions,
  181:     classId: assignment.classId,
  182:     className: assignment.className,
  183:     dueDate: assignment.dueDate,
  184:     createdAt: assignment.createdAt,
  185:     status: normaliseStatus(assignment.status),
  186:     studentCount,
  187:     completedCount: marked.length,
  188:     completionPercentage: safePercentage(marked.length, studentCount),
  189:     awaitingMarkingCount: awaitingMarking.length,
  190:     averagePercentage,
  191:     detailHref: `/teacher/exam-assignments/${assignment.id}`,
  192:   };
  193: }
  194: 
  195: export async function getUnifiedTeacherAssignments(
  196:   teacherId: string,
  197: ): Promise<UnifiedTeacherAssignmentSummary> {
  198:   const cleanedTeacherId = teacherId.trim();
  199: 
  200:   if (!cleanedTeacherId) {
  201:     return {
  202:       assignments: [],
  203:       totalAssignments: 0,
  204:       activeAssignments: 0,
  205:       overdueAssignments: 0,
  206:       completedStudentCount: 0,
  207:       totalStudentCount: 0,
  208:       awaitingMarkingCount: 0,
  209:     };
  210:   }
  211: 
  212:   const [
  213:     classes,
  214:     resources,
  215:     quizzes,
  216:     exams,
  217:   ] = await Promise.all([
  218:     getTeacherClasses(cleanedTeacherId),
  219:     getTeacherAssignments(cleanedTeacherId),
  220:     getTeacherQuizAssignments(cleanedTeacherId),
  221:     getTeacherExamAssignments(cleanedTeacherId),
  222:   ]);
  223: 
  224:   /*
  225:    * T1G integrity boundary:
  226:    * even though each assignment query is teacher-scoped, only assignments
  227:    * attached to a class owned by the authenticated teacher are admitted to
  228:    * the unified teacher portfolio. This prevents stale/corrupt class links
  229:    * from leaking into dashboard and reporting totals.
  230:    */
  231:   const ownedClassIds =
  232:     new Set(
  233:       classes.map(
  234:         (item) => item.id,
  235:       ),
  236:     );
  237: 
  238:   const examAssignments = await Promise.all(
  239:     exams.map(async (assignment) => {
  240:       const submissions = await getAssignmentSubmissions(
  241:         assignment.id,
  242:         cleanedTeacherId,
  243:       );
  244: 
  245:       return summariseExam(assignment, submissions);
  246:     }),
  247:   );
  248: 
  249:   const assignments: UnifiedTeacherAssignment[] = [
  250:     ...resources.map(convertResourceAssignment),
  251:     ...quizzes.map(convertQuizAssignment),
  252:     ...examAssignments,
  253:   ]
  254:     .filter(
  255:       (assignment) =>
  256:         ownedClassIds.has(
  257:           assignment.classId,
  258:         ),
  259:     )
  260:     .sort(
  261:     (first, second) =>
  262:       (second.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0) -
  263:       (first.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0),
  264:   );
  265: 
  266:   return {
  267:     assignments,
  268:     totalAssignments: assignments.length,
  269:     activeAssignments: assignments.filter((item) => item.status === "active")
  270:       .length,
  271:     overdueAssignments: assignments.filter(isOverdue).length,
  272:     completedStudentCount: assignments.reduce(
  273:       (total, item) => total + item.completedCount,
  274:       0,
  275:     ),
  276:     totalStudentCount: assignments.reduce(
  277:       (total, item) => total + item.studentCount,
  278:       0,
  279:     ),
  280:     awaitingMarkingCount: assignments.reduce(
  281:       (total, item) => total + item.awaitingMarkingCount,
  282:       0,
  283:     ),
  284:   };
  285: }
  286: 
  287: export function isUnifiedTeacherAssignmentOverdue(
  288:   assignment: UnifiedTeacherAssignment,
  289: ): boolean {
  290:   return isOverdue(assignment);
  291: }
  292: 
  293: export async function updateUnifiedTeacherAssignmentStatus(
  294:   assignment: Pick<UnifiedTeacherAssignment, "id" | "kind">,
  295:   status: UnifiedTeacherAssignmentStatus,
  296: ): Promise<void> {
  297:   const collectionName =
  298:     assignment.kind === "quiz"
  299:       ? "assignments"
  300:       : assignment.kind === "exam"
  301:         ? "examAssignments"
  302:         : "classAssignments";
  303: 
  304:   await updateDoc(doc(db, collectionName, assignment.id), {
  305:     status,
  306:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  307:   });
  308: }

==============================================================================================================
FILE: services\lessonAssignmentService.tsx
==============================================================================================================

    1: import { topicLibrary } from "@/data/curriculum/topics";
    2: import type { Lesson, Topic } from "@/types/curriculum";
    3: 
    4: const LESSON_RESOURCE_SEPARATOR = "::";
    5: 
    6: export type LessonAssignmentIdentity = {
    7:   topicId: string;
    8:   lessonId: string;
    9: };
   10: 
   11: export type LessonAssignmentOption = {
   12:   topicId: string;
   13:   topicTitle: string;
   14:   lesson: Lesson;
   15: };
   16: 
   17: export function createLessonAssignmentResourceId(
   18:   topicId: string,
   19:   lessonId: string,
   20: ): string {
   21:   return `${topicId}${LESSON_RESOURCE_SEPARATOR}${lessonId}`;
   22: }
   23: 
   24: export function parseLessonAssignmentResourceId(
   25:   resourceId: string,
   26: ): LessonAssignmentIdentity | null {
   27:   const separatorIndex = resourceId.indexOf(
   28:     LESSON_RESOURCE_SEPARATOR,
   29:   );
   30: 
   31:   if (separatorIndex <= 0) {
   32:     return null;
   33:   }
   34: 
   35:   const topicId = resourceId.slice(0, separatorIndex).trim();
   36:   const lessonId = resourceId
   37:     .slice(separatorIndex + LESSON_RESOURCE_SEPARATOR.length)
   38:     .trim();
   39: 
   40:   if (!topicId || !lessonId) {
   41:     return null;
   42:   }
   43: 
   44:   return { topicId, lessonId };
   45: }
   46: 
   47: export function getLessonAssignmentOption(
   48:   resourceId: string,
   49: ): LessonAssignmentOption | null {
   50:   const identity = parseLessonAssignmentResourceId(resourceId);
   51: 
   52:   if (!identity) {
   53:     return null;
   54:   }
   55: 
   56:   const topic = (topicLibrary as Record<string, Topic>)[identity.topicId];
   57: 
   58:   if (!topic) {
   59:     return null;
   60:   }
   61: 
   62:   const lesson = topic.lessons.find(
   63:     (item) => item.id === identity.lessonId,
   64:   );
   65: 
   66:   if (!lesson) {
   67:     return null;
   68:   }
   69: 
   70:   return {
   71:     topicId: topic.id,
   72:     topicTitle: topic.title,
   73:     lesson,
   74:   };
   75: }
   76: 
   77: export function getLessonAssignmentHref(
   78:   resourceId: string,
   79:   assignmentId?: string,
   80: ): string | null {
   81:   const identity = parseLessonAssignmentResourceId(resourceId);
   82: 
   83:   if (!identity) {
   84:     return null;
   85:   }
   86: 
   87:   const search = new URLSearchParams({
   88:     lesson: identity.lessonId,
   89:   });
   90: 
   91:   if (assignmentId) {
   92:     search.set("assignment", assignmentId);
   93:   }
   94: 
   95:   return `/learn/${encodeURIComponent(identity.topicId)}?${search.toString()}`;
   96: }
