   39: type FirestoreDate = Timestamp | Date | string | null | undefined;
   40: 
   41: function convertDate(value: FirestoreDate): Date | null {
   42:   if (!value) return null;
   43:   if (value instanceof Date) return value;
   44:   if (value instanceof Timestamp) return value.toDate();
   45: 
   46:   if (typeof value === "string") {
   47:     const parsedDate = new Date(value);
   48:     return Number.isNaN(parsedDate.getTime()) ? null : parsedDate;
   49:   }
   50: 
   51:   return null;
   52: }
   53: 
   54: function removeDuplicateIds(values: string[]): string[] {
   55:   return Array.from(
   56:     new Set(values.map((value) => value.trim()).filter(Boolean)),
   57:   );
   58: }
   59: 
   60: function normaliseQuizSource(value: unknown): QuizAssignmentSource {
   61:   return value === "ai-generated" ? "ai-generated" : "built-in";
   62: }
   63: 
   64: async function getStudentClassIds(studentId: string): Promise<string[]> {
   65:   const userSnapshot = await getDoc(doc(db, "users", studentId));
   66: 
   67:   if (!userSnapshot.exists()) return [];
   68: 
   69:   const data = userSnapshot.data();
   70: 
   71:   if (!Array.isArray(data.classIds)) return [];
   72: 
   73:   return removeDuplicateIds(
   74:     data.classIds.filter((value): value is string => typeof value === "string"),
   75:   );
   76: }
   77: 
   78: function splitIntoChunks(values: string[], chunkSize: number): string[][] {
   79:   const chunks: string[][] = [];
   80: 
   81:   for (let index = 0; index < values.length; index += chunkSize) {
   82:     chunks.push(values.slice(index, index + chunkSize));
   83:   }
   84: 
   85:   return chunks;
   86: }
   87: 
   88: export async function getStudentQuizAssignments(
   89:   studentId: string,
   90: ): Promise<StudentQuizAssignment[]> {
   91:   const cleanedStudentId = studentId.trim();
   92: 
   93:   if (!cleanedStudentId) return [];
   94: 
   95:   const classIds = await getStudentClassIds(cleanedStudentId);
   96: 
   97:   if (classIds.length === 0) return [];
   98: 
   99:   const classIdChunks = splitIntoChunks(classIds, 30);
  100: 
  101:   const assignmentSnapshots = await Promise.all(
  102:     classIdChunks.map((chunk) =>
  103:       getDocs(
  104:         query(
  105:           collection(db, "assignments"),
  106:           where("classId", "in", chunk),
  107:           where("type", "==", "quiz"),
  108:         ),
  109:       ),
  110:     ),
  111:   );
  112: 
  113:   const assignmentDocuments = assignmentSnapshots.flatMap(
  114:     (snapshot) => snapshot.docs,
  115:   );
  116: 
  117:   const uniqueAssignmentDocuments = Array.from(
  118:     new Map(
  119:       assignmentDocuments.map((assignmentDocument) => [
  120:         assignmentDocument.id,
  121:         assignmentDocument,
  122:       ]),
  123:     ).values(),
  124:   );
  125: 
  126:   const loadedAssignments = await Promise.all(
  127:     uniqueAssignmentDocuments.map(async (assignmentDocument) => {
  128:       const assignment = assignmentDocument.data();
  129:       const assignmentId = assignmentDocument.id;
  130:       const resultId = `${assignmentId}_${cleanedStudentId}`;
  131:       const resultReference = doc(db, "assignmentResults", resultId);
  132: 
  133:       const classReference =
  134:         typeof assignment.classId === "string" && assignment.classId.trim()
  135:           ? doc(db, "classes", assignment.classId)
  136:           : null;
  137: 
  138:       const [resultSnapshot, classSnapshot] = await Promise.all([
  139:         getDoc(resultReference),
  140:         classReference ? getDoc(classReference) : Promise.resolve(null),
  141:       ]);
  142: 
  143:       const result = resultSnapshot.exists() ? resultSnapshot.data() : null;
  144:       const classData = classSnapshot?.exists() ? classSnapshot.data() : null;
  145:       const completed = result?.status === "completed";
  146: 
  147:       return {
  148:         id: assignmentId,
  149:         teacherId:
  150:           typeof assignment.teacherId === "string" ? assignment.teacherId : "",
  151:         classId:
  152:           typeof assignment.classId === "string" ? assignment.classId : "",
  153:         title:
  154:           typeof assignment.title === "string" && assignment.title.trim()
  155:             ? assignment.title
  156:             : "Untitled Quiz",
  157:         description:
  158:           typeof assignment.description === "string"
  159:             ? assignment.description
  160:             : "",
  161:         resourceId:
  162:           typeof assignment.resourceId === "string"
  163:             ? assignment.resourceId
  164:             : "",
  165:         type: "quiz" as const,
  166:         quizSource: normaliseQuizSource(assignment.quizSource),
  167:         dueDate: convertDate(assignment.dueDate),
  168:         createdAt: convertDate(assignment.createdAt),
  169:         status:
  170:           typeof assignment.status === "string" ? assignment.status : "active",
  171:         className:
  172:           typeof classData?.name === "string" && classData.name.trim()
  173:             ? classData.name
  174:             : "Assigned class",
  175:         teacherName: "Teacher",
  176:         resultStatus: completed ? "completed" : "not_started",
  177:         score: typeof result?.score === "number" ? result.score : 0,
  178:         totalQuestions:
  179:           typeof result?.totalQuestions === "number"
  180:             ? result.totalQuestions
  181:             : 0,
  182:         percentage:
  183:           typeof result?.percentage === "number" ? result.percentage : 0,
  184:         earnedXP: typeof result?.earnedXP === "number" ? result.earnedXP : 0,
  185:         timeTakenSeconds:
  186:           typeof result?.timeTakenSeconds === "number"
  187:             ? result.timeTakenSeconds
  188:             : 0,
  189:         completedAt: convertDate(result?.completedAt),
  190:       } satisfies StudentQuizAssignment;
  191:     }),
  192:   );
  193: 
  194:   return loadedAssignments
  195:     .filter(
  196:       (assignment) =>
  197:         assignment.status !== "cancelled" &&
  198:         Boolean(assignment.resourceId.trim()),
  199:     )
  200:     .sort((first, second) => {
  201:       const firstDueDate = first.dueDate?.getTime() ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
  202:       const secondDueDate =
  203:         second.dueDate?.getTime() ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
  204: 
  205:       if (firstDueDate !== secondDueDate) return firstDueDate - secondDueDate;
  206: 
  207:       const firstCreatedAt = first.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0;
  208:       const secondCreatedAt = second.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0;
  209: 
  210:       return secondCreatedAt - firstCreatedAt;
  211:     });
  212: }

   31: 
   32: function toDate(value: unknown): Date | null {
   33:   if (!value) return null;
   34:   if (value instanceof Date) return value;
   35: 
   36:   if (
   37:     typeof value === "object" &&
   38:     value !== null &&
   39:     "toDate" in value &&
   40:     typeof (value as { toDate?: unknown }).toDate === "function"
   41:   ) {
   42:     return (value as { toDate: () => Date }).toDate();
   43:   }
   44: 
   45:   return null;
   46: }
   47: 
   48: function progressId(assignmentId: string, studentId: string): string {
   49:   return `${assignmentId}_${studentId}`;
   50: }
   51: 
   52: function toChallengeSnapshot(
   53:   challengeId: string,
   54: ): ProgrammingChallengeSnapshot {
   55:   const challenge = getProgrammingChallengeById(challengeId);
   56: 
   57:   if (!challenge) {
   58:     throw new Error(
   59:       "The selected programming challenge could not be found.",
   60:     );
   61:   }
   62: 
   63:   return {
   64:     challengeId: challenge.id,
   65:     title: challenge.title,
   66:     topicId: challenge.topicId,
   67:     skills: challenge.skills,
   68:     mode: challenge.mode,
   69:     difficulty: challenge.difficulty,
   70:     qualifications: challenge.qualifications,
   71:     examBoards: challenge.examBoards ?? [],
   72:     xpReward: challenge.xpReward,
   73:     estimatedMinutes: challenge.estimatedMinutes,
   74:   };
   75: }
   76: 
   77: function convertAssignment(
   78:   id: string,
   79:   data: Record<string, unknown>,
   80: ): ProgrammingAssignment {
   81:   const storedSnapshot =
   82:     (data.programmingChallenge as
   83:       | ProgrammingChallengeSnapshot
   84:       | undefined) ?? null;
   85: 
   86:   const challengeId = String(
   87:     data.resourceId ?? storedSnapshot?.challengeId ?? "",
   88:   );
   89: 
   90:   const challengeSnapshot =
   91:     storedSnapshot ?? toChallengeSnapshot(challengeId);
   92: 
   93:   return {
   94:     id,
   95:     teacherId: String(data.teacherId ?? ""),
   96:     teacherName: String(data.teacherName ?? "Teacher"),
   97:     classId: String(data.classId ?? ""),
   98:     className: String(data.className ?? "Class"),
   99:     studentIds: Array.isArray(data.studentIds)
  100:       ? data.studentIds.filter(
  101:           (value): value is string => typeof value === "string",
  102:         )
  103:       : [],
  104:     studentCount:
  105:       typeof data.studentCount === "number" ? data.studentCount : 0,
  106:     completedCount:
  107:       typeof data.completedCount === "number" ? data.completedCount : 0,
  108:     challengeId,
  109:     title: String(data.resourceTitle ?? challengeSnapshot.title),
  110:     topic: String(data.resourceTopic ?? challengeSnapshot.topicId),
  111:     instructions: String(data.instructions ?? ""),
  112:     dueDate: toDate(data.dueDate),
  113:     createdAt: toDate(data.createdAt),
  114:     status: data.status === "archived" ? "archived" : "active",
  115:     challengeSnapshot,
  116:   };
  117: }

  119: export type CreateProgrammingAssignmentInput = {
  120:   teacherId: string;
  121:   classId: string;
  122:   challengeId: string;
  123:   instructions: string;
  124:   dueDate: string;
  125: };
  126: 
  127: export async function createProgrammingAssignment({
  128:   teacherId,
  129:   classId,
  130:   challengeId,
  131:   instructions,
  132:   dueDate,
  133: }: CreateProgrammingAssignmentInput): Promise<string> {
  134:   const cleanedTeacherId = teacherId.trim();
  135:   const cleanedClassId = classId.trim();
  136:   const cleanedChallengeId = challengeId.trim();
  137: 
  138:   if (!cleanedTeacherId || !cleanedClassId || !cleanedChallengeId) {
  139:     throw new Error(
  140:       "Teacher, class and programming challenge are required.",
  141:     );
  142:   }
  143: 
  144:   const parsedDueDate = new Date(`${dueDate}T23:59:59`);
  145: 
  146:   if (Number.isNaN(parsedDueDate.getTime())) {
  147:     throw new Error("Select a valid due date.");
  148:   }
  149: 
  150:   const challenge = getProgrammingChallengeById(cleanedChallengeId);
  151: 
  152:   if (!challenge) {
  153:     throw new Error(
  154:       "The selected programming challenge could not be found.",
  155:     );
  156:   }
  157: 
  158:   const [classSnapshot, teacherSnapshot] = await Promise.all([
  159:     getDoc(doc(db, "classes", cleanedClassId)),
  160:     getDoc(doc(db, "users", cleanedTeacherId)),
  161:   ]);
  162: 
  163:   if (!classSnapshot.exists()) {
  164:     throw new Error("The selected class could not be found.");
  165:   }
  166: 
  167:   const classData = classSnapshot.data();
  168:   const teacherData = teacherSnapshot.exists()
  169:     ? teacherSnapshot.data()
  170:     : {};
  171: 
  172:   if (
  173:     classData.teacherId &&
  174:     classData.teacherId !== cleanedTeacherId
  175:   ) {
  176:     throw new Error(
  177:       "You cannot assign work to another teacher's class.",
  178:     );
  179:   }
  180: 
  181:   const studentIds = Array.isArray(classData.studentIds)
  182:     ? Array.from(
  183:         new Set(
  184:           classData.studentIds.filter(
  185:             (value: unknown): value is string =>
  186:               typeof value === "string" && Boolean(value.trim()),
  187:           ),
  188:         ),
  189:       )
  190:     : [];
  191: 
  192:   const teacherName = String(
  193:     teacherData.name ??
  194:       teacherData.displayName ??
  195:       teacherData.fullName ??
  196:       teacherData.email ??
  197:       "Teacher",
  198:   );
  199: 
  200:   const challengeSnapshot =
  201:     toChallengeSnapshot(challenge.id);
  202: 
  203:   const assignmentReference = await addDoc(
  204:     collection(db, "classAssignments"),
  205:     {
  206:       resourceId: challenge.id,
  207:       resourceTitle: challenge.title,
  208:       resourceTopic: challenge.topicId,
  209:       resourceType: PROGRAMMING_RESOURCE_TYPE,
  210: 
  211:       teacherId: cleanedTeacherId,
  212:       teacherName,
  213: 
  214:       classId: cleanedClassId,
  215:       className: String(classData.name ?? "Untitled Class"),
  216: 
  217:       instructions:
  218:         instructions.trim() ||
  219:         `Complete the ${challenge.title} programming challenge.`,
  220: 
  221:       dueDate: Timestamp.fromDate(parsedDueDate),
  222:       status: "active",
  223: 
  224:       studentIds,
  225:       studentCount: studentIds.length,
  226:       completedCount: 0,
  227: 
  228:       programmingChallenge: challengeSnapshot,
  229: 
  230:       createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
  231:       updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  232:     },
  233:   );
  234: 
  235:   await updateDoc(doc(db, "classes", cleanedClassId), {
  236:     assignmentIds: arrayUnion(assignmentReference.id),
  237:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  238:   });
  239: 
  240:   return assignmentReference.id;
  241: }
  242: 
  243: export async function getProgrammingAssignmentById(
  244:   assignmentId: string,
  245: ): Promise<ProgrammingAssignment | null> {
  246:   const cleanedId = assignmentId.trim();
  247:   if (!cleanedId) return null;
  248: 
  249:   const snapshot = await getDoc(
  250:     doc(db, "classAssignments", cleanedId),
  251:   );
  252: 
  253:   if (!snapshot.exists()) return null;
  254: 
  255:   const data = snapshot.data() as Record<string, unknown>;
  256: 
  257:   if (data.resourceType !== PROGRAMMING_RESOURCE_TYPE) {
  258:     return null;
  259:   }
  260: 
  261:   return convertAssignment(snapshot.id, data);
  262: }
  263: 
  264: export async function getTeacherProgrammingAssignments(
  265:   teacherId: string,
  266: ): Promise<ProgrammingAssignment[]> {
  267:   if (!teacherId.trim()) return [];
  268: 
  269:   const snapshot = await getDocs(
  270:     query(
  271:       collection(db, "classAssignments"),
  272:       where("teacherId", "==", teacherId),
  273:     ),
  274:   );
  275: 
  276:   return snapshot.docs
  277:     .filter(
  278:       (item) =>
  279:         item.data().resourceType === PROGRAMMING_RESOURCE_TYPE,
  280:     )
  281:     .map((item) =>
  282:       convertAssignment(
  283:         item.id,
  284:         item.data() as Record<string, unknown>,
  285:       ),
  286:     )
  287:     .sort(
  288:       (a, b) =>
  289:         (b.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0) -
  290:         (a.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0),
  291:     );
  292: }
  293: 
  294: export async function getProgrammingSubmission(
  295:   assignmentId: string,
  296:   studentId: string,
  297: ): Promise<ProgrammingSubmission | null> {
  298:   if (!assignmentId.trim() || !studentId.trim()) {
  299:     return null;
  300:   }
  301: 
  302:   const snapshot = await getDoc(
  303:     doc(
  304:       db,
  305:       "assignmentProgress",
  306:       progressId(assignmentId, studentId),
  307:     ),
  308:   );
  309: 
  310:   if (!snapshot.exists()) return null;
  311: 
  312:   const data = snapshot.data();
  313: 
  314:   return {
  315:     id: snapshot.id,
  316:     assignmentId,
  317:     studentId,
  318:     studentName: String(data.studentName ?? "Student"),
  319:     status:
  320:       data.status === "completed"
  321:         ? "completed"
  322:         : data.status === "in_progress"
  323:           ? "in_progress"
  324:           : "not_started",
  325:     attempts:
  326:       typeof data.programmingAttempts === "number"
  327:         ? data.programmingAttempts
  328:         : 0,
  329:     code: String(data.programmingCode ?? ""),
  330:     passedCount:
  331:       typeof data.programmingPassedCount === "number"
  332:         ? data.programmingPassedCount
  333:         : 0,
  334:     totalTests:
  335:       typeof data.programmingTotalTests === "number"
  336:         ? data.programmingTotalTests
  337:         : 0,
  338:     percentage:
  339:       typeof data.programmingPercentage === "number"
  340:         ? data.programmingPercentage
  341:         : 0,
  342:     lastPassed: data.programmingLastPassed === true,
  343:     lastError: String(data.programmingLastError ?? ""),
  344:     startedAt: toDate(data.startedAt),
  345:     completedAt: toDate(data.completedAt),
  346:     updatedAt: toDate(data.updatedAt),
  347:   };
  348: }
  349: 
  350: export type RecordProgrammingAttemptInput = {
  351:   assignmentId: string;
  352:   studentId: string;
  353:   code: string;
  354:   passed: boolean;
  355:   passedCount: number;
  356:   totalTests: number;
  357:   error?: string;
  358: };
  359: 
  360: export async function recordProgrammingAssignmentAttempt({
  361:   assignmentId,
  362:   studentId,
  363:   code,
  364:   passed,
  365:   passedCount,
  366:   totalTests,
  367:   error = "",
  368: }: RecordProgrammingAttemptInput): Promise<void> {
  369:   const assignment =
  370:     await getProgrammingAssignmentById(assignmentId);
  371: 
  372:   if (!assignment) {
  373:     throw new Error(
  374:       "The programming assignment could not be found.",
  375:     );
  376:   }
  377: 
  378:   if (!assignment.studentIds.includes(studentId)) {
  379:     throw new Error(
  380:       "You are not enrolled in this programming assignment.",
  381:     );
  382:   }
  383: 
  384:   const id = progressId(assignmentId, studentId);
  385:   const progressReference = doc(
  386:     db,
  387:     "assignmentProgress",
  388:     id,
  389:   );
  390:   const existing = await getDoc(progressReference);
  391: 
  392:   const previousAttempts =
  393:     existing.exists() &&
  394:     typeof existing.data().programmingAttempts === "number"
  395:       ? existing.data().programmingAttempts
  396:       : 0;
  397: 
  398:   const studentSnapshot = await getDoc(
  399:     doc(db, "users", studentId),
  400:   );
  401:   const studentData = studentSnapshot.exists()
  402:     ? studentSnapshot.data()
  403:     : {};
  404: 
  405:   const percentage =
  406:     totalTests > 0
  407:       ? Math.round(
  408:           (Math.max(0, passedCount) / totalTests) * 100,
  409:         )
  410:       : 0;
  411: 
  412:   await setDoc(
  413:     progressReference,
  414:     {
  415:       assignmentId,
  416:       studentId,
  417:       studentName: String(
  418:         studentData.name ??
  419:           studentData.displayName ??
  420:           studentData.fullName ??
  421:           studentData.email ??
  422:           "Student",
  423:       ),
  424:       programmingChallengeId: assignment.challengeId,
  425:       programmingCode: code,
  426:       programmingAttempts: previousAttempts + 1,
  427:       programmingPassedCount: passedCount,
  428:       programmingTotalTests: totalTests,
  429:       programmingPercentage: percentage,
  430:       programmingLastPassed: passed,
  431:       programmingLastError: error,
  432:       updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  433:     },
  434:     { merge: true },
  435:   );
  436: 
  437:   if (passed) {
  438:     await completeStudentAssignment(
  439:       assignmentId,
  440:       studentId,
  441:     );
  442:   } else {
  443:     await startStudentAssignment(
  444:       assignmentId,
  445:       studentId,
  446:     );
  447:   }
  448: }
  449: 
  450: export async function getProgrammingAssignmentResults(
  451:   assignmentId: string,
  452: ): Promise<ProgrammingAssignmentResultsSummary | null> {
  453:   const assignment =
  454:     await getProgrammingAssignmentById(assignmentId);
  455: 
  456:   if (!assignment) return null;
  457: 
  458:   const progressSnapshot = await getDocs(
  459:     query(
  460:       collection(db, "assignmentProgress"),
  461:       where("assignmentId", "==", assignmentId),
  462:     ),
  463:   );
  464: 
  465:   const progressByStudent = new Map(
  466:     progressSnapshot.docs.map((item) => [
  467:       item.data().studentId,
  468:       item.data(),
  469:     ]),
  470:   );
  471: 
  472:   const results: ProgrammingAssignmentResult[] =
  473:     await Promise.all(
  474:       assignment.studentIds.map(async (studentId) => {
  475:         const studentSnapshot = await getDoc(
  476:           doc(db, "users", studentId),
  477:         );
  478:         const studentData = studentSnapshot.exists()
  479:           ? studentSnapshot.data()
  480:           : {};
  481:         const progress = progressByStudent.get(studentId);
  482: 
  483:         const status =
  484:           progress?.status === "completed"
  485:             ? "completed"
  486:             : progress?.status === "in_progress"
  487:               ? "in_progress"
  488:               : "not_started";
  489: 
  490:         return {
  491:           studentId,
  492:           studentName: String(
  493:             progress?.studentName ??
  494:               studentData.name ??
  495:               studentData.displayName ??
  496:               studentData.fullName ??
  497:               studentData.email ??
  498:               "Student",
  499:           ),
  500:           status,
  501:           attempts:
  502:             typeof progress?.programmingAttempts === "number"
  503:               ? progress.programmingAttempts
  504:               : 0,
  505:           passedCount:
  506:             typeof progress?.programmingPassedCount === "number"
  507:               ? progress.programmingPassedCount
  508:               : 0,
  509:           totalTests:
  510:             typeof progress?.programmingTotalTests === "number"
  511:               ? progress.programmingTotalTests
  512:               : 0,
  513:           percentage:
  514:             typeof progress?.programmingPercentage === "number"
  515:               ? progress.programmingPercentage
  516:               : 0,
  517:           completedAt: toDate(progress?.completedAt),
  518:         };
  519:       }),
  520:     );
  521: 
  522:   const completed = results.filter(
  523:     (result) => result.status === "completed",
  524:   ).length;
  525: 
  526:   const inProgress = results.filter(
  527:     (result) => result.status === "in_progress",
  528:   ).length;
  529: 
  530:   const notStarted =
  531:     results.length - completed - inProgress;
  532: 
  533:   const attempted = results.filter(
  534:     (result) => result.attempts > 0,
  535:   );
  536: 
  537:   const averagePercentage =
  538:     attempted.length > 0
  539:       ? Math.round(
  540:           attempted.reduce(
  541:             (total, result) =>
  542:               total + result.percentage,
  543:             0,
  544:           ) / attempted.length,
  545:         )
  546:       : 0;
  547: 
  548:   return {
  549:     assignment,
  550:     results,
  551:     completed,
  552:     inProgress,
  553:     notStarted,
  554:     completionPercentage:
  555:       results.length > 0
  556:         ? Math.round(
  557:             (completed / results.length) * 100,
  558:           )
  559:         : 0,
  560:     averagePercentage,
  561:   };
  562: }
  563: 
  564: export const PROGRAMMING_ASSIGNMENT_RESOURCE_TYPE =
  565:   PROGRAMMING_RESOURCE_TYPE;

  108: function convertAssignmentDocument(
  109:   assignmentId: string,

  148: function createDefaultStudentProgress(

  166: function validateAssignmentInput(input: CreateResourceAssignmentInput): void {
  167:   if (!input.resourceId.trim()) {
  168:     throw new Error("A valid teaching resource is required.");
  169:   }
  170: 
  171:   if (!input.teacherId.trim()) {
  172:     throw new Error("A valid teacher account is required.");
  173:   }
  174: 
  175:   if (!input.classId.trim()) {
  176:     throw new Error("Select a class before assigning the resource.");
  177:   }
  178: 
  179:   if (!input.className.trim()) {
  180:     throw new Error("The selected class does not have a valid name.");
  181:   }
  182: 
  183:   if (
  184:     !(input.dueDate instanceof Date) ||
  185:     Number.isNaN(input.dueDate.getTime())
  186:   ) {
  187:     throw new Error("Select a valid due date.");
  188:   }
  189: 
  190:   const today = new Date();
  191: 
  192:   today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  193: 
  194:   if (input.dueDate < today) {
  195:     throw new Error("The due date cannot be in the past.");
  196:   }
  197: }
  198: 
  199: export async function createResourceAssignment(
  200:   input: CreateResourceAssignmentInput,
  201: ): Promise<string> {
  202:   validateAssignmentInput(input);
  203: 
  204:   const uniqueStudentIds = Array.from(
  205:     new Set(
  206:       input.studentIds.map((studentId) => studentId.trim()).filter(Boolean),
  207:     ),
  208:   );
  209: 
  210:   const assignmentReference = await addDoc(collection(db, "classAssignments"), {
  211:     resourceId: input.resourceId.trim(),
  212:     resourceTitle: input.resourceTitle.trim(),
  213:     resourceTopic: input.resourceTopic.trim(),
  214:     resourceType: input.resourceType.trim(),
  215: 
  216:     teacherId: input.teacherId.trim(),
  217:     teacherName: input.teacherName?.trim() || "Teacher",
  218: 
  219:     classId: input.classId.trim(),
  220:     className: input.className.trim(),
  221: 
  222:     instructions: input.instructions?.trim() || "",
  223: 
  224:     dueDate: Timestamp.fromDate(input.dueDate),
  225: 
  226:     status: "active",
  227: 
  228:     studentIds: uniqueStudentIds,
  229:     studentCount: uniqueStudentIds.length,
  230:     completedCount: 0,
  231: 
  232:     createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
  233:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  234:   });
  235: 
  236:   return assignmentReference.id;
  237: }

  239: export async function getTeacherAssignments(
  240:   teacherId: string,
  241: ): Promise<ResourceAssignment[]> {
  242:   if (!teacherId.trim()) {
  243:     return [];
  244:   }
  245: 
  246:   const assignmentsQuery = query(
  247:     collection(db, "classAssignments"),
  248:     where("teacherId", "==", teacherId),
  249:     orderBy("createdAt", "desc"),
  250:   );
  251: 
  252:   const snapshot = await getDocs(assignmentsQuery);
  253: 
  254:   return snapshot.docs.map((assignmentDocument) =>
  255:     convertAssignmentDocument(
  256:       assignmentDocument.id,
  257:       assignmentDocument.data() as FirestoreResourceAssignment,
  258:     ),
  259:   );
  260: }

  262: export async function getStudentAssignments(
  263:   studentId: string,
  264: ): Promise<StudentAssignmentWithProgress[]> {
  265:   if (!studentId.trim()) {
  266:     return [];
  267:   }
  268: 
  269:   const assignmentsQuery = query(
  270:     collection(db, "classAssignments"),
  271:     where("studentIds", "array-contains", studentId),
  272:     orderBy("dueDate", "asc"),
  273:   );
  274: 
  275:   const snapshot = await getDocs(assignmentsQuery);
  276: 
  277:   const assignments = snapshot.docs.map((assignmentDocument) =>
  278:     convertAssignmentDocument(
  279:       assignmentDocument.id,
  280:       assignmentDocument.data() as FirestoreResourceAssignment,
  281:     ),
  282:   );
  283: 
  284:   return Promise.all(
  285:     assignments.map(async (assignment) => {
  286:       const studentProgress = await getStudentAssignmentProgress(
  287:         assignment.id,
  288:         studentId,
  289:       );
  290: 
  291:       return {
  292:         ...assignment,
  293:         studentProgress,
  294:       };
  295:     }),
  296:   );
  297: }

  299: export async function getAssignmentById(
  300:   assignmentId: string,
  301: ): Promise<ResourceAssignment | null> {
  302:   if (!assignmentId.trim()) {
  303:     return null;
  304:   }
  305: 
  306:   const assignmentReference = doc(db, "classAssignments", assignmentId);
  307: 
  308:   const snapshot = await getDoc(assignmentReference);
  309:

  320: export async function getStudentAssignmentProgress(
  321:   assignmentId: string,
  322:   studentId: string,
  323: ): Promise<StudentAssignmentProgress> {
  324:   if (!assignmentId.trim() || !studentId.trim()) {
  325:     return createDefaultStudentProgress(assignmentId, studentId);
  326:   }
  327: 
  328:   const progressId = createProgressDocumentId(assignmentId, studentId);
  329: 
  330:   const progressReference = doc(db, "assignmentProgress", progressId);
  331: 
  332:   const snapshot = await getDoc(progressReference);
  333: 
  334:   if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  335:     return createDefaultStudentProgress(assignmentId, studentId);
  336:   }
  337: 
  338:   const data = snapshot.data() as FirestoreStudentAssignmentProgress;
  339: 
  340:   return {
  341:     id: snapshot.id,
  342: 
  343:     assignmentId: data.assignmentId ?? assignmentId,
  344: 
  345:     studentId: data.studentId ?? studentId,
  346: 
  347:     status: data.status ?? "not_started",
  348: 
  349:     startedAt: convertTimestamp(data.startedAt),
  350: 
  351:     completedAt: convertTimestamp(data.completedAt),
  352: 
  353:     updatedAt: convertTimestamp(data.updatedAt),
  354:   };
  355: }
  356: 
  357: export async function startStudentAssignment(
  358:   assignmentId: string,
  359:   studentId: string,
  360: ): Promise<void> {
  361:   if (!assignmentId.trim() || !studentId.trim()) {
  362:     throw new Error("A valid assignment and student are required.");
  363:   }
  364: 
  365:   const progressId = createProgressDocumentId(assignmentId, studentId);
  366: 
  367:   const progressReference = doc(db, "assignmentProgress", progressId);
  368: 
  369:   const existingProgress = await getDoc(progressReference);
  370: 
  371:   if (
  372:     existingProgress.exists() &&
  373:     existingProgress.data().status === "completed"
  374:   ) {
  375:     return;
  376:   }
  377: 
  378:   await setDoc(
  379:     progressReference,
  380:     {
  381:       assignmentId,
  382:       studentId,
  383: 
  384:       status: "in_progress",
  385: 
  386:       startedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  387:       completedAt: null,
  388:       updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  389:     },
  390:     {
  391:       merge: true,
  392:     },
  393:   );
  394: }
  395: 
  396: export async function completeStudentAssignment(
  397:   assignmentId: string,
  398:   studentId: string,
  399: ): Promise<void> {
  400:   if (!assignmentId.trim() || !studentId.trim()) {
  401:     throw new Error("A valid assignment and student are required.");
  402:   }
  403: 
  404:   const assignmentReference = doc(db, "classAssignments", assignmentId);
  405: 
  406:   const progressId = createProgressDocumentId(assignmentId, studentId);
  407: 
  408:   const progressReference = doc(db, "assignmentProgress", progressId);
  409: 
  410:   await runTransaction(db, async (transaction) => {
  411:     const assignmentSnapshot = await transaction.get(assignmentReference);
  412: 
  413:     if (!assignmentSnapshot.exists()) {
  414:       throw new Error("This assignment could not be found.");
  415:     }
  416: 
  417:     const assignmentData =
  418:       assignmentSnapshot.data() as FirestoreResourceAssignment;
  419: 
  420:     if (!assignmentData.studentIds?.includes(studentId)) {
  421:       throw new Error("You are not enrolled in this assignment.");
  422:     }
  423: 
  424:     const progressSnapshot = await transaction.get(progressReference);
  425: 
  426:     const alreadyCompleted =
  427:       progressSnapshot.exists() &&
  428:       progressSnapshot.data().status === "completed";
  429: 
  430:     if (alreadyCompleted) {
  431:       return;
  432:     }
  433: 
  434:     transaction.set(
  435:       progressReference,
  436:       {
  437:         assignmentId,
  438:         studentId,
  439: 
  440:         status: "completed",
  441: 
  442:         startedAt: progressSnapshot.exists()
  443:           ? (progressSnapshot.data().startedAt ?? serverTimestamp())
  444:           : serverTimestamp(),
  445: 
  446:         completedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  447:         updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  448:       },
  449:       {
  450:         merge: true,
  451:       },
  452:     );
  453: 
  454:     const currentCompletedCount = assignmentData.completedCount ?? 0;
  455: 
  456:     const maximumCompletedCount =
  457:       assignmentData.studentCount ?? assignmentData.studentIds.length;
  458: 
  459:     transaction.update(assignmentReference, {
  460:       completedCount: Math.min(
  461:         currentCompletedCount + 1,
  462:         maximumCompletedCount,
  463:       ),
  464: 
  465:       updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  466:     });
  467:   });
  468: }
  469: 
  470: export async function updateAssignmentStatus(
  471:   assignmentId: string,
  472:   status: AssignmentStatus,
  473: ): Promise<void> {
  474:   await updateDoc(doc(db, "classAssignments", assignmentId), {
  475:     status,
  476:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  477:   });
  478: }

  480: export async function updateAssignmentDetails(
  481:   assignmentId: string,
  482:   updates: {
  483:     instructions?: string;
  484:     dueDate?: Date;
  485:   },
  486: ): Promise<void> {
  487:   const cleanedUpdates: {
  488:     instructions?: string;
  489:     dueDate?: Timestamp;
  490:     updatedAt: ReturnType<typeof serverTimestamp>;
  491:   } = {
  492:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  493:   };
  494: 
  495:   if (updates.instructions !== undefined) {
  496:     cleanedUpdates.instructions = updates.instructions.trim();
  497:   }
  498: 
  499:   if (updates.dueDate !== undefined) {
  500:     if (Number.isNaN(updates.dueDate.getTime())) {
  501:       throw new Error("Select a valid due date.");
  502:     }
  503: 
  504:     cleanedUpdates.dueDate = Timestamp.fromDate(updates.dueDate);
  505:   }
  506: 
  507:   await updateDoc(doc(db, "classAssignments", assignmentId), cleanedUpdates);
  508: }
  509: export async function getAssignmentProgressForTeacher(
  510:   assignmentId: string,
  511: ): Promise<StudentAssignmentProgress[]> {
  512:   if (!assignmentId.trim()) {
  513:     return [];
  514:   }
  515: 
  516:   const assignment = await getAssignmentById(assignmentId);
  517: 
  518:   if (!assignment) {
  519:     return [];
  520:   }
  521: 
  522:   return Promise.all(
  523:     assignment.studentIds.map((studentId) =>
  524:       getStudentAssignmentProgress(assignmentId, studentId),
  525:     ),
  526:   );
  527: }

  112:         "code" in caughtError &&
  113:         typeof (caughtError as { code?: unknown }).code === "string"
  114:           ? (caughtError as { code: string }).code

  124:           : caughtError instanceof Error

  126:             : "Intervention data could not be loaded.",
  127:       );

  130:     }

  159:           {item.status !== "completed" && (

  161:               type="button"
  162:               onClick={() =>
  163:                 void updateInterventionStatus(
  164:                   item.id,
  165:                   "completed",
  166:                 ).then(load)
  167:               }
  168:               className="rounded-xl border px-5 py-3 font-bold"
  169:             >
  170:               Mark completed manually
  171:             </button>
  172:           )}
  173: 
  174:           {item.status !== "cancelled" && (
  175:             <button
  176:               type="button"
  177:               onClick={() =>
  178:                 void updateInterventionStatus(
  179:                   item.id,
  180:                   "cancelled",
  181:                 ).then(load)
  182:               }
  183:               className="rounded-xl border border-red-300 px-5 py-3 font-bold text-red-700"
  184:             >
  185:               Cancel
  186:             </button>
  187:           )}
  188:         </div>
  189:       </Card>
  190:     </div>
  191:   );
  192: }
  193: 
  194: function Metric({
  195:   label,
  196:   value,
  197: }: {
  198:   label: string;
  199:   value: string;
  200: }) {
  201:   return (
  202:     <Card>
  203:       <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-500">
  204:         {label}
  205:       </p>
  206:       <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black">
  207:         {value}
  208:       </p>
  209:     </Card>
  210:   );
  211: }

   30:     "lesson" | "quiz" | "exam" | "complete"

   76:       toast.error("Choose a due date.");

  100:         examAssignmentId: examId.trim(),

  106:         createdResult.trim()
  107:       ) {

FILE: components\teacher\interventions\InterventionEffectivenessOverview.tsx

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import Link from "next/link";
    4: import {
    5:   Activity,
    6:   AlertTriangle,
    7:   ArrowRight,
    8:   CheckCircle2,
    9:   Clock3,
   10:   RefreshCcw,
   11:   Target,
   12: } from "lucide-react";
   13: import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
   14: 
   15: import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
   16: import {
   17:   buildInterventionEffectivenessItem,
   18:   buildInterventionEffectivenessSummary,
   19:   type InterventionEffectivenessSource,
   20: } from "@/services/analytics/interventionEffectivenessService";
   21: import { getInterventionImpact } from "@/services/analytics/interventionImpactService";
   22: import type {
   23:   InterventionEffectivenessItem,
   24:   InterventionEffectivenessStatus,
   25: } from "@/types/interventionEffectiveness";
   26: 
   27: function badgeStyle(status: InterventionEffectivenessStatus): string {
   28:   switch (status) {
   29:     case "improving":
   30:       return "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800";
   31:     case "declining":
   32:       return "bg-red-100 text-red-800";
   33:     case "awaiting_evidence":
   34:       return "bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-800";
   35:     case "baseline_needed":
   36:       return "bg-amber-100 text-amber-800";
   37:     default:
   38:       return "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700";
   39:   }
   40: }
   41: 
   42: function changeText(value: number | null, suffix = ""): string {
   43:   if (value === null) return "â€”";
   44:   if (value > 0) return `+${value}${suffix}`;
   45:   return `${value}${suffix}`;
   46: }
   47: 
   48: export default function InterventionEffectivenessOverview({
   49:   teacherId,
   50:   interventions,
   51: }: {
   52:   teacherId: string;
   53:   interventions: InterventionEffectivenessSource[];
   54: }) {
   55:   const [items, setItems] = useState<InterventionEffectivenessItem[]>([]);
   56:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   57:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   58: 
   59:   const load = useCallback(() => {
   60:     if (!teacherId.trim() || interventions.length === 0) {
   61:       return Promise.resolve().then(() => {
   62:         setItems([]);
   63:         setError("");
   64:         setLoading(false);
   65:       });
   66:     }
   67: 
   68:     return Promise.resolve()
   69:       .then(() => {
   70:         setLoading(true);
   71:         setError("");
   72: 
   73:         return Promise.all(
   74:           interventions.map(async (intervention) => {
   75:             try {
   76:               const impact = await getInterventionImpact({
   77:                 interventionId: intervention.id,
   78:                 teacherId,
   79:               });
   80: 
   81:               if (!impact) return null;
   82: 
   83:               return buildInterventionEffectivenessItem({
   84:                 source: intervention,
   85:                 impact,
   86:               });
   87:             } catch (caughtError) {
   88:               console.error(
   89:                 `Unable to load intervention effectiveness for ${intervention.id}:`,
   90:                 caughtError,
   91:               );
   92: 
   93:               return null;
   94:             }
   95:           }),
   96:         );
   97:       })
   98:       .then((results) => {
   99:         setItems(
  100:           results.filter(
  101:             (item): item is InterventionEffectivenessItem => Boolean(item),
  102:           ),
  103:         );
  104:       })
  105:       .catch((caughtError) => {
  106:         setError(
  107:           caughtError instanceof Error
  108:             ? caughtError.message
  109:             : "Intervention effectiveness could not be loaded.",
  110:         );
  111:       })
  112:       .finally(() => {
  113:         setLoading(false);
  114:       });
  115:   }, [teacherId, interventions]);
  116: 
  117:   useEffect(() => {
  118:     void load();
  119:   }, [load]);
  120: 
  121:   const summary = useMemo(
  122:     () => buildInterventionEffectivenessSummary(items),
  123:     [items],
  124:   );
  125: 
  126:   return (
  127:     <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-teal-200 p-0">
  128:       <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-950 via-teal-950 to-emerald-900 p-6 text-white">
  129:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 lg:flex-row lg:items-end lg:justify-between">
  130:           <div>
  131:             <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-teal-200">
  132:               Intervention effectiveness intelligence
  133:             </p>
  134: 
  135:             <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black">
  136:               Which interventions are producing improvement?
  137:             </h2>
  138: 
  139:             <p className="mt-2 max-w-4xl text-sm leading-6 text-white/70">
  140:               Compare each intervention baseline with subsequent graded
  141:               evidence. Statuses are decision-support signals and should be
  142:               reviewed alongside the underlying assessment evidence.
  143:             </p>
  144:           </div>
  145: 
  146:           <button
  147:             type="button"
  148:             onClick={() => void load()}
  149:             className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-white px-4 text-sm font-black text-teal-950"
  150:           >
  151:             <RefreshCcw className="h-4 w-4" />
  152:             Refresh effectiveness
  153:           </button>
  154:         </div>
  155:       </div>
  156: 
  157:       {loading ? (
  158:         <div className="grid gap-4 p-6 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-5">
  159:           {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, index) => (
  160:             <div
  161:               key={index}
  162:               className="h-24 animate-pulse rounded-2xl bg-slate-100"
  163:             />
  164:           ))}
  165:         </div>
  166:       ) : error ? (
  167:         <div className="p-6">
  168:           <div className="rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-5 text-sm font-bold text-amber-900">
  169:             {error}
  170:           </div>
  171:         </div>
  172:       ) : interventions.length === 0 ? (
  173:         <div className="p-6">
  174:           <p className="font-black text-slate-950">
  175:             No interventions available yet
  176:           </p>
  177:           <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
  178:             Create an intervention and capture a baseline before effectiveness
  179:             can be evaluated.
  180:           </p>
  181:         </div>
  182:       ) : (
  183:         <>
  184:           <div className="grid gap-4 p-6 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-5">
  185:             <Summary
  186:               label="Improving"
  187:               value={summary.improving}
  188:               icon={<CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5" />}
  189:               tone="emerald"
  190:             />
  191:             <Summary
  192:               label="Limited change"
  193:               value={summary.limitedChange}
  194:               icon={<Activity className="h-5 w-5" />}
  195:               tone="slate"
  196:             />
  197:             <Summary
  198:               label="Declining"
  199:               value={summary.declining}
  200:               icon={<AlertTriangle className="h-5 w-5" />}
  201:               tone="red"
  202:             />

  204:               label="Awaiting evidence"
  205:               value={summary.awaitingEvidence}
  206:               icon={<Clock3 className="h-5 w-5" />}
  207:               tone="indigo"
  208:             />
  209:             <Summary
  210:               label="Success rate"
  211:               value={
  212:                 summary.successfulRate === null
  213:                   ? "â€”"
  214:                   : `${summary.successfulRate}%`
  215:               }
  216:               icon={<Target className="h-5 w-5" />}
  217:               tone="teal"
  218:             />
  219:           </div>
  220: 
  221:           {summary.baselineNeeded > 0 && (
  222:             <div className="mx-6 mb-6 rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-4 text-sm text-amber-900">
  223:               <span className="font-black">
  224:                 {summary.baselineNeeded} intervention
  225:                 {summary.baselineNeeded === 1 ? "" : "s"} need a baseline.
  226:               </span>{" "}
  227:               Effectiveness cannot be judged reliably until a before-intervention
  228:               comparison point exists.
  229:             </div>
  230:           )}
  231: 
  232:           <div className="border-t border-slate-100">
  233:             <div className="grid grid-cols-[1.3fr_1fr_1fr_0.8fr_0.8fr_auto] gap-3 bg-slate-50 px-6 py-3 text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-400">
  234:               <span>Learner / topic</span>
  235:               <span>Status</span>
  236:               <span>Attainment</span>
  237:               <span>Mastery</span>
  238:               <span>Evidence</span>
  239:               <span>Action</span>
  240:             </div>
  241: 
  242:             {summary.items.map((item) => (
  243:               <div
  244:                 key={item.interventionId}
  245:                 className="grid gap-4 border-t border-slate-100 px-6 py-5 lg:grid-cols-[1.3fr_1fr_1fr_0.8fr_0.8fr_auto] lg:items-center"
  246:               >
  247:                 <div>
  248:                   <p className="font-black text-slate-950">
  249:                     {item.studentName}
  250:                   </p>
  251:                   <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-teal-700">
  252:                     {item.topic}
  253:                   </p>
  254:                   <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
  255:                     {item.summary}
  256:                   </p>
  257:                 </div>
  258: 
  259:                 <div>
  260:                   <span
  261:                     className={`inline-flex rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-black ${badgeStyle(
  262:                       item.effectivenessStatus,
  263:                     )}`}
  264:                   >
  265:                     {item.headline}
  266:                   </span>
  267:                 </div>
  268: 
  269:                 <p className="font-black text-slate-800">
  270:                   {changeText(item.attainmentChange, "%")}
  271:                 </p>
  272: 
  273:                 <p className="font-black text-slate-800">
  274:                   {changeText(item.topicMasteryChange, "%")}
  275:                 </p>
  276: 
  277:                 <p className="font-black text-slate-800">
  278:                   +{item.evidenceChange}
  279:                 </p>
  280: 
  281:                 <Link
  282:                   href={item.reviewHref}
  283:                   className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-950 px-4 text-xs font-black text-white"
  284:                 >
  285:                   Review
  286:                   <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
  287:                 </Link>
  288:               </div>
  289:             ))}
  290:           </div>
  291: 
  292:           <div className="border-t border-teal-100 bg-teal-50 px-6 py-4 text-xs font-bold leading-5 text-teal-950">
  293:             Success rate only uses interventions with enough post-baseline
  294:             evidence to classify as improving, limited/mixed change or declining.
  295:             Interventions awaiting evidence or a baseline are excluded.
  296:           </div>
  297:         </>
  298:       )}
  299:     </Card>
  300:   );
  301: }
  302: 
  303: function Summary({
  304:   label,
  305:   value,
  306:   icon,
  307:   tone,
  308: }: {
  309:   label: string;
  310:   value: number | string;
  311:   icon: React.ReactNode;
  312:   tone: "emerald" | "slate" | "red" | "indigo" | "teal";
  313: }) {
  314:   const styles = {
  315:     emerald: "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700",
  316:     slate: "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700",
  317:     red: "bg-red-50 text-red-700",
  318:     indigo: "bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700",
  319:     teal: "bg-teal-50 text-teal-700",
  320:   };
  321: 
  322:   return (
  323:     <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-4">
  324:       <div
  325:         className={`flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl ${styles[tone]}`}
  326:       >
  327:         {icon}
  328:       </div>
  329:       <p className="mt-4 text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-400">
  330:         {label}
  331:       </p>
  332:       <p className="mt-1 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">{value}</p>
  333:     </div>
  334:   );
  335: }

   34: function delta(
   35:   value: number | null,
   36:   suffix = "",
   37: ): string {
   38:   if (value === null) return "â€”";
   39:   return value > 0 ? `+${value}${suffix}` : `${value}${suffix}`;
   40: }
   41: 
   42: function decisionLabel(
   43:   value: InterventionReviewDecision,
   44: ): string {
   45:   switch (value) {
   46:     case "close_successful":
   47:       return "Close as successful";
   48:     case "retarget":
   49:       return "Retarget support";
   50:     case "escalate":
   51:       return "Escalate support";
   52:     default:
   53:       return "Continue intervention";
   54:   }

   57: export default function InterventionImpactCard({
   58:   interventionId,

   63: }) {
   64:   const [impact, setImpact] =
   65:     useState<InterventionImpact | null>(null);
   66: 
   67:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   68:   const [savingBaseline, setSavingBaseline] = useState(false);
   69:   const [savingDecision, setSavingDecision] = useState(false);
   70:   const [reviewNote, setReviewNote] = useState("");
   71:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   72: 
   73:   const load = useCallback(() => {
   74:     return Promise.resolve()
   75:       .then(() => {
   76:         setLoading(true);
   77:         setError("");
   78: 
   79:         return getInterventionImpact({
   80:           interventionId,
   81:           teacherId,
   82:         });
   83:       })
   84:       .then((loadedImpact) => {
   85:         setImpact(loadedImpact);
   86:       })
   87:       .catch((caughtError) => {
   88:         console.error(
   89:           "Unable to load intervention impact:",
   90:           caughtError,
   91:         );
   92: 
   93:         setError(
   94:           caughtError instanceof Error
   95:             ? caughtError.message
   96:             : "Intervention impact could not be loaded.",
   97:         );
   98:       })
   99:       .finally(() => {
  100:         setLoading(false);
  101:       });
  102:   }, [interventionId, teacherId]);
  103: 
  104:   useEffect(() => {
  105:     void load();
  106:   }, [load]);
  107: 
  108:   const review = useMemo(
  109:     () =>
  110:       impact
  111:         ? buildInterventionReviewCycle(impact)
  112:         : null,
  113:     [impact],
  114:   );
  115: 
  116:   if (loading) {
  117:     return (
  118:       <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200">
  119:         <div className="h-56 animate-pulse rounded-2xl bg-slate-100" />
  120:       </Card>
  121:     );
  122:   }
  123: 
  124:   if (!impact) {
  125:     return error ? <Card>{error}</Card> : null;
  126:   }
  127: 
  128:   async function captureBaseline() {
  129:     if (!impact) {
  130:       return;
  131:     }
  132: 
  133:     try {
  134:       setSavingBaseline(true);
  135: 
  136:       await captureInterventionBaseline({
  137:         interventionId,
  138:         teacherId,
  139:         studentId: impact.studentId,
  140:         topic: impact.topic,
  141:       });
  142: 
  143:       toast.success("Intervention baseline captured.");
  144:       await load();
  145:     } catch (caughtError) {
  146:       toast.error(
  147:         caughtError instanceof Error
  148:           ? caughtError.message
  149:           : "Baseline could not be captured.",
  150:       );
  151:     } finally {
  152:       setSavingBaseline(false);
  153:     }
  154:   }
  155: 
  156:   async function saveDecision(
  157:     decision: InterventionReviewDecision,
  158:   ) {
  159:     if (!impact) {
  160:       return;
  161:     }
  162:

  166:       await saveInterventionReviewDecision({

  172:

  174:         decision === "close_successful"
  175:           ? "Intervention closed as successful."
  176:           : "Intervention review saved.",
  177:       );
  178: 
  179:       await load();
  180:     } catch (caughtError) {
  181:       toast.error(
  182:         caughtError instanceof Error
  183:           ? caughtError.message
  184:           : "The intervention review could not be saved.",
  185:       );
  186:     } finally {
  187:       setSavingDecision(false);
  188:     }

  192:     buildInterventionReassessmentHref({
  193:       impact,

  195:

  201:             <Activity className="h-5 w-5" />
  202:             <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.16em]">
  203:               Intervention impact
  204:             </p>
  205:           </div>

  427:             <label className="block">

  429:                 Teacher review note
  430:               </span>
  431: 
  432:               <textarea
  433:                 rows={3}
  434:                 value={reviewNote}
  435:                 onChange={(event) =>
  436:                   setReviewNote(event.target.value)
  437:                 }
  438:                 placeholder="Record why you are continuing, retargeting, closing or escalating the intervention."
  439:                 className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm"
  440:               />
  441:             </label>
  442: 
  443:             <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  444:               <button
  445:                 type="button"
  446:                 disabled={savingDecision}
  447:                 onClick={() =>
  448:                   void saveDecision(
  449:                     review.recommendedDecision,
  450:                   )
  451:                 }
  452:                 className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-teal-600 px-4 text-xs font-black text-white disabled:opacity-60"
  453:               >
  454:                 <ClipboardCheck className="h-4 w-4" />
  455:                 {savingDecision
  456:                   ? "Saving..."
  457:                   : decisionLabel(
  458:                       review.recommendedDecision,
  459:                     )}
  460:               </button>
  461: 
  462:               {review.recommendedDecision !== "continue" && (
  463:                 <button
  464:                   type="button"
  465:                   disabled={savingDecision}
  466:                   onClick={() =>
  467:                     void saveDecision("continue")
  468:                   }
  469:                   className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 text-xs font-black text-slate-700 disabled:opacity-60"
  470:                 >
  471:                   Continue instead
  472:                 </button>
  473:               )}
  474: 
  475:               {review.canCloseSuccessful &&
  476:                 review.recommendedDecision !==
  477:                   "close_successful" && (
  478:                   <button
  479:                     type="button"
  480:                     disabled={savingDecision}
  481:                     onClick={() =>
  482:                       void saveDecision(
  483:                         "close_successful",
  484:                       )
  485:                     }
  486:                     className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 px-4 text-xs font-black text-white disabled:opacity-60"
  487:                   >
  488:                     Close as successful
  489:                   </button>
  490:                 )}
  491:             </div>
  492:           </div>
  493:         </Card>
  494:       )}
  495:     </div>
  496:   );
  497: }
  498: 
  499: function Metric({
  500:   label,

  513:         {label}
  514:       </p>
  515: 
  516:       <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
  517:         {before} â†’
  518:       </p>

FILE: services\interventionService.ts
==============================================================================================================

    1: import {
    2:   addDoc,
    3:   collection,
    4:   doc,
    5:   getDoc,
    6:   getDocs,
    7:   orderBy,
    8:   query,
    9:   serverTimestamp,
   10:   Timestamp,
   11:   updateDoc,
   12:   where,
   13: } from "firebase/firestore";
   14: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
   15: import type {
   16:   CreateInterventionInput,
   17:   Intervention,
   18:   InterventionStep,
   19:   InterventionStepStatus,
   20:   InterventionStatus,
   21: } from "@/types/intervention";
   22: 
   23: type FirestoreStep = Omit<InterventionStep, "completedAt"> & {
   24:   completedAt?: Timestamp | null;
   25: };
   26: type FirestoreIntervention = Omit<
   27:   Intervention,
   28:   "id" | "dueDate" | "steps" | "createdAt" | "updatedAt" | "completedAt"
   29: > & {
   30:   dueDate?: Timestamp;
   31:   steps?: FirestoreStep[];
   32:   createdAt?: Timestamp;
   33:   updatedAt?: Timestamp;
   34:   completedAt?: Timestamp | null;
   35: };
   36: 
   37: const toDate = (value?: Timestamp | null) =>
   38:   value?.toDate ? value.toDate() : null;
   39: const clamp = (value: number) => Math.min(100, Math.max(0, value));
   40: 
   41: function convert(id: string, data: FirestoreIntervention): Intervention {
   42:   return {
   43:     id,
   44:     ...data,
   45:     teacherName: data.teacherName || "Teacher",
   46:     studentName: data.studentName || "Student",
   47:     studentEmail: data.studentEmail || "",
   48:     classId: data.classId || "",
   49:     className: data.className || "",
   50:     reason: data.reason || "",
   51:     priority: data.priority || "medium",
   52:     status: data.status || "active",
   53:     baselineScore: clamp(data.baselineScore || 0),
   54:     currentScore: clamp(data.currentScore || 0),
   55:     impact: typeof data.impact === "number" ? data.impact : 0,
   56:     dueDate: toDate(data.dueDate),
   57:     steps: (data.steps || []).map((step) => ({
   58:       ...step,
   59:       completedAt: toDate(step.completedAt),
   60:     })),
   61:     createdAt: toDate(data.createdAt),
   62:     updatedAt: toDate(data.updatedAt),
   63:     completedAt: toDate(data.completedAt),
   64:   };
   65: }
   66: 
   67: function buildSteps(input: CreateInterventionInput): InterventionStep[] {
   68:   const xp = Math.max(0, input.xpPerStep || 25);
   69:   const steps: InterventionStep[] = [];
   70:   const add = (
   71:     type: InterventionStep["type"],
   72:     title: string,
   73:     description: string,
   74:     href: string,
   75:     sourceId = "",
   76:     sourceCollection: InterventionStep["sourceCollection"] = "",
   77:   ) => {
   78:     steps.push({
   79:       id: `${type}-${Date.now()}-${steps.length}`,
   80:       type,
   81:       title,
   82:       description,
   83:       href,
   84:       sourceId,
   85:       sourceCollection,
   86:       status: "not_started",
   87:       xpReward: xp,
   88:       completedAt: null,
   89:     });
   90:   };
   91:   if (input.pathway === "lesson" || input.pathway === "complete")
   92:     add(
   93:       "lesson",
   94:       `Review ${input.topic}`,
   95:       "Revisit the core knowledge and examples.",
   96:       input.lessonHref?.trim() ||
   97:         `/learn?search=${encodeURIComponent(input.topic)}`,
   98:     );
   99:   if (input.pathway === "quiz" || input.pathway === "complete")
  100:     add(
  101:       "quiz",
  102:       `Retrieval quiz: ${input.topic}`,
  103:       "Complete retrieval practice and correct misconceptions.",
  104:       input.quizAssignmentId?.trim()
  105:         ? `/assignments/quiz/${input.quizAssignmentId.trim()}`
  106:         : `/quiz?topic=${encodeURIComponent(input.topic)}`,
  107:       input.quizAssignmentId?.trim() || "",
  108:       input.quizAssignmentId?.trim() ? "assignments" : "",
  109:     );
  110:   if (input.pathway === "exam" || input.pathway === "complete")
  111:     add(
  112:       "exam",
  113:       `Exam practice: ${input.topic}`,
  114:       "Attempt an exam-style question and review the mark scheme.",
  115:       input.examAssignmentId?.trim()
  116:         ? `/assignments/exam/${input.examAssignmentId.trim()}`
  117:         : "/assignments?filter=exams",
  118:       input.examAssignmentId?.trim() || "",
  119:       input.examAssignmentId?.trim() ? "examAssignments" : "",
  120:     );
  121:   if (input.pathway === "complete")
  122:     add(
  123:       "review",
  124:       "Review progress",
  125:       "Reflect on feedback and agree the next target.",
  126:       "/revision-plan",
  127:     );
  128:   return steps;
  129: }
  130: 
  131: export async function createIntervention(
  132:   input: CreateInterventionInput,
  133: ): Promise<string> {
  134:   if (
  135:     !input.teacherId.trim() ||
  136:     !input.studentId.trim() ||
  137:     !input.title.trim() ||
  138:     !input.topic.trim()
  139:   )
  140:     throw new Error("Teacher, student, title and topic are required.");
  141:   if (!(input.dueDate instanceof Date) || Number.isNaN(input.dueDate.getTime()))
  142:     throw new Error("Select a valid due date.");
  143:   const existing = await getDocs(
  144:     query(
  145:       collection(db, "interventions"),
  146:       where("teacherId", "==", input.teacherId.trim()),
  147:       where("studentId", "==", input.studentId.trim()),
  148:     ),
  149:   );
  150:   if (
  151:     existing.docs.some(
  152:       (d) =>
  153:         d.data().status === "active" &&
  154:         String(d.data().topic || "").toLowerCase() ===
  155:           input.topic.trim().toLowerCase(),
  156:     )
  157:   )
  158:     throw new Error(
  159:       "An active intervention already exists for this student and topic.",
  160:     );
  161:   const baselineScore = clamp(input.baselineScore);
  162:   const ref = await addDoc(collection(db, "interventions"), {
  163:     teacherId: input.teacherId.trim(),
  164:     teacherName: input.teacherName?.trim() || "Teacher",
  165:     studentId: input.studentId.trim(),
  166:     studentName: input.studentName.trim(),
  167:     studentEmail: input.studentEmail?.trim() || "",
  168:     classId: input.classId?.trim() || "",
  169:     className: input.className?.trim() || "",
  170:     title: input.title.trim(),
  171:     topic: input.topic.trim(),
  172:     reason: input.reason.trim(),
  173:     priority: input.priority,
  174:     status: "active",
  175:     baselineScore,
  176:     currentScore: baselineScore,
  177:     impact: 0,
  178:     dueDate: Timestamp.fromDate(input.dueDate),
  179:     steps: buildSteps(input),
  180:     createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
  181:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  182:     completedAt: null,
  183:   });
  184:   return ref.id;
  185: }
  186: 
  187: export async function getInterventionById(
  188:   id: string,
  189: ): Promise<Intervention | null> {
  190:   const snap = await getDoc(doc(db, "interventions", id));
  191:   return snap.exists()
  192:     ? convert(snap.id, snap.data() as FirestoreIntervention)
  193:     : null;
  194: }
  195: 
  196: export async function getTeacherInterventions(
  197:   teacherId: string,
  198: ): Promise<Intervention[]> {
  199:   if (!teacherId.trim()) return [];
  200:   const snap = await getDocs(
  201:     query(
  202:       collection(db, "interventions"),
  203:       where("teacherId", "==", teacherId.trim()),
  204:       orderBy("createdAt", "desc"),
  205:     ),
  206:   );
  207:   return snap.docs.map((d) => convert(d.id, d.data() as FirestoreIntervention));
  208: }
  209: 
  210: async function linkedStatus(
  211:   intervention: Intervention,
  212:   step: InterventionStep,
  213: ): Promise<InterventionStepStatus> {
  214:   if (!step.sourceId || !step.sourceCollection) return step.status;
  215:   if (step.sourceCollection === "assignments") {
  216:     const snap = await getDoc(
  217:       doc(
  218:         db,
  219:         "assignmentResults",
  220:         `${step.sourceId}_${intervention.studentId}`,
  221:       ),
  222:     );
  223:     return snap.exists() && snap.data().status === "completed"
  224:       ? "completed"
  225:       : step.status;
  226:   }
  227:   if (step.sourceCollection === "examAssignments") {
  228:     const snap = await getDoc(
  229:       doc(db, "examSubmissions", `${step.sourceId}_${intervention.studentId}`),
  230:     );
  231:     if (!snap.exists()) return step.status;
  232:     const status = snap.data().status;
  233:     if (status === "marked") return "completed";
  234:     if (["in_progress", "submitted", "marking"].includes(status))
  235:       return "in_progress";
  236:   }
  237:   return step.status;
  238: }
  239: 
  240: export async function getStudentInterventions(
  241:   studentId: string,
  242: ): Promise<Intervention[]> {
  243:   if (!studentId.trim()) return [];
  244:   const snap = await getDocs(
  245:     query(
  246:       collection(db, "interventions"),
  247:       where("studentId", "==", studentId.trim()),
  248:       orderBy("createdAt", "desc"),
  249:     ),
  250:   );
  251:   const items = snap.docs.map((d) =>
  252:     convert(d.id, d.data() as FirestoreIntervention),
  253:   );
  254:   return Promise.all(
  255:     items.map(async (item) => ({
  256:       ...item,
  257:       steps: await Promise.all(
  258:         item.steps.map(async (step) => ({
  259:           ...step,
  260:           status: await linkedStatus(item, step),
  261:         })),
  262:       ),
  263:     })),
  264:   );
  265: }
  266: 
  267: export async function updateInterventionStep(
  268:   interventionId: string,
  269:   studentId: string,
  270:   stepId: string,
  271:   status: InterventionStepStatus,
  272: ): Promise<void> {
  273:   const item = await getInterventionById(interventionId);
  274:   if (!item || item.studentId !== studentId)
  275:     throw new Error("Intervention not found for this student.");
  276:   const steps = item.steps.map((step) =>
  277:     step.id === stepId
  278:       ? {
  279:           ...step,
  280:           status,
  281:           completedAt: status === "completed" ? new Date() : null,
  282:         }
  283:       : step,
  284:   );
  285:   const allCompleted = steps.every((step) => step.status === "completed");
  286:   await updateDoc(doc(db, "interventions", interventionId), {
  287:     steps: steps.map((step) => ({
  288:       ...step,
  289:       completedAt: step.completedAt
  290:         ? Timestamp.fromDate(step.completedAt)
  291:         : null,
  292:     })),
  293:     status: allCompleted ? "completed" : "active",
  294:     completedAt: allCompleted ? serverTimestamp() : null,
  295:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  296:   });
  297: }
  298: 
  299: export async function updateInterventionStatus(
  300:   id: string,
  301:   status: InterventionStatus,
  302: ): Promise<void> {
  303:   await updateDoc(doc(db, "interventions", id), {
  304:     status,
  305:     completedAt: status === "completed" ? serverTimestamp() : null,
  306:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  307:   });
  308: }
  309: 
  310: export async function updateInterventionImpact(
  311:   id: string,
  312:   currentScore: number,
  313: ): Promise<void> {
  314:   const item = await getInterventionById(id);
  315:   if (!item) throw new Error("Intervention not found.");
  316:   const score = clamp(currentScore);
  317:   await updateDoc(doc(db, "interventions", id), {
  318:     currentScore: score,
  319:     impact: score - item.baselineScore,
  320:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  321:   });
  322: }

==============================================================================================================
FILE: services\interventionAnalyticsService.ts
==============================================================================================================

    1: import { collection, getDocs, query, where } from "firebase/firestore";
    2: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
    3: import { getStudentAnalytics } from "@/services/studentAnalyticsService";
    4: import {
    5:   getAllStudents,
    6:   type StudentDirectoryRecord,
    7: } from "@/services/studentProfileService";
    8: 
    9: export type InterventionCandidate = {
   10:   student: StudentDirectoryRecord;
   11:   classId: string;
   12:   className: string;
   13:   combinedAverage: number;
   14:   predictedGrade: string;
   15:   improvementTrend: number;
   16:   overdueAssignments: number;
   17:   awaitingMarking: number;
   18:   priorityTopic: string;
   19:   priorityTopicScore: number;
   20:   recommendation: string;
   21:   priority: "high" | "medium" | "low";
   22: };
   23: 
   24: export async function getInterventionCandidates(
   25:   teacherId: string,
   26: ): Promise<InterventionCandidate[]> {
   27:   if (!teacherId.trim()) return [];
   28:   const classSnap = await getDocs(
   29:     query(
   30:       collection(db, "classes"),
   31:       where("teacherId", "==", teacherId.trim()),
   32:     ),
   33:   );
   34:   const classNames = new Map(
   35:     classSnap.docs.map((d) => [
   36:       d.id,
   37:       typeof d.data().name === "string" ? d.data().name : "Class",
   38:     ]),
   39:   );
   40:   const classIds = new Set(classNames.keys());
   41:   const students = (await getAllStudents()).filter((student) =>
   42:     student.classIds.some((id) => classIds.has(id)),
   43:   );
   44:   const candidates = await Promise.all(
   45:     students.map(async (student) => {
   46:       const analytics = await getStudentAnalytics(student.uid, teacherId);
   47:       if (!analytics) return null;
   48:       const weak = analytics.weakestTopics[0];
   49:       const classId = student.classIds.find((id) => classIds.has(id)) || "";
   50:       const avg = analytics.metrics.combinedAssessmentAverage;
   51:       const overdue = analytics.metrics.overdueAssignments;
   52:       const trend = analytics.metrics.improvementTrend;
   53:       return {
   54:         student,
   55:         classId,
   56:         className: classNames.get(classId) || "",
   57:         combinedAverage: avg,
   58:         predictedGrade: analytics.metrics.predictedGrade,
   59:         improvementTrend: trend,
   60:         overdueAssignments: overdue,
   61:         awaitingMarking: analytics.metrics.awaitingMarkingExamAssignments,
   62:         priorityTopic: weak?.topic || "General revision",
   63:         priorityTopicScore: weak?.averageScore || avg,
   64:         recommendation:
   65:           analytics.recommendations[0]?.description ||
   66:           "Continue targeted revision and assessment practice.",
   67:         priority:
   68:           avg < 40 || overdue > 0
   69:             ? "high"
   70:             : avg < 60 || trend < 0
   71:               ? "medium"
   72:               : "low",
   73:       } satisfies InterventionCandidate;
   74:     }),
   75:   );
   76:   const weight = { high: 0, medium: 1, low: 2 };
   77:   return candidates
   78:     .filter((item): item is InterventionCandidate => item !== null)
   79:     .sort(
   80:       (a, b) =>
   81:         weight[a.priority] - weight[b.priority] ||
   82:         a.combinedAverage - b.combinedAverage,
   83:     );
   84: }

==============================================================================================================
FILE: services\analytics\interventionEffectivenessService.ts

    1: import { buildInterventionReviewCycle } from "@/services/analytics/interventionReviewCycleService";
    2: import type { InterventionImpact } from "@/types/interventionImpact";
    3: import type {
    4:   InterventionEffectivenessItem,
    5:   InterventionEffectivenessStatus,
    6:   InterventionEffectivenessSummary,
    7: } from "@/types/interventionEffectiveness";
    8: 
    9: export type InterventionEffectivenessSource = {
   10:   id: string;
   11:   studentName: string;
   12:   topic: string;
   13:   status: string;
   14: };
   15: 
   16: function effectivenessStatus(
   17:   impact: InterventionImpact,
   18: ): InterventionEffectivenessStatus {
   19:   if (!impact.baseline) {
   20:     return "baseline_needed";
   21:   }
   22: 
   23:   if (impact.impactStatus === "awaiting_new_evidence") {
   24:     return "awaiting_evidence";
   25:   }
   26: 
   27:   if (impact.impactStatus === "improving") {
   28:     return "improving";
   29:   }
   30: 
   31:   if (impact.impactStatus === "declining") {
   32:     return "declining";
   33:   }
   34: 
   35:   return "limited_change";
   36: }
   37: 
   38: function headlineForStatus(
   39:   status: InterventionEffectivenessStatus,
   40: ): string {
   41:   switch (status) {
   42:     case "improving":
   43:       return "Improving";
   44:     case "declining":
   45:       return "Declining";
   46:     case "awaiting_evidence":
   47:       return "Awaiting reassessment";
   48:     case "baseline_needed":
   49:       return "Baseline required";
   50:     default:
   51:       return "Limited / mixed change";
   52:   }
   53: }
   54: 
   55: function statusRank(
   56:   status: InterventionEffectivenessStatus,
   57: ): number {
   58:   switch (status) {
   59:     case "declining":
   60:       return 0;
   61:     case "baseline_needed":
   62:       return 1;
   63:     case "awaiting_evidence":
   64:       return 2;
   65:     case "limited_change":
   66:       return 3;
   67:     case "improving":
   68:       return 4;
   69:   }
   70: }
   71: 
   72: export function buildInterventionEffectivenessItem({
   73:   source,
   74:   impact,
   75: }: {
   76:   source: InterventionEffectivenessSource;
   77:   impact: InterventionImpact;
   78: }): InterventionEffectivenessItem {
   79:   const status = effectivenessStatus(impact);
   80:   const review = buildInterventionReviewCycle(impact);
   81: 
   82:   return {
   83:     interventionId: source.id,
   84:     studentId: impact.studentId,
   85:     studentName: source.studentName,
   86:     topic: impact.topic || source.topic,
   87:     interventionStatus: source.status,
   88:     effectivenessStatus: status,
   89:     headline: headlineForStatus(status),
   90:     summary: impact.summary,
   91:     evidenceChange: impact.evidenceChange,
   92:     attainmentChange: impact.attainmentChange,
   93:     topicMasteryChange: impact.topicMasteryChange,
   94:     completionChange: impact.completionChange,
   95:     workingGradeChange: impact.workingGradeChange,
   96:     recommendedAction: review.explanation,
   97:     reviewHref: `/teacher/interventions/${encodeURIComponent(source.id)}`,
   98:   };
   99: }
  100: 
  101: export function buildInterventionEffectivenessSummary(
  102:   items: InterventionEffectivenessItem[],
  103: ): InterventionEffectivenessSummary {
  104:   const improving = items.filter(
  105:     (item) => item.effectivenessStatus === "improving",
  106:   ).length;
  107: 
  108:   const limitedChange = items.filter(
  109:     (item) => item.effectivenessStatus === "limited_change",
  110:   ).length;
  111: 
  112:   const declining = items.filter(
  113:     (item) => item.effectivenessStatus === "declining",
  114:   ).length;
  115: 
  116:   const awaitingEvidence = items.filter(
  117:     (item) => item.effectivenessStatus === "awaiting_evidence",
  118:   ).length;
  119: 
  120:   const baselineNeeded = items.filter(
  121:     (item) => item.effectivenessStatus === "baseline_needed",
  122:   ).length;
  123: 
  124:   const reviewReady = improving + limitedChange + declining;
  125: 
  126:   const successfulRate =
  127:     reviewReady > 0
  128:       ? Math.round((improving / reviewReady) * 100)
  129:       : null;
  130: 
  131:   return {
  132:     total: items.length,
  133:     improving,
  134:     limitedChange,
  135:     declining,
  136:     awaitingEvidence,
  137:     baselineNeeded,
  138:     reviewReady,
  139:     successfulRate,
  140:     items: [...items].sort((first, second) => {
  141:       const statusDifference =
  142:         statusRank(first.effectivenessStatus) -
  143:         statusRank(second.effectivenessStatus);
  144: 
  145:       if (statusDifference !== 0) {
  146:         return statusDifference;
  147:       }
  148: 
  149:       return first.studentName.localeCompare(second.studentName);
  150:     }),
  151:   };
  152: }

FILE: services\analytics\interventionImpactService.ts

    1: import { doc, getDoc, serverTimestamp, updateDoc } from "firebase/firestore";
    2: 
    3: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
    4: import { getTeacherStudentAnalytics } from "@/services/analytics/teacherAnalyticsService";

    7:   InterventionAnalyticsBaseline,
