   11: 
   12: function gradeValue(value: string | null): number | null {
   13:   if (!value) return null;
   14: 
   15:   const cleaned = value.trim().toUpperCase();
   16:   const numeric = Number(cleaned);
   17: 
   18:   if (Number.isFinite(numeric)) {
   19:     return numeric;
   20:   }
   21: 
   22:   const aLevelOrder: Record<string, number> = {
   23:     E: 1,
   24:     D: 2,
   25:     C: 3,
   26:     B: 4,
   27:     A: 5,
   28:     "A*": 6,
   29:   };
   30: 
   31:   return aLevelOrder[cleaned] ?? null;
   32: }
   33: 
   34: function topicMastery(
   35:   row: TeacherStudentAnalyticsRow,
   36:   topic: string,
   37: ): number | null {
   38:   const normalised = topic.trim().toLowerCase();
   39: 
   40:   const exactMatch = row.analytics.topics.find(
   41:     (item) => item.topic.trim().toLowerCase() === normalised,
   42:   );
   43: 
   44:   if (exactMatch) {
   45:     return exactMatch.weightedPercentage;
   46:   }
   47: 
   48:   const partialMatch = row.analytics.topics.find((item) => {
   49:     const candidate = item.topic.trim().toLowerCase();
   50: 
   51:     return (
   52:       candidate.includes(normalised) ||
   53:       normalised.includes(candidate)
   54:     );
   55:   });
   56: 
   57:   return partialMatch?.weightedPercentage ?? null;
   58: }
   59: 
   60: function evidenceCount(row: TeacherStudentAnalyticsRow): number {
   61:   return row.analytics.evidence.filter((item) => item.graded).length;
   62: }
   63: 
   64: function createBaseline(
   65:   row: TeacherStudentAnalyticsRow,
   66:   topic: string,
   67: ): InterventionAnalyticsBaseline {
   68:   return {
   69:     capturedAt: new Date(),
   70:     workingGrade: row.workingGrade,
   71:     targetGrade: row.targetGrade,
   72:     gradeGap: row.gradeGap,
   73:     weightedPercentage: row.workingPercentage ?? 0,
   74:     trend: row.trend,
   75:     completionRate: row.completionRate,
   76:     evidenceCount: evidenceCount(row),
   77:     topic,
   78:     topicMastery: topicMastery(row, topic),
   79:   };
   80: }
   81: 
   82: function convertStoredBaseline(
   83:   value: unknown,
   84: ): InterventionAnalyticsBaseline | null {
   85:   if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return null;
   86: 
   87:   const data = value as Record<string, unknown>;
   88:   let capturedAt: Date | null = null;
   89:   const raw = data.capturedAt;
   90: 
   91:   if (
   92:     raw &&
   93:     typeof raw === "object" &&
   94:     "toDate" in raw &&
   95:     typeof (raw as { toDate?: unknown }).toDate === "function"
   96:   ) {
   97:     capturedAt = (raw as { toDate: () => Date }).toDate();
   98:   } else if (raw instanceof Date) {
   99:     capturedAt = raw;
  100:   }
  101: 
  102:   return {
  103:     capturedAt,
  104:     workingGrade:
  105:       typeof data.workingGrade === "string" ? data.workingGrade : null,
  106:     targetGrade:
  107:       typeof data.targetGrade === "string" ? data.targetGrade : null,
  108:     gradeGap:
  109:       typeof data.gradeGap === "number" ? data.gradeGap : null,
  110:     weightedPercentage:
  111:       typeof data.weightedPercentage === "number"
  112:         ? data.weightedPercentage
  113:         : 0,
  114:     trend:
  115:       typeof data.trend === "string" ? data.trend : "insufficient",
  116:     completionRate:
  117:       typeof data.completionRate === "number" ? data.completionRate : 0,
  118:     evidenceCount:
  119:       typeof data.evidenceCount === "number" ? data.evidenceCount : 0,
  120:     topic:
  121:       typeof data.topic === "string"
  122:         ? data.topic
  123:         : "General Computer Science",
  124:     topicMastery:
  125:       typeof data.topicMastery === "number" ? data.topicMastery : null,
  126:   };
  127: }
  128: 
  129: function determineImpactStatus(
  130:   baseline: InterventionAnalyticsBaseline | null,
  131:   current: InterventionAnalyticsBaseline,
  132: ): InterventionImpactStatus {
  133:   if (!baseline) return "baseline_missing";
  134: 
  135:   if (current.evidenceCount <= baseline.evidenceCount) {
  136:     return "awaiting_new_evidence";
  137:   }
  138: 
  139:   const attainment =
  140:     current.weightedPercentage - baseline.weightedPercentage;
  141:   const completion =
  142:     current.completionRate - baseline.completionRate;
  143:   const topic =
  144:     baseline.topicMastery !== null && current.topicMastery !== null
  145:       ? current.topicMastery - baseline.topicMastery
  146:       : null;
  147: 
  148:   const baselineGrade = gradeValue(baseline.workingGrade);
  149:   const currentGrade = gradeValue(current.workingGrade);
  150:   const grade =
  151:     baselineGrade !== null && currentGrade !== null
  152:       ? currentGrade - baselineGrade
  153:       : null;
  154: 
  155:   const positive = [
  156:     attainment >= 5,
  157:     completion >= 5,
  158:     topic !== null && topic >= 5,
  159:     grade !== null && grade > 0,
  160:   ].filter(Boolean).length;
  161: 
  162:   const negative = [
  163:     attainment <= -5,
  164:     completion <= -5,
  165:     topic !== null && topic <= -5,
  166:     grade !== null && grade < 0,
  167:   ].filter(Boolean).length;
  168: 
  169:   if (positive >= 2 && negative === 0) return "improving";
  170:   if (negative >= 2 && positive === 0) return "declining";
  171:   if (positive > 0 && negative > 0) return "mixed";
  172: 
  173:   return "no_change";
  174: }
  175: 
  176: function summary(status: InterventionImpactStatus): string {
  177:   switch (status) {
  178:     case "baseline_missing":
  179:       return "No reliable before-intervention baseline is available for this older intervention.";
  180:     case "awaiting_new_evidence":
  181:       return "The baseline is captured, but no new graded evidence is available yet.";
  182:     case "improving":
  183:       return "Post-intervention evidence shows improvement across multiple indicators.";
  184:     case "declining":
  185:       return "Post-intervention evidence has weakened across multiple indicators.";
  186:     case "mixed":
  187:       return "Some indicators improved while others weakened.";
  188:     default:
  189:       return "New evidence exists, but there is no material overall change yet.";
  190:   }
  191: }
  192: 
  193: function nextAction(status: InterventionImpactStatus): string {
  194:   switch (status) {
  195:     case "baseline_missing":
  196:       return "Capture a baseline now for future comparison.";
  197:     case "awaiting_new_evidence":
  198:       return "Collect another graded task before reviewing impact.";
  199:     case "improving":
  200:       return "Continue the strategy and reassess after the next meaningful task.";
  201:     case "declining":
  202:       return "Redesign or escalate the support and use a more focused reassessment.";
  203:     case "mixed":
  204:       return "Keep the effective elements and retarget the areas that have not improved.";
  205:     default:
  206:       return "Maintain support and review after the next assessment.";
  207:   }
  208: }
  209: 
  210: export async function captureInterventionBaseline({
  211:   interventionId,
  212:   teacherId,
  213:   studentId,
  214:   topic,
  215: }: {
  216:   interventionId: string;
  217:   teacherId: string;
  218:   studentId: string;
  219:   topic: string;
  220: }): Promise<void> {
  221:   const cleanedInterventionId = interventionId.trim();
  222:   const cleanedTeacherId = teacherId.trim();
  223:   const cleanedStudentId = studentId.trim();
  224:   const cleanedTopic = topic.trim() || "General Computer Science";
  225: 
  226:   if (!cleanedInterventionId) {
  227:     throw new Error("A valid intervention is required.");
  228:   }
  229: 
  230:   if (!cleanedTeacherId) {
  231:     throw new Error("A valid teacher account is required.");
  232:   }
  233: 
  234:   if (!cleanedStudentId) {
  235:     throw new Error("A valid student account is required.");
  236:   }
  237: 
  238:   const interventionReference = doc(
  239:     db,
  240:     "interventions",
  241:     cleanedInterventionId,
  242:   );
  243: 
  244:   const interventionSnapshot = await getDoc(interventionReference);
  245: 
  246:   if (!interventionSnapshot.exists()) {
  247:     throw new Error("The intervention could not be found.");
  248:   }
  249: 
  250:   const interventionData = interventionSnapshot.data();
  251: 
  252:   if (
  253:     typeof interventionData.teacherId !== "string" ||
  254:     interventionData.teacherId !== cleanedTeacherId
  255:   ) {
  256:     throw new Error(
  257:       "This intervention does not belong to the signed-in teacher.",
  258:     );
  259:   }
  260: 
  261:   if (
  262:     typeof interventionData.studentId !== "string" ||
  263:     interventionData.studentId !== cleanedStudentId
  264:   ) {
  265:     throw new Error(
  266:       "The intervention does not belong to the selected student.",
  267:     );
  268:   }
  269: 
  270:   const row = await getTeacherStudentAnalytics({
  271:     teacherId: cleanedTeacherId,
  272:     studentId: cleanedStudentId,
  273:   });
  274: 
  275:   if (!row) {
  276:     throw new Error("Student analytics could not be loaded.");
  277:   }
  278: 
  279:   const baseline = createBaseline(row, cleanedTopic);
  280: 
  281:   await updateDoc(interventionReference, {
  282:     analyticsBaseline: {
  283:       ...baseline,
  284:       capturedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  285:     },
  286:     analyticsBaselineVersion: 1,
  287:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  288:   });
  289: }
  290: 
  291: export async function getInterventionImpact({
  292:   interventionId,
  293:   teacherId,
  294: }: {
  295:   interventionId: string;
  296:   teacherId: string;
  297: }): Promise<InterventionImpact | null> {
  298:   const cleanedInterventionId = interventionId.trim();
  299:   const cleanedTeacherId = teacherId.trim();
  300: 
  301:   if (!cleanedInterventionId || !cleanedTeacherId) {
  302:     return null;
  303:   }
  304: 
  305:   const snapshot = await getDoc(
  306:     doc(db, "interventions", cleanedInterventionId),
  307:   );
  308: 
  309:   if (!snapshot.exists()) return null;
  310: 
  311:   const data = snapshot.data();
  312: 
  313:   if (
  314:     typeof data.teacherId !== "string" ||
  315:     data.teacherId !== cleanedTeacherId
  316:   ) {
  317:     throw new Error(
  318:       "This intervention does not belong to the signed-in teacher.",
  319:     );
  320:   }
  321: 
  322:   const studentId =
  323:     typeof data.studentId === "string" ? data.studentId.trim() : "";
  324: 
  325:   if (!studentId) return null;
  326: 
  327:   const topic =
  328:     (typeof data.topic === "string" && data.topic.trim()) ||
  329:     (typeof data.priorityTopic === "string" && data.priorityTopic.trim()) ||
  330:     "General Computer Science";
  331: 
  332:   const row = await getTeacherStudentAnalytics({
  333:     teacherId: cleanedTeacherId,
  334:     studentId,
  335:   });
  336: 
  337:   if (!row) return null;
  338: 
  339:   const baseline = convertStoredBaseline(data.analyticsBaseline);
  340:   const current = createBaseline(row, topic);
  341:   const impactStatus = determineImpactStatus(baseline, current);
  342: 
  343:   const baselineGrade = gradeValue(baseline?.workingGrade ?? null);
  344:   const currentGrade = gradeValue(current.workingGrade);
  345: 
  346:   const createdAt =
  347:     data.createdAt &&
  348:     typeof data.createdAt === "object" &&
  349:     "toDate" in data.createdAt &&
  350:     typeof (data.createdAt as { toDate?: unknown }).toDate === "function"
  351:       ? (data.createdAt as { toDate: () => Date }).toDate()
  352:       : null;
  353: 
  354:   return {
  355:     interventionId: snapshot.id,
  356:     studentId,
  357:     title:
  358:       (typeof data.title === "string" && data.title.trim()) ||
  359:       (typeof data.recommendation === "string" && data.recommendation.trim()) ||
  360:       "Student intervention",
  361:     topic,
  362:     status:
  363:       typeof data.status === "string" ? data.status : "active",
  364:     createdAt,
  365:     baseline,
  366:     current,
  367:     impactStatus,
  368:     workingGradeChange:
  369:       baselineGrade !== null && currentGrade !== null
  370:         ? currentGrade - baselineGrade
  371:         : null,
  372:     gradeGapChange:
  373:       baseline?.gradeGap !== null &&
  374:       baseline?.gradeGap !== undefined &&
  375:       current.gradeGap !== null
  376:         ? current.gradeGap - baseline.gradeGap
  377:         : null,
  378:     attainmentChange:
  379:       baseline
  380:         ? current.weightedPercentage - baseline.weightedPercentage
  381:         : 0,
  382:     completionChange:
  383:       baseline
  384:         ? current.completionRate - baseline.completionRate
  385:         : 0,
  386:     topicMasteryChange:
  387:       baseline?.topicMastery !== null &&
  388:       baseline?.topicMastery !== undefined &&
  389:       current.topicMastery !== null
  390:         ? current.topicMastery - baseline.topicMastery
  391:         : null,
  392:     evidenceChange:
  393:       baseline
  394:         ? current.evidenceCount - baseline.evidenceCount
  395:         : 0,
  396:     summary: summary(impactStatus),
  397:     nextAction: nextAction(impactStatus),
  398:   };
  399: }

==============================================================================================================
FILE: services\analytics\interventionReviewCycleService.ts
==============================================================================================================

    1: import {
    2:   doc,
    3:   serverTimestamp,
    4:   updateDoc,
    5: } from "firebase/firestore";
    6: 
    7: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
    8: import type { InterventionImpact } from "@/types/interventionImpact";
    9: import type {
   10:   InterventionReviewCycle,
   11:   InterventionReviewDecision,
   12: } from "@/types/interventionReviewCycle";
   13: 
   14: function changeText(
   15:   label: string,
   16:   value: number | null,
   17:   suffix = "",
   18: ): string | null {
   19:   if (value === null || value === 0) {
   20:     return null;
   21:   }
   22: 
   23:   const direction =
   24:     value > 0 ? "increased" : "decreased";
   25: 
   26:   return `${label} ${direction} by ${Math.abs(value)}${suffix}.`;
   27: }
   28: 
   29: export function buildInterventionReviewCycle(
   30:   impact: InterventionImpact,
   31: ): InterventionReviewCycle {
   32:   const allChanges = [
   33:     changeText("Attainment", impact.attainmentChange, "%"),
   34:     changeText("Completion", impact.completionChange, "%"),
   35:     changeText("Topic mastery", impact.topicMasteryChange, "%"),
   36:     changeText("Working grade", impact.workingGradeChange),
   37:   ].filter((value): value is string => Boolean(value));
   38: 
   39:   const successSignals = allChanges.filter(
   40:     (value) => value.includes("increased"),
   41:   );
   42: 
   43:   const concernSignals = allChanges.filter(
   44:     (value) => value.includes("decreased"),
   45:   );
   46: 
   47:   if (!impact.baseline) {
   48:     return {
   49:       phase: "baseline_needed",
   50:       headline: "Capture a baseline before judging impact",
   51:       explanation:
   52:         "This intervention does not yet have a reliable before-intervention comparison point.",
   53:       evidenceReady: false,
   54:       reassessmentRecommended: false,
   55:       canCloseSuccessful: false,
   56:       recommendedDecision: "continue",
   57:       successSignals,
   58:       concernSignals,
   59:     };
   60:   }
   61: 
   62:   if (impact.impactStatus === "awaiting_new_evidence") {
   63:     return {
   64:       phase: "awaiting_evidence",
   65:       headline: "The intervention needs a reassessment",
   66:       explanation:
   67:         "The baseline exists, but there is no new graded evidence yet. Assign a focused reassessment before deciding whether the intervention worked.",
   68:       evidenceReady: false,
   69:       reassessmentRecommended: true,
   70:       canCloseSuccessful: false,
   71:       recommendedDecision: "continue",
   72:       successSignals,
   73:       concernSignals,
   74:     };
   75:   }
   76: 
   77:   if (impact.impactStatus === "improving") {
   78:     return {
   79:       phase: "successful",
   80:       headline: "Evidence indicates that the intervention is working",
   81:       explanation:
   82:         "Multiple post-intervention indicators have improved. The teacher can continue briefly for consolidation or close the intervention as successful.",
   83:       evidenceReady: true,
   84:       reassessmentRecommended: false,
   85:       canCloseSuccessful: true,
   86:       recommendedDecision: "close_successful",
   87:       successSignals,
   88:       concernSignals,
   89:     };
   90:   }
   91: 
   92:   if (impact.impactStatus === "declining") {
   93:     return {
   94:       phase: "escalation_recommended",
   95:       headline: "The current support is not producing the required improvement",
   96:       explanation:
   97:         "Multiple indicators have weakened since the baseline. Review the misconception, change the support strategy and use a focused reassessment.",
   98:       evidenceReady: true,
   99:       reassessmentRecommended: true,
  100:       canCloseSuccessful: false,
  101:       recommendedDecision: "escalate",
  102:       successSignals,
  103:       concernSignals,
  104:     };
  105:   }
  106: 
  107:   if (impact.impactStatus === "mixed") {
  108:     return {
  109:       phase: "needs_retargeting",
  110:       headline: "Some evidence improved, but the intervention needs retargeting",
  111:       explanation:
  112:         "The response is mixed. Keep the successful elements, retarget the weak area and collect another meaningful assessment.",
  113:       evidenceReady: true,
  114:       reassessmentRecommended: true,
  115:       canCloseSuccessful: false,
  116:       recommendedDecision: "retarget",
  117:       successSignals,
  118:       concernSignals,
  119:     };
  120:   }
  121: 
  122:   return {
  123:     phase: "ready_to_review",
  124:     headline: "New evidence is available for teacher review",
  125:     explanation:
  126:       "There is post-intervention evidence, but the overall change is not yet large enough to classify as improving or declining.",
  127:     evidenceReady: true,
  128:     reassessmentRecommended: true,
  129:     canCloseSuccessful: false,
  130:     recommendedDecision: "continue",
  131:     successSignals,
  132:     concernSignals,
  133:   };
  134: }
  135: 
  136: export function buildInterventionReassessmentHref({
  137:   impact,
  138: }: {
  139:   impact: InterventionImpact;
  140: }): string {
  141:   return (
  142:     `/teacher/assignment-wizard?source=intervention-review` +
  143:     `&studentId=${encodeURIComponent(impact.studentId)}` +
  144:     `&topic=${encodeURIComponent(impact.topic)}` +
  145:     `&interventionId=${encodeURIComponent(impact.interventionId)}`
  146:   );
  147: }
  148: 
  149: export async function saveInterventionReviewDecision({
  150:   interventionId,
  151:   teacherId,
  152:   decision,
  153:   note,
  154: }: {
  155:   interventionId: string;
  156:   teacherId: string;
  157:   decision: InterventionReviewDecision;
  158:   note: string;
  159: }): Promise<void> {
  160:   const reference = doc(
  161:     db,
  162:     "interventions",
  163:     interventionId,
  164:   );
  165: 
  166:   const update: Record<string, unknown> = {
  167:     reviewDecision: decision,
  168:     reviewNote: note.trim(),
  169:     reviewedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  170:     reviewedBy: teacherId.trim(),
  171:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  172:   };
  173: 
  174:   if (decision === "close_successful") {
  175:     update.status = "completed";
  176:     update.completedAt = serverTimestamp();
  177:   }
  178: 
  179:   if (decision === "escalate") {
  180:     update.status = "active";
  181:     update.escalatedAt = serverTimestamp();
  182:   }
  183: 
  184:   if (decision === "retarget") {
  185:     update.status = "active";
  186:     update.retargetedAt = serverTimestamp();
  187:   }
  188: 
  189:   await updateDoc(reference, update);
  190: }

   69:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   70:   const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState("");
   71:   const [kindFilter, setKindFilter] = useState<KindFilter>("all");
   72:   const [lifecycleFilter, setLifecycleFilter] = useState<LifecycleFilter>("all");
   73:   const [qualificationFilter, setQualificationFilter] = useState("all");
   74:   const [examBoardFilter, setExamBoardFilter] = useState("all");

   76: 
   77:   const load = useCallback(async () => {
   78:     await Promise.resolve();
   79: 
   80:     const teacherId = user?.uid;
   81:     if (!teacherId) {
   82:       setItems([]);
   83:       setLoading(false);
   84:       return;
   85:     }
   86: 
   87:     try {
   88:       setLoading(true);
   89:       setError("");
   90:       setItems(await getTeacherContentLibrary(teacherId));
   91:     } catch (caughtError) {
   92:       console.error("Unable to load teacher content library:", caughtError);
   93:       setError(
   94:         caughtError instanceof Error
   95:           ? caughtError.message
   96:           : "The Content Hub could not be loaded.",
   97:       );
   98:     } finally {
   99:       setLoading(false);
  100:     }
  101:   }, [user?.uid]);
  102: 
  103:   useEffect(() => {
  104:     if (authLoading || !profileReady) return;

  110:     [items],

  114:     [items],

  123:           item.title,

  137:         (qualificationFilter === "all" || item.qualification === qualificationFilter) &&
  138:         (examBoardFilter === "all" || item.examBoard === examBoardFilter)
  139:       );
  140:     });

  148:       archived: items.filter((item) => item.lifecycle === "archived").length,

  351:       <span className="sr-only">Content filter</span>

  354:     </label>
  355:   );

   56:         <div
   57:           key={index}
   58:           className="animate-pulse rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5"
   59:         >
   60:           <div className="h-5 w-24 rounded bg-slate-200" />
   61:           <div className="mt-5 h-7 w-3/4 rounded bg-slate-200" />
   62:           <div className="mt-3 h-4 w-full rounded bg-slate-100" />
   63:           <div className="mt-2 h-4 w-2/3 rounded bg-slate-100" />
   64: 
   65:           <div className="mt-6 flex gap-2">
   66:             <div className="h-7 w-20 rounded-full bg-slate-100" />
   67:             <div className="h-7 w-20 rounded-full bg-slate-100" />
   68:           </div>
   69: 
   70:           <div className="mt-6 h-10 rounded-xl bg-slate-100" />
   71:         </div>
   72:       ))}
   73:     </div>
   74:   );
   75: }
   76: 
   77: function EmptyLibrary({
   78:   hasFilters,
   79:   onClearFilters,
   80: }: {
   81:   hasFilters: boolean;
   82:   onClearFilters: () => void;
   83: }) {
   84:   return (
   85:     <div className="rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-white px-6 py-16 text-center">
   86:       <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-600">
   87:         <BookOpen className="h-8 w-8" />
   88:       </div>
   89: 
   90:       <h2 className="mt-5 text-xl font-bold text-slate-950">
   91:         {hasFilters
   92:           ? "No matching resources"
   93:           : "Your resource library is empty"}
   94:       </h2>
   95: 
   96:       <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-lg text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
   97:         {hasFilters
   98:           ? "Try changing your search term or clearing the selected filters."
   99:           : "Generate or load a teaching resource in the AI Teacher Assistant, then save it to your library."}
  100:       </p>
  101: 
  102:       <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col justify-center gap-3 sm:flex-row">
  103:         {hasFilters && (
  104:           <button
  105:             type="button"
  106:             onClick={onClearFilters}
  107:             className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-50"
  108:           >
  109:             <RefreshCcw className="h-4 w-4" />
  110:             Clear filters
  111:           </button>
  112:         )}
  113: 
  114:         <Link
  115:           href="/teacher/assistant"
  116:           className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-indigo-700"
  117:         >
  118:           <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4" />
  119:           Open AI Assistant
  120:         </Link>
  121:       </div>
  122:     </div>
  123:   );
  124: }
  125: 
  126: function ResourceCard({
  127:   resource,
  128:   deletingId,
  129:   onDelete,
  130: }: {
  131:   resource: SavedTeacherResource;
  132:   deletingId: string | null;
  133:   onDelete: (resource: SavedTeacherResource) => void;
  134: }) {
  135:   const isDeleting = deletingId === resource.id;
  136: 
  137:   return (
  138:     <article className="flex h-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:shadow-md">
  139:       <div className="h-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-sky-500" />
  140: 
  141:       <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col p-5">
  142:         <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
  143:           <span className="inline-flex rounded-full border border-indigo-100 bg-indigo-50 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-indigo-700">
  144:             {formatResourceType(resource.resourceType)}
  145:           </span>
  146: 
  147:           <span
  148:             className={
  149:               resource.status === "published"
  150:                 ? "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold capitalize text-emerald-700"
  151:                 : "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold capitalize text-amber-700"
  152:             }
  153:           >
  154:             {resource.status === "published" && (
  155:               <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
  156:             )}
  157: 
  158:             {resource.status}
  159:           </span>
  160:         </div>
  161: 
  162:         <div className="mt-5">
  163:           <h2 className="line-clamp-2 text-xl font-bold text-slate-950">
  164:             {resource.title}
  165:           </h2>
  166: 
  167:           <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
  168:             {resource.content.overview}
  169:           </p>
  170:         </div>
  171: 
  172:         <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
  173:           <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
  174:             {resource.yearGroup}
  175:           </span>
  176: 
  177:           <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
  178:             {resource.examBoard}
  179:           </span>
  180: 
  181:           <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold capitalize text-slate-600">
  182:             {resource.difficulty}
  183:           </span>
  184:         </div>
  185: 
  186:         <div className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 border-t border-slate-100 pt-5 text-sm text-slate-600">
  187:           <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
  188:             <Clock3 className="h-4 w-4 text-slate-400" />
  189:             {resource.duration}
  190:           </span>
  191: 
  192:           <span className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 text-right">
  193:             <FileText className="h-4 w-4 text-slate-400" />
  194:             {resource.content.sections.length} sections
  195:           </span>
  196:         </div>
  197: 
  198:         <p className="mt-4 text-xs text-slate-400">
  199:           Saved {formatDate(resource.createdAt)}
  200:         </p>
  201: 
  202:         <div className="mt-auto flex gap-3 pt-6">
  203:           <Link
  204:             href={`/teacher/resources/${resource.id}`}
  205:             className="inline-flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-indigo-700"
  206:           >
  207:             <BookOpen className="h-4 w-4" />
  208:             Open resource
  209:           </Link>
  210: 
  211:           <button
  212:             type="button"
  213:             onClick={() => onDelete(resource)}
  214:             disabled={isDeleting || resource.status !== "archived"}
  215:             title={resource.status === "archived" ? "Delete archived resource" : "Archive this resource in Content Hub before deleting it"}
  216:             aria-label={`Delete ${resource.title}`}
  217:             className="inline-flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-600 transition hover:bg-red-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
  218:           >
  219:             {isDeleting ? (
  220:               <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
  221:             ) : (
  222:               <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
  223:             )}
  224:           </button>
  225:         </div>
  226:       </div>
  227:     </article>
  228:   );
  229: }
  230: 
  231: export default function TeacherResourcesPage() {
  232:   const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  233: 
  234:   const [authLoading, setAuthLoading] = useState(true);
  235: 
  236:   const [resources, setResources] = useState<SavedTeacherResource[]>([]);
  237: 
  238:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  239: 
  240:   const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  241: 
  242:   const [success, setSuccess] = useState<string | null>(null);
  243: 
  244:   const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState("");
  245: 
  246:   const [resourceTypeFilter, setResourceTypeFilter] = useState("all");
  247: 
  248:   const [yearGroupFilter, setYearGroupFilter] = useState("all");
  249: 
  250:   const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<StatusFilter>("all");
  251: 
  252:   const [deletingId, setDeletingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  253: 
  254:   useEffect(() => {
  255:     const unsubscribe = onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
  256:       setCurrentUser(user);
  257:       setAuthLoading(false);
  258:     });
  259: 
  260:     return unsubscribe;
  261:   }, []);
  262: 
  263:   const loadResources = useCallback(() => {
  264:     if (!currentUser) {
  265:       return Promise.resolve().then(() => {
  266:         setResources([]);
  267:         setError(null);
  268:         setLoading(false);
  269:       });
  270:     }
  271: 
  272:     const teacherId = currentUser.uid;
  273: 
  274:     return Promise.resolve()
  275:       .then(() => {
  276:         setLoading(true);
  277:         setError(null);
  278:         return getTeacherResources(teacherId);
  279:       })
  280:       .then((savedResources) => {
  281:         setResources(savedResources);
  282:       })
  283:       .catch((caughtError) => {
  284:         console.error("Failed to load teacher resources:", caughtError);
  285: 
  286:         setError(
  287:           caughtError instanceof Error
  288:             ? caughtError.message
  289:             : "Your resource library could not be loaded.",
  290:         );
  291:       })
  292:       .finally(() => {
  293:         setLoading(false);
  294:       });
  295:   }, [currentUser]);
  296: 
  297:   useEffect(() => {
  298:     if (!authLoading) {
  299:       void loadResources();
  300:     }
  301:   }, [authLoading, loadResources]);
  302: 
  303:   const resourceTypes = useMemo(() => {
  304:     return Array.from(
  305:       new Set(resources.map((resource) => resource.resourceType)),
  306:     ).sort();
  307:   }, [resources]);
  308: 
  309:   const yearGroups = useMemo(() => {
  310:     return Array.from(
  311:       new Set(resources.map((resource) => resource.yearGroup)),
  312:     ).sort();
  313:   }, [resources]);
  314: 
  315:   const filteredResources = useMemo(() => {
  316:     const normalisedSearch = searchTerm.trim().toLowerCase();
  317: 
  318:     return resources.filter((resource) => {
  319:       const matchesSearch =
  320:         !normalisedSearch ||
  321:         resource.title.toLowerCase().includes(normalisedSearch) ||
  322:         resource.topic.toLowerCase().includes(normalisedSearch) ||
  323:         resource.content.overview.toLowerCase().includes(normalisedSearch);
  324: 
  325:       const matchesType =
  326:         resourceTypeFilter === "all" ||
  327:         resource.resourceType === resourceTypeFilter;
  328: 
  329:       const matchesYearGroup =
  330:         yearGroupFilter === "all" || resource.yearGroup === yearGroupFilter;
  331: 
  332:       const matchesStatus =
  333:         statusFilter === "all" || resource.status === statusFilter;
  334: 
  335:       return matchesSearch && matchesType && matchesYearGroup && matchesStatus;
  336:     });
  337:   }, [
  338:     resources,
  339:     searchTerm,
  340:     resourceTypeFilter,
  341:     yearGroupFilter,
  342:     statusFilter,
  343:   ]);
  344: 
  345:   const hasFilters =
  346:     searchTerm.trim().length > 0 ||
  347:     resourceTypeFilter !== "all" ||
  348:     yearGroupFilter !== "all" ||
  349:     statusFilter !== "all";
  350: 
  351:   function clearFilters() {
  352:     setSearchTerm("");
  353:     setResourceTypeFilter("all");
  354:     setYearGroupFilter("all");
  355:     setStatusFilter("all");
  356:   }
  357: 
  358:   async function handleDelete(resource: SavedTeacherResource) {
  359:     if (resource.status !== "archived") {
  360:       setError("Archive a resource in Content Hub before deleting it permanently.");
  361:       return;
  362:     }
  363: 
  364:     const confirmed = window.confirm(
  365:       `Permanently delete archived resource "${resource.title}"? This action cannot be undone.`,
  366:     );
  367: 
  368:     if (!confirmed) {
  369:       return;
  370:     }
  371: 
  372:     setDeletingId(resource.id);
  373:     setError(null);
  374:     setSuccess(null);
  375: 
  376:     try {
  377:       await deleteTeacherResource(resource.id);
  378: 
  379:       setResources((current) =>
  380:         current.filter((item) => item.id !== resource.id),
  381:       );
  382: 
  383:       setSuccess(`"${resource.title}" was deleted successfully.`);
  384:     } catch (caughtError) {
  385:       console.error("Failed to delete teacher resource:", caughtError);
  386: 
  387:       setError(
  388:         caughtError instanceof Error
  389:           ? caughtError.message
  390:           : "The resource could not be deleted.",
  391:       );
  392:     } finally {
  393:       setDeletingId(null);
  394:     }
  395:   }
  396: 
  397:   if (authLoading) {
  398:     return (
  399:       <div className="flex min-h-[50vh] items-center justify-center">
  400:         <Loader2 className="h-9 w-9 animate-spin text-indigo-600" />
  401:       </div>
  402:     );
  403:   }
  404: 
  405:   return (
  406:     <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl space-y-8 px-4 py-6 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
  407:       <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-700 via-indigo-600 to-sky-600 p-6 text-white shadow-lg sm:p-8">
  408:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
  409:           <div>
  410:             <div className="mb-3 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-white/15 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold backdrop-blur">
  411:               <BookOpen className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
  412:               Teacher Resource Library
  413:             </div>
  414: 
  415:             <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">
  416:               Your teaching resources
  417:             </h1>
  418: 
  419:             <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl leading-7 text-indigo-50">
  420:               Find, organise and manage the classroom resources you created with
  421:               the AI Teacher Assistant.
  422:             </p>
  423:           </div>
  424: 
  425:           <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  426:             <Link
  427:               href="/teacher/content"
  428:               className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-white/25 bg-white/10 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white"
  429:             >
  430:               Content Hub
  431:             </Link>
  432: 
  433:             <Link
  434:               href="/teacher/assistant"
  435:               className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-indigo-700 transition hover:bg-indigo-50"
  436:             >
  437:               <Plus className="h-5 w-5" />
  438:               Create resource
  439:             </Link>
  440:           </div>
  441:         </div>
  442:       </section>
  443: 
  444:       <section className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
  445:         <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5">
  446:           <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500">
  447:             Total resources
  448:           </p>
  449: 
  450:           <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-950">
  451:             {resources.length}
  452:           </p>
  453:         </div>
  454: 
  455:         <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5">
  456:           <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500">
  457:             Draft resources
  458:           </p>
  459: 
  460:           <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-extrabold text-amber-600">
  461:             {resources.filter((resource) => resource.status === "draft").length}
  462:           </p>
  463:         </div>
  464: 
  465:         <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5">
  466:           <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500">
  467:             Published resources
  468:           </p>
  469: 
  470:           <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-extrabold text-emerald-600">
  471:             {
  472:               resources.filter((resource) => resource.status === "published")
  473:                 .length
  474:             }
  475:           </p>
  476:         </div>
  477:       </section>
  478: 
  479:       {error && (
  480:         <div
  481:           role="alert"
  482:           className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-red-800"
  483:         >
  484:           <AlertCircle className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0" />
  485: 
  486:           <div className="flex-1">
  487:             <p className="font-bold">Resource library error</p>
  488: 
  489:             <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6">{error}</p>
  490:           </div>
  491: 
  492:           <button
  493:             type="button"
  494:             onClick={() => void loadResources()}
  495:             className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-red-700 hover:bg-red-50"
  496:           >
  497:             <RefreshCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
  498:             Retry
  499:           </button>
  500:         </div>
  501:       )}
  502: 
  503:       {success && (
  504:         <div
  505:           role="status"
  506:           className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 p-4 text-emerald-800"
  507:         >
  508:           <CheckCircle2 className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0" />
  509:           <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{success}</p>
  510:         </div>
  511:       )}
  512: 
  513:       <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-4 sm:p-5">
  514:         <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_repeat(3,minmax(150px,auto))]">
  515:           <div>
  516:             <label htmlFor="resource-search" className="sr-only">
  517:               Search resources
  518:             </label>
  519: 
  520:             <div className="relative">
  521:               <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
  522: 
  523:               <input
  524:                 id="resource-search"
  525:                 type="search"
  526:                 value={searchTerm}
  527:                 onChange={(event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) =>
  528:                   setSearchTerm(event.target.value)
  529:                 }
  530:                 placeholder="Search by title, topic or description..."
  531:                 className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white py-3 pl-10 pr-4 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none transition placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
  532:               />
  533:             </div>
  534:           </div>
  535: 
  536:           <div className="relative">
  537:             <Filter className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
  538: 
  539:             <select
  540:               aria-label="Filter by resource type"
  541:               value={resourceTypeFilter}
  542:               onChange={(event) => setResourceTypeFilter(event.target.value)}
  543:               className="w-full appearance-none rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white py-3 pl-10 pr-4 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
  544:             >
  545:               <option value="all">All resource types</option>
  546: 
  547:               {resourceTypes.map((resourceType) => (
  548:                 <option key={resourceType} value={resourceType}>
  549:                   {formatResourceType(resourceType)}
  550:                 </option>
  551:               ))}
  552:             </select>
  553:           </div>
  554: 
  555:           <select
  556:             aria-label="Filter by year group"
  557:             value={yearGroupFilter}
  558:             onChange={(event) => setYearGroupFilter(event.target.value)}
  559:             className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
  560:           >
  561:             <option value="all">All year groups</option>
  562: 
  563:             {yearGroups.map((yearGroup) => (
  564:               <option key={yearGroup} value={yearGroup}>
  565:                 {yearGroup}
  566:               </option>
  567:             ))}
  568:           </select>
  569: 
  570:           <select
  571:             aria-label="Filter by status"
  572:             value={statusFilter}
  573:             onChange={(event) =>
  574:               setStatusFilter(event.target.value as StatusFilter)
  575:             }
  576:             className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
  577:           >
  578:             <option value="all">All statuses</option>
  579:             <option value="draft">Draft</option>
  580:             <option value="published">Published</option>
  581:             <option value="archived">Archived</option>
  582:           </select>
  583:         </div>
  584: 
  585:         <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-4 text-sm text-slate-500">
  586:           <p>
  587:             Showing{" "}
  588:             <span className="font-bold text-slate-800">
  589:               {filteredResources.length}
  590:             </span>{" "}
  591:             of{" "}
  592:             <span className="font-bold text-slate-800">{resources.length}</span>{" "}
  593:             resources
  594:           </p>
  595: 
  596:           {hasFilters && (
  597:             <button
  598:               type="button"
  599:               onClick={clearFilters}
  600:               className="font-bold text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-700"
  601:             >
  602:               Clear filters
  603:             </button>
  604:           )}
  605:         </div>
  606:       </section>
  607: 
  608:       {loading ? (
  609:         <ResourceLibrarySkeleton />
  610:       ) : filteredResources.length === 0 ? (
  611:         <EmptyLibrary hasFilters={hasFilters} onClearFilters={clearFilters} />
  612:       ) : (
  613:         <section className="grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
  614:           {filteredResources.map((resource) => (
  615:             <ResourceCard
  616:               key={resource.id}
  617:               resource={resource}
  618:               deletingId={deletingId}
  619:               onDelete={handleDelete}
  620:             />
  621:           ))}
  622:         </section>
  623:       )}
  624:     </main>
  625:   );
  626: }

    1: "use client";
    2: import { AlertCircle, ArrowLeft, BookOpen, CheckCircle2, Clock3, FileQuestion, GraduationCap, Lightbulb, Pencil, RefreshCcw, Send, Tag, Target, Users } from "lucide-react";
    3: 
    4: import { onAuthStateChanged, User } from "firebase/auth";
    5: 
    6: import { doc, getDoc } from "firebase/firestore";
    7: 
    8: import Link from "next/link";
    9: 
   10: import { useParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
   11: 
   12: import { ReactNode, useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
   13: 
   14: import { auth, db } from "@/lib/firebase";
   15: 
   16: import { SavedTeacherResource } from "@/services/teacherResourceService";
   17: import ResourcePublishControls from "@/components/teacher/resources/ResourcePublishControls";
   18: import ExportMenu from "@/components/teacher/resources/ExportMenu";
   19: import AssignResourceModal from "@/components/teacher/resources/AssignResourceModal";
   20: 
   21: type FirestoreTeacherResource = Omit<
   22:   SavedTeacherResource,
   23:   "id" | "createdAt" | "updatedAt"
   24: > & {
   25:   createdAt?: {
   26:     toDate?: () => Date;
   27:   };
   28:   updatedAt?: {
   29:     toDate?: () => Date;
   30:   };
   31: };
   32: 
   33: function convertTimestamp(
   34:   value: FirestoreTeacherResource["createdAt"],
   35: ): Date | null {
   36:   if (value && typeof value.toDate === "function") {
   37:     return value.toDate();
   38:   }
   39: 
   40:   return null;
   41: }
   42: 
   43: function formatDate(value: Date | null): string {
   44:   if (!value) {
   45:     return "Recently saved";
   46:   }
   47: 
   48:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
   49:     day: "numeric",
   50:     month: "long",
   51:     year: "numeric",
   52:     hour: "2-digit",
   53:     minute: "2-digit",
   54:   }).format(value);
   55: }
   56: 
   57: function formatResourceType(value: string): string {
   58:   return value
   59:     .replace(/-/g, " ")
   60:     .replace(/\b\w/g, (character) => character.toUpperCase());
   61: }
   62: 
   63: function BulletList({
   64:   items,
   65:   emptyMessage = "No information was provided.",
   66: }: {
   67:   items: string[];
   68:   emptyMessage?: string;
   69: }) {
   70:   if (items.length === 0) {
   71:     return <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">{emptyMessage}</p>;
   72:   }
   73: 
   74:   return (
   75:     <ul className="space-y-2.5">
   76:       {items.map((item, index) => (
   77:         <li
   78:           key={`${item}-${index}`}
   79:           className="flex items-start gap-2.5 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-700"
   80:         >
   81:           <CheckCircle2
   82:             aria-hidden="true"
   83:             className="mt-1 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-emerald-500"
   84:           />
   85: 
   86:           <span>{item}</span>
   87:         </li>
   88:       ))}
   89:     </ul>
   90:   );
   91: }
   92: 
   93: function ContentCard({
   94:   title,
   95:   description,
   96:   icon,
   97:   children,
   98: }: {
   99:   title: string;
  100:   description?: string;
  101:   icon: ReactNode;
  102:   children: ReactNode;
  103: }) {
  104:   return (
  105:     <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-6">
  106:       <div className="mb-5 flex items-start gap-3">
  107:         <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-600">
  108:           {icon}
  109:         </div>
  110: 
  111:         <div>
  112:           <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-950">{title}</h2>
  113: 
  114:           {description && (
  115:             <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{description}</p>
  116:           )}
  117:         </div>
  118:       </div>
  119: 
  120:       {children}
  121:     </section>
  122:   );
  123: }
  124: 
  125: function ResourceViewerSkeleton() {
  126:   return (
  127:     <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl space-y-6 px-4 py-6 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
  128:       <div className="h-10 w-40 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-slate-200" />
  129: 
  130:       <div className="animate-pulse rounded-3xl bg-slate-200 p-8">
  131:         <div className="h-6 w-48 rounded bg-slate-300" />
  132:         <div className="mt-6 h-10 w-2/3 rounded bg-slate-300" />
  133:         <div className="mt-5 h-5 w-full max-w-3xl rounded bg-slate-300" />
  134:         <div className="mt-3 h-5 w-3/4 rounded bg-slate-300" />
  135:       </div>
  136: 
  137:       <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
  138:         {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, index) => (
  139:           <div
  140:             key={index}
  141:             className="h-64 animate-pulse rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6"
  142:           >
  143:             <div className="h-8 w-48 rounded bg-slate-200" />
  144:             <div className="mt-6 space-y-3">
  145:               <div className="h-4 rounded bg-slate-100" />
  146:               <div className="h-4 rounded bg-slate-100" />
  147:               <div className="h-4 w-3/4 rounded bg-slate-100" />
  148:             </div>
  149:           </div>
  150:         ))}
  151:       </div>
  152:     </main>
  153:   );
  154: }
  155: 
  156: export default function TeacherResourceViewerPage() {
  157:   const params = useParams<{
  158:     resourceId: string;
  159:   }>();
  160: 
  161:   const router = useRouter();
  162: 
  163:   const resourceId = params.resourceId;
  164: 
  165:   const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  166: 
  167:   const [authLoading, setAuthLoading] = useState(true);
  168: 
  169:   const [resource, setResource] = useState<SavedTeacherResource | null>(null);
  170: 
  171:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  172: 
  173:   const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  174: 
  175:   const [isAssignModalOpen, setIsAssignModalOpen] = useState(false);
  176: 
  177:   useEffect(() => {
  178:     const unsubscribe = onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
  179:       setCurrentUser(user);
  180:       setAuthLoading(false);
  181:     });
  182: 
  183:     return unsubscribe;
  184:   }, []);
  185: 
  186:   const loadResource = useCallback(() => {
  187:     if (!currentUser || !resourceId) {
  188:       return Promise.resolve().then(() => {
  189:         setResource(null);
  190:         setError(null);
  191:         setLoading(false);
  192:       });
  193:     }
  194: 
  195:     const teacherId = currentUser.uid;
  196: 
  197:     return Promise.resolve()
  198:       .then(() => {
  199:         setLoading(true);
  200:         setError(null);
  201: 
  202:         const resourceReference = doc(db, "teacherResources", resourceId);
  203: 
  204:         return getDoc(resourceReference);
  205:       })
  206:       .then((resourceSnapshot) => {
  207:         if (!resourceSnapshot.exists()) {
  208:           setResource(null);
  209:           setError("This teaching resource could not be found.");
  210:           return;
  211:         }
  212: 
  213:         const data = resourceSnapshot.data() as FirestoreTeacherResource;
  214: 
  215:         if (data.teacherId !== teacherId) {
  216:           setResource(null);
  217:           setError("You do not have permission to view this resource.");
  218:           return;
  219:         }
  220: 
  221:         setResource({
  222:           id: resourceSnapshot.id,
  223:           teacherId: data.teacherId,
  224:           sourceResourceId: data.sourceResourceId,
  225:           title: data.title,
  226:           topic: data.topic,
  227:           resourceType: data.resourceType,
  228:           yearGroup: data.yearGroup,
  229:           examBoard: data.examBoard,
  230:           duration: data.duration,
  231:           difficulty: data.difficulty,
  232:           content: data.content,
  233:           status: data.status,
  234:           createdAt: convertTimestamp(data.createdAt),
  235:           updatedAt: convertTimestamp(data.updatedAt),
  236:         });
  237:       })
  238:       .catch((caughtError) => {
  239:         console.error("Failed to load teacher resource:", caughtError);
  240: 
  241:         setError(
  242:           caughtError instanceof Error
  243:             ? caughtError.message
  244:             : "The teaching resource could not be loaded.",
  245:         );
  246:       })
  247:       .finally(() => {
  248:         setLoading(false);
  249:       });
  250:   }, [currentUser, resourceId]);
  251: 
  252:   useEffect(() => {
  253:     if (!authLoading) {
  254:       void loadResource();
  255:     }
  256:   }, [authLoading, loadResource]);
  257: 
  258:   if (authLoading || loading) {
  259:     return <ResourceViewerSkeleton />;
  260:   }
  261: 
  262:   if (error || !resource || !currentUser) {
  263:     return (
  264:       <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-4xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
  265:         <Link
  266:           href="/teacher/resources"
  267:           className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-700"
  268:         >
  269:           <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
  270:           Back to Resource Library
  271:         </Link>
  272: 
  273:         <div className="mt-8 rounded-3xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-8 text-center">
  274:           <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-red-100 text-red-600">
  275:             <AlertCircle className="h-7 w-7" />
  276:           </div>
  277: 
  278:           <h1 className="mt-5 text-2xl font-bold text-red-950">
  279:             Resource unavailable
  280:           </h1>
  281: 
  282:           <p className="mx-auto mt-3 max-w-xl text-sm leading-6 text-red-800">
  283:             {error || "This teaching resource could not be found."}
  284:           </p>
  285: 
  286:           <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col justify-center gap-3 sm:flex-row">
  287:             <button
  288:               type="button"
  289:               onClick={() => void loadResource()}
  290:               className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-red-700 hover:bg-red-100"
  291:             >
  292:               <RefreshCcw className="h-4 w-4" />
  293:               Try again
  294:             </button>
  295: 
  296:             <button
  297:               type="button"
  298:               onClick={() => router.push("/teacher/resources")}
  299:               className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-red-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-red-700"
  300:             >
  301:               <BookOpen className="h-4 w-4" />
  302:               Resource Library
  303:             </button>
  304:           </div>
  305:         </div>
  306:       </main>
  307:     );
  308:   }
  309: 
  310:   const content = resource.content;
  311: 
  312:   return (
  313:     <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl space-y-6 px-4 py-6 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
  314:       <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
  315:         <Link
  316:           href="/teacher/resources"
  317:           className="inline-flex w-fit items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-indigo-600 transition hover:text-indigo-700"
  318:         >
  319:           <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
  320:           Back to Resource Library
  321:         </Link>
  322: 
  323:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 xl:flex-row xl:items-center">
  324:           <button
  325:             type="button"
  326:             onClick={() => {
  327:               setIsAssignModalOpen(true);
  328:             }}
  329:             disabled={resource.status !== "published"}
  330:             title={
  331:               resource.status === "published"
  332:                 ? "Assign this resource to a class"
  333:                 : "Publish this resource before assigning it"
  334:             }
  335:             className="inline-flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-sm transition hover:bg-indigo-700 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:ring-offset-2 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-300 disabled:text-slate-600"
  336:           >
  337:             <Send className="h-4 w-4" />
  338:             Assign to class
  339:           </button>
  340: 
  341:           <Link
  342:             href={`/teacher/resources/${resource.id}/edit`}
  343:             className="inline-flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-indigo-200 bg-indigo-50 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-indigo-700 transition hover:bg-indigo-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:ring-offset-2"
  344:           >
  345:             <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" />
  346:             Edit resource
  347:           </Link>
  348: 
  349:           <ExportMenu resource={resource} />
  350: 
  351:           <ResourcePublishControls
  352:             resourceId={resource.id}
  353:             status={resource.status}
  354:             content={resource.content}
  355:             onStatusChange={(newStatus) => {
  356:               setResource((current) => {
  357:                 if (!current) {
  358:                   return current;
  359:                 }
  360: 
  361:                 return {
  362:                   ...current,
  363:                   status: newStatus,
  364:                   updatedAt: new Date(),
  365:                 };
  366:               });
  367:             }}
  368:           />
  369:         </div>
  370:       </div>
  371: 
  372:       <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-700 via-indigo-600 to-sky-600 p-6 text-white shadow-xl shadow-indigo-100 sm:p-8">
  373:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 xl:flex-row xl:items-start xl:justify-between">
  374:           <div className="max-w-4xl">
  375:             <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
  376:               <span className="rounded-full bg-white/15 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold backdrop-blur">
  377:                 {formatResourceType(resource.resourceType)}
  378:               </span>
  379: 
  380:               <span className="rounded-full bg-white/15 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold backdrop-blur">
  381:                 {resource.yearGroup}
  382:               </span>
  383: 
  384:               <span className="rounded-full bg-white/15 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold backdrop-blur">
  385:                 {resource.examBoard}
  386:               </span>
  387: 
  388:               <span className="rounded-full bg-white/15 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold capitalize backdrop-blur">
  389:                 {resource.difficulty}
  390:               </span>
  391: 
  392:               <span
  393:                 className={
  394:                   resource.status === "published"
  395:                     ? "rounded-full bg-emerald-400/20 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold capitalize text-emerald-50 backdrop-blur"
  396:                     : "rounded-full bg-amber-300/20 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold capitalize text-amber-50 backdrop-blur"
  397:                 }
  398:               >
  399:                 {resource.status}
  400:               </span>
  401:             </div>
  402: 
  403:             <h1 className="mt-5 text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">
  404:               {resource.title}
  405:             </h1>
  406: 
  407:             <p className="mt-4 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-7 text-indigo-50 sm:text-base">
  408:               {content.overview}
  409:             </p>
  410: 
  411:             <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-6 gap-y-3 text-sm text-indigo-50">
  412:               <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
  413:                 <Clock3 className="h-4 w-4" />
  414:                 {resource.duration}
  415:               </span>
  416: 
  417:               <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
  418:                 <BookOpen className="h-4 w-4" />
  419:                 {resource.topic}
  420:               </span>
  421: 
  422:               <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
  423:                 <FileQuestion className="h-4 w-4" />
  424:                 {content.assessmentQuestions.length} assessment questions
  425:               </span>
  426:             </div>
  427:           </div>
  428: 
  429:           <div className="shrink-0 rounded-2xl border border-white/20 bg-white/10 p-4 text-sm backdrop-blur">
  430:             <p className="font-bold text-white">Resource details</p>
  431: 
  432:             <dl className="mt-3 space-y-2 text-indigo-50">
  433:               <div className="flex justify-between gap-8">
  434:                 <dt>Sections</dt>
  435:                 <dd className="font-bold text-white">
  436:                   {content.sections.length}
  437:                 </dd>
  438:               </div>
  439: 
  440:               <div className="flex justify-between gap-8">
  441:                 <dt>Saved</dt>
  442:                 <dd className="font-bold text-white">
  443:                   {formatDate(resource.createdAt)}
  444:                 </dd>
  445:               </div>
  446:             </dl>
  447:           </div>
  448:         </div>
  449:       </section>
  450: 
  451:       <div className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-2">
  452:         <ContentCard
  453:           title="Learning objectives"
  454:           description="What students should know or be able to do"
  455:           icon={<Target className="h-5 w-5" />}
  456:         >
  457:           <BulletList items={content.learningObjectives} />
  458:         </ContentCard>
  459: 
  460:         <ContentCard
  461:           title="Success criteria"
  462:           description="How successful learning will be demonstrated"
  463:           icon={<CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5" />}
  464:         >
  465:           <BulletList items={content.successCriteria} />
  466:         </ContentCard>
  467:       </div>
  468: 
  469:       <div className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-2">
  470:         <ContentCard
  471:           title="Prior knowledge"
  472:           description="Knowledge students should already possess"
  473:           icon={<BookOpen className="h-5 w-5" />}
  474:         >
  475:           <BulletList
  476:             items={content.priorKnowledge}
  477:             emptyMessage="No specific prior knowledge is required."
  478:           />
  479:         </ContentCard>
  480: 
  481:         <ContentCard
  482:           title="Key vocabulary"
  483:           description="Important terminology for this resource"
  484:           icon={<Tag className="h-5 w-5" />}
  485:         >
  486:           {content.keywords.length > 0 ? (
  487:             <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
  488:               {content.keywords.map((keyword, index) => (
  489:                 <span
  490:                   key={`${keyword}-${index}`}
  491:                   className="rounded-full border border-indigo-100 bg-indigo-50 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-indigo-700"
  492:                 >
  493:                   {keyword}
  494:                 </span>
  495:               ))}
  496:             </div>
  497:           ) : (
  498:             <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
  499:               No key vocabulary was provided.
  500:             </p>
  501:           )}
  502:         </ContentCard>
  503:       </div>
  504: 
  505:       <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-6">
  506:         <div className="mb-6 flex items-start gap-3">
  507:           <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-600">
  508:             <BookOpen className="h-5 w-5" />
  509:           </div>
  510: 
  511:           <div>
  512:             <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-950">
  513:               Resource sequence
  514:             </h2>
  515: 
  516:             <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
  517:               Complete teaching sequence, student tasks and assessment
  518:               opportunities
  519:             </p>
  520:           </div>
  521:         </div>
  522: 
  523:         <div className="space-y-6">
  524:           {content.sections.map((section, index) => (
  525:             <article
  526:               key={`${section.title}-${index}`}
  527:               className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200"
  528:             >
  529:               <header className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-5 py-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
  530:                 <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
  531:                   <span className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-sm font-bold text-white">
  532:                     {index + 1}
  533:                   </span>
  534: 
  535:                   <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-950">{section.title}</h3>
  536:                 </div>
  537: 
  538:                 <span className="inline-flex w-fit items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-600">
  539:                   <Clock3 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
  540:                   {section.duration}
  541:                 </span>
  542:               </header>
  543: 
  544:               <div className="grid gap-6 p-5 lg:grid-cols-2">
  545:                 <div>
  546:                   <h4 className="mb-2 text-sm font-bold text-slate-950">
  547:                     Teacher instructions
  548:                   </h4>
  549: 
  550:                   <p className="whitespace-pre-line text-sm leading-7 text-slate-700">
  551:                     {section.teacherInstructions}
  552:                   </p>
  553:                 </div>
  554: 
  555:                 <div>
  556:                   <h4 className="mb-2 text-sm font-bold text-slate-950">
  557:                     Student task
  558:                   </h4>
  559: 
  560:                   <p className="whitespace-pre-line text-sm leading-7 text-slate-700">
  561:                     {section.studentTask}
  562:                   </p>
  563:                 </div>
  564: 
  565:                 <div>
  566:                   <h4 className="mb-2 text-sm font-bold text-slate-950">
  567:                     Assessment opportunity
  568:                   </h4>
  569: 
  570:                   <p className="whitespace-pre-line text-sm leading-7 text-slate-700">
  571:                     {section.assessment}
  572:                   </p>
  573:                 </div>
  574: 
  575:                 <div>
  576:                   <h4 className="mb-2 text-sm font-bold text-slate-950">
  577:                     Resources required
  578:                   </h4>
  579: 
  580:                   <BulletList
  581:                     items={section.resources}
  582:                     emptyMessage="No additional resources are required."
  583:                   />
  584:                 </div>
  585:               </div>
  586:             </article>
  587:           ))}
  588:         </div>
  589:       </section>
  590: 
  591:       <ContentCard
  592:         title="Differentiation"
  593:         description="Support and challenge for different learner needs"
  594:         icon={<Users className="h-5 w-5" />}
  595:       >
  596:         <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-3">

  250: export default function EditTeacherResourcePage() {
  251:   const params = useParams<{
  252:     resourceId: string;
  253:   }>();
  254: 
  255:   const router = useRouter();
  256:   const resourceId = params.resourceId;
  257: 
  258:   const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  259: 
  260:   const [authLoading, setAuthLoading] = useState(true);
  261: 
  262:   const [resource, setResource] = useState<GeneratedTeachingResource | null>(
  263:     null,
  264:   );
  265: 
  266:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  267: 
  268:   const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  269: 
  270:   const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  271: 
  272:   const [success, setSuccess] = useState<string | null>(null);
  273: 
  274:   useEffect(() => {
  275:     const unsubscribe = onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
  276:       setCurrentUser(user);
  277:       setAuthLoading(false);
  278:     });
  279: 
  280:     return unsubscribe;
  281:   }, []);
  282: 
  283:   const loadResource = useCallback(async () => {
  284:     await Promise.resolve();
  285: 
  286:     if (!currentUser || !resourceId) {
  287:       setLoading(false);
  288:       return;
  289:     }
  290: 
  291:     setLoading(true);
  292:     setError(null);
  293: 
  294:     try {
  295:       const resourceReference = doc(db, "teacherResources", resourceId);
  296: 
  297:       const resourceSnapshot = await getDoc(resourceReference);
  298: 
  299:       if (!resourceSnapshot.exists()) {
  300:         setError("This teaching resource could not be found.");
  301:         setResource(null);
  302:         return;
  303:       }
  304: 
  305:       const data = resourceSnapshot.data() as EditableResourceDocument;
  306: 
  307:       if (data.teacherId !== currentUser.uid) {
  308:         setError("You do not have permission to edit this resource.");
  309:         setResource(null);
  310:         return;
  311:       }
  312: 
  313:       setResource(data.content);
  314:     } catch (caughtError) {
  315:       console.error("Failed to load editable resource:", caughtError);
  316: 
  317:       setError(
  318:         caughtError instanceof Error
  319:           ? caughtError.message
  320:           : "The resource could not be loaded.",
  321:       );
  322:     } finally {
  323:       setLoading(false);
  324:     }
  325:   }, [currentUser, resourceId]);
  326: 
  327:   useEffect(() => {
  328:     if (!authLoading) {
  329:       void Promise.resolve().then(() => loadResource());
  330:     }
  331:   }, [authLoading, loadResource]);
  332: 
  333:   function updateTopLevelField<K extends keyof GeneratedTeachingResource>(
  334:     field: K,
  335:     value: GeneratedTeachingResource[K],
  336:   ) {
  337:     setResource((current) => {
  338:       if (!current) {
  339:         return current;
  340:       }
  341: 
  342:       return {
  343:         ...current,
  344:         [field]: value,
  345:       };
  346:     });
  347: 
  348:     setError(null);
  349:     setSuccess(null);
  350:   }
  351: 
  352:   function updateStringArrayItem(
  353:     field: StringArrayField,
  354:     index: number,
  355:     value: string,
  356:   ) {
  357:     setResource((current) => {
  358:       if (!current) {
  359:         return current;
  360:       }
  361: 
  362:       const updatedItems = [...current[field]];
  363:       updatedItems[index] = value;
  364: 
  365:       return {
  366:         ...current,
  367:         [field]: updatedItems,
  368:       };
  369:     });
  370: 
  371:     setSuccess(null);
  372:   }
  373: 
  374:   function addStringArrayItem(field: StringArrayField) {
  375:     setResource((current) => {
  376:       if (!current) {
  377:         return current;
  378:       }
  379: 
  380:       return {
  381:         ...current,
  382:         [field]: [...current[field], ""],
  383:       };
  384:     });
  385:   }
  386: 
  387:   function removeStringArrayItem(field: StringArrayField, index: number) {
  388:     setResource((current) => {
  389:       if (!current || current[field].length <= 1) {
  390:         return current;
  391:       }
  392: 
  393:       return {
  394:         ...current,
  395:         [field]: current[field].filter((_, itemIndex) => itemIndex !== index),
  396:       };
  397:     });
  398:   }
  399: 
  400:   function updateSection(
  401:     index: number,
  402:     field: keyof ResourceSection,
  403:     value: string | string[],
  404:   ) {
  405:     setResource((current) => {
  406:       if (!current) {
  407:         return current;
  408:       }
  409: 
  410:       const sections = [...current.sections];
  411: 
  412:       sections[index] = {
  413:         ...sections[index],
  414:         [field]: value,
  415:       };
  416: 
  417:       return {
  418:         ...current,
  419:         sections,
  420:       };
  421:     });
  422: 
  423:     setSuccess(null);
  424:   }
