  553:     setResource((current) => {
  554:       if (!current) {
  555:         return current;
  556:       }
  557: 
  558:       return {
  559:         ...current,
  560:         misconceptions: current.misconceptions.filter(
  561:           (_, itemIndex) => itemIndex !== index,
  562:         ),
  563:       };
  564:     });
  565:   }
  566: 
  567:   function updateAssessmentQuestion(
  568:     index: number,
  569:     field: keyof ResourceAssessmentQuestion,
  570:     value: string | number,
  571:   ) {
  572:     setResource((current) => {
  573:       if (!current) {
  574:         return current;
  575:       }
  576: 
  577:       const assessmentQuestions = [...current.assessmentQuestions];
  578: 
  579:       assessmentQuestions[index] = {
  580:         ...assessmentQuestions[index],
  581:         [field]: value,
  582:       };
  583: 
  584:       return {
  585:         ...current,
  586:         assessmentQuestions,
  587:       };
  588:     });
  589:   }
  590: 
  591:   function addAssessmentQuestion() {
  592:     setResource((current) => {
  593:       if (!current) {
  594:         return current;
  595:       }
  596: 
  597:       return {
  598:         ...current,
  599:         assessmentQuestions: [
  600:           ...current.assessmentQuestions,
  601:           createEmptyAssessmentQuestion(),
  602:         ],
  603:       };
  604:     });
  605:   }
  606: 
  607:   function removeAssessmentQuestion(index: number) {
  608:     setResource((current) => {
  609:       if (!current || current.assessmentQuestions.length <= 1) {
  610:         return current;
  611:       }
  612: 
  613:       return {
  614:         ...current,
  615:         assessmentQuestions: current.assessmentQuestions.filter(
  616:           (_, itemIndex) => itemIndex !== index,
  617:         ),
  618:       };
  619:     });
  620:   }
  621: 
  622:   function validateResource(): string | null {
  623:     if (!resource) {
  624:       return "The resource could not be loaded.";
  625:     }
  626: 
  627:     if (resource.title.trim().length < 2) {
  628:       return "Enter a valid resource title.";
  629:     }
  630: 
  631:     if (resource.topic.trim().length < 2) {
  632:       return "Enter a valid topic.";
  633:     }
  634: 
  635:     if (!resource.overview.trim()) {
  636:       return "Enter a resource overview.";
  637:     }
  638: 
  639:     if (resource.learningObjectives.some((item) => !item.trim())) {
  640:       return "Learning objectives cannot contain blank items.";
  641:     }
  642: 
  643:     if (resource.successCriteria.some((item) => !item.trim())) {
  644:       return "Success criteria cannot contain blank items.";
  645:     }
  646: 
  647:     if (
  648:       resource.sections.some(
  649:         (section) =>
  650:           !section.title.trim() ||
  651:           !section.teacherInstructions.trim() ||
  652:           !section.studentTask.trim(),
  653:       )
  654:     ) {
  655:       return "Every section requires a title, teacher instructions and student task.";
  656:     }
  657: 
  658:     if (
  659:       resource.assessmentQuestions.some(
  660:         (question) =>
  661:           !question.question.trim() ||
  662:           !question.answer.trim() ||
  663:           question.marks < 1,
  664:       )
  665:     ) {
  666:       return "Every assessment question requires a question, answer and valid mark value.";
  667:     }
  668: 
  669:     return null;
  670:   }
  671: 
  672:   async function handleSave() {
  673:     const validationError = validateResource();
  674: 
  675:     if (validationError) {
  676:       setError(validationError);
  677:       setSuccess(null);
  678:       return;
  679:     }
  680: 
  681:     if (!resource) {
  682:       return;
  683:     }
  684: 
  685:     setSaving(true);
  686:     setError(null);
  687:     setSuccess(null);
  688: 
  689:     try {
  690:       const cleanedResource: GeneratedTeachingResource = {
  691:         ...resource,
  692:         title: resource.title.trim(),
  693:         topic: resource.topic.trim(),
  694:         overview: resource.overview.trim(),
  695:         learningObjectives: resource.learningObjectives.map((item) =>
  696:           item.trim(),
  697:         ),
  698:         successCriteria: resource.successCriteria.map((item) => item.trim()),
  699:         keywords: resource.keywords.map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean),
  700:         priorKnowledge: resource.priorKnowledge
  701:           .map((item) => item.trim())
  702:           .filter(Boolean),
  703:         sections: resource.sections.map((section) => ({
  704:           ...section,
  705:           title: section.title.trim(),
  706:           duration: section.duration.trim(),
  707:           teacherInstructions: section.teacherInstructions.trim(),
  708:           studentTask: section.studentTask.trim(),
  709:           assessment: section.assessment.trim(),
  710:           resources: section.resources
  711:             .map((item) => item.trim())
  712:             .filter(Boolean),
  713:         })),
  714:         differentiation: {
  715:           support: resource.differentiation.support
  716:             .map((item) => item.trim())
  717:             .filter(Boolean),
  718:           core: resource.differentiation.core
  719:             .map((item) => item.trim())
  720:             .filter(Boolean),
  721:           stretch: resource.differentiation.stretch
  722:             .map((item) => item.trim())
  723:             .filter(Boolean),
  724:         },
  725:         misconceptions: resource.misconceptions
  726:           .map((item) => ({
  727:             misconception: item.misconception.trim(),
  728:             correction: item.correction.trim(),
  729:           }))
  730:           .filter((item) => item.misconception && item.correction),
  731:         assessmentQuestions: resource.assessmentQuestions.map((question) => ({
  732:           question: question.question.trim(),
  733:           answer: question.answer.trim(),
  734:           marks: Math.max(1, Math.round(question.marks)),
  735:         })),
  736:         homework: resource.homework.trim(),
  737:         teacherNotes: resource.teacherNotes.trim(),
  738:       };
  739: 
  740:       await updateTeacherResourceContent(resourceId, cleanedResource);
  741: 
  742:       setResource(cleanedResource);
  743:       setSuccess("Your resource changes were saved successfully.");
  744: 
  745:       window.setTimeout(() => {
  746:         router.push(`/teacher/resources/${resourceId}`);
  747:       }, 800);
  748:     } catch (caughtError) {
  749:       console.error("Failed to save resource changes:", caughtError);
  750: 
  751:       setError(
  752:         caughtError instanceof Error
  753:           ? caughtError.message
  754:           : "Your changes could not be saved.",
  755:       );
  756:     } finally {
  757:       setSaving(false);
  758:     }
  759:   }
  760: 
  761:   if (authLoading || loading) {
  762:     return <EditPageSkeleton />;
  763:   }
  764: 
  765:   if (error && !resource) {
  766:     return (
  767:       <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-4xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
  768:         <Link
  769:           href="/teacher/resources"
  770:           className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-700"
  771:         >
  772:           <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
  773:           Back to Resource Library
  774:         </Link>
  775: 
  776:         <section className="mt-8 rounded-3xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-8 text-center">
  777:           <AlertCircle className="mx-auto h-10 w-10 text-red-600" />
  778: 
  779:           <h1 className="mt-4 text-2xl font-bold text-red-950">
  780:             Resource unavailable
  781:           </h1>
  782: 
  783:           <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-800">{error}</p>
  784:         </section>
  785:       </main>
  786:     );
  787:   }
  788: 
  789:   if (!resource) {
  790:     return null;
  791:   }
  792: 
  793:   return (
  794:     <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl space-y-6 px-4 py-6 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
  795:       <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
  796:         <Link
  797:           href={`/teacher/resources/${resourceId}`}
  798:           className="inline-flex w-fit items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-700"
  799:         >
  800:           <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
  801:           Cancel editing
  802:         </Link>
  803: 
  804:         <button
  805:           type="button"
  806:           onClick={handleSave}
  807:           disabled={saving}
  808:           className="inline-flex w-fit items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-lg shadow-indigo-100 transition hover:bg-indigo-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-300 disabled:shadow-none"
  809:         >
  810:           {saving ? (
  811:             <>
  812:               <Loader2 className="h-5 w-5 animate-spin" />
  813:               Saving changes...
  814:             </>
  815:           ) : (
  816:             <>
  817:               <Save className="h-5 w-5" />
  818:               Save changes
  819:             </>
  820:           )}
  821:         </button>
  822:       </div>
  823: 
  824:       <section className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-700 via-indigo-600 to-sky-600 p-6 text-white shadow-lg sm:p-8">
  825:         <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
  826:           <div className="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-white/15">
  827:             <BookOpen className="h-6 w-6" />
  828:           </div>
  829: 
  830:           <div>
  831:             <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight">
  832:               Edit teaching resource
  833:             </h1>
  834: 
  835:             <p className="mt-2 max-w-2xl leading-7 text-indigo-50">
  836:               Refine the generated lesson content before publishing, exporting
  837:               or assigning it to a class.
  838:             </p>
  839:           </div>
  840:         </div>
  841:       </section>
  842: 
  843:       {error && (
  844:         <div
  845:           role="alert"
  846:           className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-red-800"
  847:         >
  848:           <AlertCircle className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0" />
  849: 
  850:           <div>
  851:             <p className="font-bold">Changes not saved</p>
  852: 
  853:             <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6">{error}</p>
  854:           </div>
  855:         </div>
  856:       )}
  857: 
  858:       {success && (
  859:         <div
  860:           role="status"
  861:           className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 p-4 text-emerald-800"
  862:         >
  863:           <CheckCircle2 className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0" />
  864: 
  865:           <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{success}</p>
  866:         </div>
  867:       )}
  868: 
  869:       <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-6">
  870:         <SectionHeader
  871:           icon={<BookOpen className="h-5 w-5" />}
  872:           title="Resource details"
  873:           description="Update the resource title, topic and curriculum information."
  874:         />
  875: 
  876:         <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-2">
  877:           <div>
  878:             <FieldLabel htmlFor="resource-title" required>
  879:               Resource title
  880:             </FieldLabel>
  881: 
  882:             <input
  883:               id="resource-title"
  884:               value={resource.title}
  885:               onChange={(event) =>
  886:                 updateTopLevelField("title", event.target.value)
  887:               }
  888:               maxLength={200}
  889:               className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none transition focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
  890:             />
  891:           </div>
  892: 
  893:           <div>
  894:             <FieldLabel htmlFor="resource-topic" required>
  895:               Topic
  896:             </FieldLabel>
  897: 
  898:             <input
  899:               id="resource-topic"
  900:               value={resource.topic}
  901:               onChange={(event) =>
  902:                 updateTopLevelField("topic", event.target.value)
  903:               }
  904:               maxLength={150}
  905:               className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none transition focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
  906:             />
  907:           </div>
  908: 
  909:           <div>
  910:             <FieldLabel htmlFor="resource-year">
  911:               Year group or qualification
  912:             </FieldLabel>
  913: 
  914:             <div className="relative">
  915:               <select
  916:                 id="resource-year"
  917:                 value={resource.yearGroup}
  918:                 onChange={(event) =>
  919:                   updateTopLevelField("yearGroup", event.target.value)
  920:                 }
  921:                 className="w-full appearance-none rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 pr-10 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none transition focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
  922:               >
  923:                 {YEAR_GROUPS.map((yearGroup) => (
  924:                   <option key={yearGroup} value={yearGroup}>
  925:                     {yearGroup}
  926:                   </option>
  927:                 ))}
  928:               </select>
  929: 
  930:               <ChevronDown className="pointer-events-none absolute right-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
  931:             </div>
  932:           </div>
  933: 
  934:           <div>
  935:             <FieldLabel htmlFor="resource-board">Exam board</FieldLabel>
  936: 
  937:             <div className="relative">
  938:               <select
  939:                 id="resource-board"
  940:                 value={resource.examBoard}
  941:                 onChange={(event) =>
  942:                   updateTopLevelField("examBoard", event.target.value)
  943:                 }
  944:                 className="w-full appearance-none rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 pr-10 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none transition focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
  945:               >
  946:                 {EXAM_BOARDS.map((examBoard) => (
  947:                   <option key={examBoard} value={examBoard}>
  948:                     {examBoard}
  949:                   </option>
  950:                 ))}
  951:               </select>
  952: 
  953:               <ChevronDown className="pointer-events-none absolute right-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
  954:             </div>
  955:           </div>
  956: 
  957:           <div>
  958:             <FieldLabel htmlFor="resource-duration">Duration</FieldLabel>
  959: 
  960:             <div className="relative">
  961:               <Clock3 className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
  962: 
  963:               <input
  964:                 id="resource-duration"
  965:                 value={resource.duration}
  966:                 onChange={(event) =>
  967:                   updateTopLevelField("duration", event.target.value)
  968:                 }
  969:                 placeholder="For example: 60 minutes"
  970:                 className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white py-3 pl-10 pr-4 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none transition focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
  971:               />
  972:             </div>
  973:           </div>
  974: 
  975:           <div>
  976:             <FieldLabel htmlFor="resource-difficulty">Difficulty</FieldLabel>
  977: 
  978:             <select
  979:               id="resource-difficulty"
  980:               value={resource.difficulty}
  981:               onChange={(event) =>
  982:                 updateTopLevelField("difficulty", event.target.value)
  983:               }
  984:               className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm capitalize text-slate-900 outline-none transition focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
  985:             >
  986:               {DIFFICULTY_LEVELS.map((difficulty) => (
  987:                 <option key={difficulty} value={difficulty}>
  988:                   {difficulty}
  989:                 </option>
  990:               ))}
  991:             </select>
  992:           </div>
  993:         </div>
  994: 
  995:         <div className="mt-5">
  996:           <FieldLabel htmlFor="resource-overview" required>
  997:             Resource overview
  998:           </FieldLabel>
  999: 
 1000:           <textarea
 1001:             id="resource-overview"
 1002:             value={resource.overview}
 1003:             onChange={(event) =>
 1004:               updateTopLevelField("overview", event.target.value)
 1005:             }
 1006:             rows={5}
 1007:             maxLength={2500}
 1008:             className="w-full resize-y rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-7 text-slate-900 outline-none transition focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
 1009:           />
 1010:         </div>
 1011:       </section>
 1012: 
 1013:       <div className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-2">
 1014:         <EditableStringList
 1015:           title="Learning objectives"
 1016:           description="What students should know or be able to do."
 1017:           icon={<Target className="h-5 w-5" />}
 1018:           items={resource.learningObjectives}
 1019:           placeholder="Enter a measurable learning objective."
 1020:           onChange={(index, value) =>
 1021:             updateStringArrayItem("learningObjectives", index, value)
 1022:           }
 1023:           onAdd={() => addStringArrayItem("learningObjectives")}
 1024:           onRemove={(index) =>
 1025:             removeStringArrayItem("learningObjectives", index)
 1026:           }
 1027:         />
 1028: 
 1029:         <EditableStringList
 1030:           title="Success criteria"
 1031:           description="How students will demonstrate successful learning."
 1032:           icon={<CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5" />}
 1033:           items={resource.successCriteria}
 1034:           placeholder="Enter a success criterion."
 1035:           onChange={(index, value) =>
 1036:             updateStringArrayItem("successCriteria", index, value)
 1037:           }
 1038:           onAdd={() => addStringArrayItem("successCriteria")}
 1039:           onRemove={(index) => removeStringArrayItem("successCriteria", index)}
 1040:         />
 1041: 
 1042:         <EditableStringList
 1043:           title="Prior knowledge"
 1044:           description="Knowledge students should already possess."
 1045:           icon={<BookOpen className="h-5 w-5" />}
 1046:           items={
 1047:             resource.priorKnowledge.length > 0 ? resource.priorKnowledge : [""]
 1048:           }
 1049:           placeholder="Enter required prior knowledge."
 1050:           onChange={(index, value) =>
 1051:             updateStringArrayItem("priorKnowledge", index, value)
 1052:           }
 1053:           onAdd={() => addStringArrayItem("priorKnowledge")}
 1054:           onRemove={(index) => removeStringArrayItem("priorKnowledge", index)}
 1055:         />
 1056: 
 1057:         <EditableStringList
 1058:           title="Keywords"
 1059:           description="Important vocabulary students should use."
 1060:           icon={<Tag className="h-5 w-5" />}
 1061:           items={resource.keywords.length > 0 ? resource.keywords : [""]}
 1062:           placeholder="Enter a keyword."
 1063:           onChange={(index, value) =>
 1064:             updateStringArrayItem("keywords", index, value)
 1065:           }
 1066:           onAdd={() => addStringArrayItem("keywords")}
 1067:           onRemove={(index) => removeStringArrayItem("keywords", index)}
 1068:         />
 1069:       </div>
 1070: 
 1071:       <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-6">
 1072:         <SectionHeader
 1073:           icon={<BookOpen className="h-5 w-5" />}
 1074:           title="Resource sequence"
 1075:           description="Edit teacher instructions, student tasks, assessment opportunities and required resources."
 1076:         />
 1077: 
 1078:         <div className="space-y-6">
 1079:           {resource.sections.map((section, index) => (
 1080:             <article
 1081:               key={`section-${index}`}
 1082:               className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200"
 1083:             >
 1084:               <header className="flex flex-col gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
 1085:                 <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
 1086:                   <span className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-sm font-bold text-white">
 1087:                     {index + 1}
 1088:                   </span>
 1089: 
 1090:                   <p className="font-bold text-slate-950">
 1091:                     Section {index + 1}
 1092:                   </p>
 1093:                 </div>
 1094: 
 1095:                 <button
 1096:                   type="button"
 1097:                   onClick={() => removeSection(index)}
 1098:                   disabled={resource.sections.length === 1}
 1099:                   className="inline-flex w-fit items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-red-600 transition hover:bg-red-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
 1100:                 >
 1101:                   <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
 1102:                   Remove section
 1103:                 </button>
 1104:               </header>
 1105: 
 1106:               <div className="grid gap-5 p-5 lg:grid-cols-2">
 1107:                 <div>
 1108:                   <FieldLabel htmlFor={`section-title-${index}`} required>
 1109:                     Section title
 1110:                   </FieldLabel>
 1111: 
 1112:                   <input
 1113:                     id={`section-title-${index}`}
 1114:                     value={section.title}
 1115:                     onChange={(event) =>
 1116:                       updateSection(index, "title", event.target.value)
 1117:                     }
 1118:                     className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
 1119:                   />
 1120:                 </div>
 1121: 
 1122:                 <div>
 1123:                   <FieldLabel htmlFor={`section-duration-${index}`}>
 1124:                     Duration
 1125:                   </FieldLabel>
 1126: 
 1127:                   <input
 1128:                     id={`section-duration-${index}`}
 1129:                     value={section.duration}
 1130:                     onChange={(event) =>
 1131:                       updateSection(index, "duration", event.target.value)
 1132:                     }
 1133:                     className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
 1134:                   />
 1135:                 </div>
 1136: 
 1137:                 <div>
 1138:                   <FieldLabel
 1139:                     htmlFor={`teacher-instructions-${index}`}
 1140:                     required
 1141:                   >
 1142:                     Teacher instructions
 1143:                   </FieldLabel>
 1144: 
 1145:                   <textarea
 1146:                     id={`teacher-instructions-${index}`}
 1147:                     value={section.teacherInstructions}
 1148:                     onChange={(event) =>
 1149:                       updateSection(
 1150:                         index,
 1151:                         "teacherInstructions",
 1152:                         event.target.value,
 1153:                       )
 1154:                     }
 1155:                     rows={6}

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import {
    4:   CalendarDays,
    5:   CheckCircle2,

    7:   School,
    8:   Send,
    9:   Users,
   10:   X,
   11: } from "lucide-react";
   12: 
   13: import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
   14: 
   15: import toast from "react-hot-toast";
   16: 
   17: import { getTeacherClasses, TeacherClass } from "@/services/classService";
   18: 
   19: import { createResourceAssignment } from "@/services/resourceAssignmentService";
   20: 
   21: type AssignableResource = {
   22:   id: string;
   23:   title: string;
   24:   topic: string;
   25:   resourceType: string;
   26:   status: string;
   27: };
   28: 
   29: type AssignResourceModalProps = {
   30:   isOpen: boolean;
   31:   onClose: () => void;
   32: 
   33:   resource: AssignableResource;
   34: 
   35:   teacherId: string;
   36:   teacherName?: string;
   37: 
   38:   onAssignmentCreated?: (assignmentId: string) => void;
   39: };
   40: 
   41: function getMinimumDueDate(): string {
   42:   const tomorrow = new Date();
   43: 
   44:   tomorrow.setDate(tomorrow.getDate() + 1);
   45: 
   46:   return tomorrow.toISOString().split("T")[0];
   47: }
   48: 
   49: function formatClassDescription(teacherClass: TeacherClass): string {
   50:   return [
   51:     teacherClass.subject,
   52:     teacherClass.yearGroup,
   53:     teacherClass.academicYear,
   54:   ]
   55:     .filter(Boolean)
   56:     .join(" Â· ");
   57: }
   58: 
   59: export default function AssignResourceModal({
   60:   isOpen,
   61:   onClose,
   62:   resource,
   63:   teacherId,
   64:   teacherName,
   65:   onAssignmentCreated,
   66: }: AssignResourceModalProps) {
   67:   const [classes, setClasses] = useState<TeacherClass[]>([]);
   68: 
   69:   const [selectedClassId, setSelectedClassId] = useState("");
   70: 
   71:   const [dueDate, setDueDate] = useState("");
   72: 
   73:   const [instructions, setInstructions] = useState("");
   74: 
   75:   const [isLoadingClasses, setIsLoadingClasses] = useState(false);
   76: 
   77:   const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
   78: 
   79:   const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState("");
   80: 
   81:   const activeClasses = useMemo(
   82:     () => classes.filter((teacherClass) => teacherClass.status === "active"),
   83:     [classes],
   84:   );
   85: 
   86:   const selectedClass = useMemo(
   87:     () =>
   88:       activeClasses.find(
   89:         (teacherClass) => teacherClass.id === selectedClassId,
   90:       ) ?? null,
   91:     [activeClasses, selectedClassId],
   92:   );
   93: 
   94:   useEffect(() => {
   95:     if (!isOpen || !teacherId) {
   96:       return;
   97:     }
   98: 
   99:     async function loadClasses() {
  100:       try {
  101:         setIsLoadingClasses(true);
  102:         setLoadError("");
  103: 
  104:         const teacherClasses = await getTeacherClasses(teacherId);
  105: 
  106:         setClasses(teacherClasses);
  107:       } catch (error) {
  108:         console.error("Unable to load classes:", error);
  109: 
  110:         setLoadError(
  111:           error instanceof Error
  112:             ? error.message
  113:             : "Unable to load your classes.",
  114:         );
  115:       } finally {
  116:         setIsLoadingClasses(false);
  117:       }
  118:     }
  119: 
  120:     void loadClasses();
  121:   }, [isOpen, teacherId]);
  122: 
  123:   function resetForm() {
  124:     setSelectedClassId("");
  125:     setDueDate("");
  126:     setInstructions("");
  127:     setLoadError("");
  128:   }
  129: 
  130:   function handleClose() {
  131:     if (isSubmitting) {
  132:       return;
  133:     }
  134: 
  135:     resetForm();
  136:     onClose();
  137:   }
  138: 
  139:   async function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
  140:     event.preventDefault();
  141: 
  142:     if (resource.status !== "published") {
  143:       toast.error("Publish this resource before assigning it.");
  144: 
  145:       return;
  146:     }
  147: 
  148:     if (!selectedClass) {
  149:       toast.error("Select a class.");
  150: 
  151:       return;
  152:     }
  153: 
  154:     if (selectedClass.studentIds.length === 0) {
  155:       toast.error(
  156:         "Add at least one student to this class before assigning work.",
  157:       );
  158: 
  159:       return;
  160:     }
  161: 
  162:     if (!dueDate) {
  163:       toast.error("Select a due date.");
  164: 
  165:       return;
  166:     }
  167: 
  168:     try {
  169:       setIsSubmitting(true);
  170: 
  171:       const selectedDueDate = new Date(`${dueDate}T23:59:59`);
  172: 
  173:       const assignmentId = await createResourceAssignment({
  174:         resourceId: resource.id,
  175:         resourceTitle: resource.title,
  176:         resourceTopic: resource.topic,
  177:         resourceType: resource.resourceType,
  178: 
  179:         teacherId,
  180:         teacherName: teacherName || "Teacher",
  181: 
  182:         classId: selectedClass.id,
  183:         className: selectedClass.name,
  184: 
  185:         instructions,
  186:         dueDate: selectedDueDate,
  187: 
  188:         studentIds: selectedClass.studentIds,
  189:       });
  190: 
  191:       toast.success(`${resource.title} assigned to ${selectedClass.name}.`);
  192: 
  193:       onAssignmentCreated?.(assignmentId);
  194: 
  195:       resetForm();
  196:       onClose();
  197:     } catch (error) {
  198:       console.error("Unable to create assignment:", error);
  199: 
  200:       toast.error(
  201:         error instanceof Error
  202:           ? error.message
  203:           : "Unable to assign this resource.",
  204:       );
  205:     } finally {
  206:       setIsSubmitting(false);
  207:     }
  208:   }
  209: 
  210:   if (!isOpen) {
  211:     return null;
  212:   }
  213: 
  214:   return (
  215:     <div
  216:       className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/60 p-4"
  217:       role="dialog"
  218:       aria-modal="true"
  219:       aria-labelledby="assign-resource-title"
  220:       onMouseDown={(event) => {
  221:         if (event.target === event.currentTarget) {
  222:           handleClose();
  223:         }
  224:       }}
  225:     >
  226:       <div className="flex max-h-[92vh] w-full max-w-2xl flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-3xl bg-white shadow-2xl">
  227:         <header className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 p-6">
  228:           <div>
  229:             <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-indigo-600">
  230:               Class assignment
  231:             </p>
  232: 
  233:             <h2
  234:               id="assign-resource-title"
  235:               className="mt-2 text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-950"
  236:             >

   30: export default function ExistingTeachingResourceSelector({
   31:   selectedResource,
   32:   onSelect,
   33: }: Props) {
   34:   const {
   35:     user,
   36:     loading:
   37:       authLoading,
   38:   } = useAuth();
   39: 
   40:   const [
   41:     resources,
   42:     setResources,
   43:   ] =
   44:     useState<
   45:       SavedTeacherResource[]
   46:     >([]);
   47: 
   48:   const [
   49:     loading,
   50:     setLoading,
   51:   ] =
   52:     useState(true);
   53: 
   54:   const [
   55:     searchTerm,
   56:     setSearchTerm,
   57:   ] =
   58:     useState("");
   59: 
   60:   useEffect(() => {
   61:     if (
   62:       authLoading
   63:     ) {
   64:       return;
   65:     }
   66: 
   67:     /*
   68:      * Guard Firebase user first.
   69:      *
   70:      * Do not create teacherId with user?.uid before this check because
   71:      * TypeScript would retain string | undefined inside the async closure.
   72:      */
   73:     if (!user?.uid) {
   74:       void Promise.resolve().then(() => {
   75:         setResources([]);
   76:         setLoading(false);
   77:       });
   78: 
   79:       return;
   80:     }
   81: 
   82:     /*
   83:      * From this point teacherId is guaranteed to be a string.
   84:      * Capturing it here also keeps the nested async function type-safe.
   85:      */
   86:     const teacherId =
   87:       user.uid;
   88: 
   89:     let cancelled =
   90:       false;
   91: 
   92:     async function load() {
   93:       try {
   94:         await Promise.resolve();
   95: 
   96:         if (cancelled) {
   97:           return;
   98:         }
   99: 
  100:         setLoading(true);
  101: 
  102:         const loaded =
  103:           await getTeacherResources(
  104:             teacherId,
  105:           );
  106: 
  107:         if (
  108:           !cancelled
  109:         ) {
  110:           setResources(
  111:             loaded.filter(
  112:               (resource) =>
  113:                 resource.status ===
  114:                 "published",
  115:             ),
  116:           );
  117:         }
  118:       } catch (error) {
  119:         console.error(
  120:           "Unable to load published teacher resources:",
  121:           error,
  122:         );
  123: 
  124:         if (
  125:           !cancelled
  126:         ) {
  127:           setResources([]);
  128:         }
  129:       } finally {
  130:         if (
  131:           !cancelled
  132:         ) {
  133:           setLoading(false);
  134:         }
  135:       }
  136:     }
  137: 
  138:     void load();
  139: 
  140:     return () => {
  141:       cancelled = true;
  142:     };
  143:   }, [
  144:     authLoading,
  145:     user?.uid,
  146:   ]);
  147: 
  148:   const filtered =
  149:     useMemo(() => {
  150:       const search =
  151:         searchTerm
  152:           .trim()
  153:           .toLowerCase();
  154: 
  155:       if (!search) {
  156:         return resources;
  157:       }
  158: 
  159:       return resources.filter(
  160:         (resource) =>
  161:           [
  162:             resource.title,
  163:             resource.topic,
  164:             resource.examBoard,
  165:             resource.yearGroup,
  166:           ]
  167:             .join(" ")
  168:             .toLowerCase()
  169:             .includes(search),
  170:       );
  171:     }, [
  172:       resources,
  173:       searchTerm,
  174:     ]);
  175: 
  176:   return (
  177:     <div className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-indigo-200 bg-indigo-50/50 p-6">
  178:       <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-indigo-600">
  179:         Teacher Resource Library
  180:       </p>
  181: 
  182:       <h3 className="mt-2 text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
  183:         Choose a published teaching resource
  184:       </h3>
  185: 
  186:       <input
  187:         type="search"
  188:         value={
  189:           searchTerm
  190:         }
  191:         onChange={(
  192:           event,
  193:         ) =>
  194:           setSearchTerm(
  195:             event.target
  196:               .value,
  197:           )
  198:         }
  199:         placeholder="Search title, topic, board or year group..."
  200:         className="mt-5 w-full rounded-xl border border-indigo-200 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-indigo-500"
  201:       />
  202: 
  203:       {loading ? (
  204:         <div className="mt-5 space-y-3">
  205:           <div className="h-24 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-white" />
  206:           <div className="h-24 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-white" />
  207:         </div>
  208:       ) : filtered.length ===
  209:         0 ? (
  210:         <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl bg-white p-6 text-center">
  211:           <p className="font-bold text-slate-900">
  212:             No published resources match
  213:           </p>
  214: 
  215:           <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
  216:             Publish a teaching resource in the Content Hub before assigning it.
  217:           </p>
  218:         </div>

  220:         <div className="mt-5 grid gap-3">
  221:           {filtered.map(
  222:             (resource) => {
  223:               const selected =
  224:                 selectedResource
  225:                   ?.resourceType ===
  226:                   "teaching-resource" &&
  227:                 selectedResource
  228:                   .resourceId ===
  229:                   resource.id;
  230: 
  231:               return (
  232:                 <button
  233:                   key={
  234:                     resource.id
  235:                   }
  236:                   type="button"
  237:                   onClick={() =>
  238:                     onSelect({

  240:                         resource.id,

  243:                         resource.title,

  251:                         "teaching-resource",
  252:

FILE: components\teacher\resources\ResourcePublishControls.tsx

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import {
    4:   AlertCircle,
    5:   CheckCircle2,
    6:   FileCheck2,
    7:   Loader2,
    8:   RotateCcw,
    9:   Send,
   10:   X,
   11: } from "lucide-react";
   12: 
   13: import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
   14: 
   15: import {
   16:   GeneratedTeachingResource,
   17:   updateTeacherResourceStatus,
   18: } from "@/services/teacherResourceService";
   19: 
   20: type ResourceStatus = "draft" | "published" | "archived";
   21: 
   22: type ResourcePublishControlsProps = {
   23:   resourceId: string;
   24:   status: ResourceStatus;
   25:   content: GeneratedTeachingResource;
   26:   onStatusChange: (status: ResourceStatus) => void;
   27: };
   28: 
   29: type ConfirmationAction = "publish" | "unpublish" | null;
   30: 
   31: function validateResourceForPublishing(
   32:   resource: GeneratedTeachingResource,
   33: ): string[] {
   34:   const issues: string[] = [];
   35: 
   36:   if (!resource.title?.trim()) {
   37:     issues.push("The resource needs a title.");
   38:   }
   39: 
   40:   if (!resource.topic?.trim()) {
   41:     issues.push("The resource needs a topic.");
   42:   }
   43: 
   44:   if (!resource.overview?.trim()) {
   45:     issues.push("The resource needs an overview.");
   46:   }
   47: 
   48:   if (
   49:     !resource.learningObjectives ||
   50:     resource.learningObjectives.length === 0 ||
   51:     resource.learningObjectives.some((objective) => !objective.trim())
   52:   ) {
   53:     issues.push("Add at least one complete learning objective.");
   54:   }
   55: 
   56:   if (
   57:     !resource.successCriteria ||
   58:     resource.successCriteria.length === 0 ||
   59:     resource.successCriteria.some((criterion) => !criterion.trim())
   60:   ) {
   61:     issues.push("Add at least one complete success criterion.");
   62:   }
   63: 
   64:   if (!resource.sections || resource.sections.length === 0) {
   65:     issues.push("Add at least one lesson section.");
   66:   } else {
   67:     const incompleteSection = resource.sections.some(
   68:       (section) =>
   69:         !section.title?.trim() ||
   70:         !section.teacherInstructions?.trim() ||
   71:         !section.studentTask?.trim(),
   72:     );
   73: 
   74:     if (incompleteSection) {
   75:       issues.push(
   76:         "Every lesson section needs a title, teacher instructions and student task.",
   77:       );
   78:     }
   79:   }
   80: 
   81:   if (
   82:     !resource.assessmentQuestions ||
   83:     resource.assessmentQuestions.length === 0
   84:   ) {
   85:     issues.push("Add at least one assessment question.");
   86:   } else {
   87:     const incompleteQuestion = resource.assessmentQuestions.some(
   88:       (question) =>
   89:         !question.question?.trim() ||
   90:         !question.answer?.trim() ||
   91:         question.marks < 1,
   92:     );
   93: 
   94:     if (incompleteQuestion) {
   95:       issues.push(
   96:         "Every assessment question needs a question, model answer and valid mark value.",
   97:       );
   98:     }
   99:   }
  100: 
  101:   return issues;
  102: }
  103: 
  104: export default function ResourcePublishControls({
  105:   resourceId,
  106:   status,
  107:   content,
  108:   onStatusChange,
  109: }: ResourcePublishControlsProps) {
  110:   const [confirmationAction, setConfirmationAction] =
  111:     useState<ConfirmationAction>(null);
  112: 
  113:   const [updating, setUpdating] = useState(false);
  114: 
  115:   const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  116: 
  117:   const [success, setSuccess] = useState<string | null>(null);
  118: 
  119:   const [validationIssues, setValidationIssues] = useState<string[]>([]);
  120: 
  121:   useEffect(() => {
  122:     if (!success) {
  123:       return;
  124:     }
  125: 
  126:     const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
  127:       setSuccess(null);
  128:     }, 4000);
  129: 
  130:     return () => {
  131:       window.clearTimeout(timer);
  132:     };
  133:   }, [success]);
  134: 
  135:   useEffect(() => {
  136:     if (!confirmationAction) {
  137:       return;
  138:     }
  139: 
  140:     function handleKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
  141:       if (event.key === "Escape" && !updating) {
  142:         setConfirmationAction(null);
  143:       }
  144:     }
  145: 
  146:     window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
  147: 
  148:     return () => {
  149:       window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
  150:     };
  151:   }, [confirmationAction, updating]);
  152: 
  153:   function openPublishConfirmation() {
  154:     const issues = validateResourceForPublishing(content);
  155: 
  156:     setError(null);
  157:     setSuccess(null);
  158:     setValidationIssues(issues);
  159:     setConfirmationAction("publish");
  160:   }
  161: 
  162:   function openUnpublishConfirmation() {
  163:     setError(null);
  164:     setSuccess(null);
  165:     setValidationIssues([]);
  166:     setConfirmationAction("unpublish");
  167:   }
  168: 
  169:   function closeConfirmation() {
  170:     if (updating) {
  171:       return;
  172:     }
  173: 
  174:     setConfirmationAction(null);
  175:     setValidationIssues([]);
  176:   }
  177: 
  178:   async function confirmStatusChange() {
  179:     if (!confirmationAction) {
  180:       return;
  181:     }
  182: 
  183:     if (confirmationAction === "publish" && validationIssues.length > 0) {
  184:       return;
  185:     }
  186: 
  187:     const newStatus: ResourceStatus =
  188:       confirmationAction === "publish" ? "published" : "draft";
  189: 
  190:     setUpdating(true);
  191:     setError(null);
  192:     setSuccess(null);
  193: 
  194:     try {
  195:       await updateTeacherResourceStatus(resourceId, newStatus);
  196: 
  197:       onStatusChange(newStatus);
  198: 
  199:       setSuccess(
  200:         newStatus === "published"
  201:           ? "Resource published successfully."
  202:           : "Resource returned to draft.",
  203:       );
  204: 
  205:       setConfirmationAction(null);
  206:       setValidationIssues([]);
  207:     } catch (caughtError) {
  208:       console.error("Failed to update resource status:", caughtError);
  209: 
  210:       setError(
  211:         caughtError instanceof Error
  212:           ? caughtError.message
  213:           : "The resource status could not be updated.",
  214:       );
  215:     } finally {
  216:       setUpdating(false);
  217:     }
  218:   }
  219: 
  220:   return (
  221:     <>
  222:       <div className="space-y-3">
  223:         {status === "archived" ? (
  224:           <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-100 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-slate-600">
  225:             Archived Â· restore this resource from the Content Hub
  226:           </div>
  227:         ) : status === "draft" ? (
  228:           <button
  229:             type="button"
  230:             onClick={openPublishConfirmation}
  231:             className="inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-lg shadow-emerald-100 transition hover:bg-emerald-700 sm:w-auto"
  232:           >
  233:             <Send className="h-4 w-4" />
  234:             Publish resource
  235:           </button>
  236:         ) : (
  237:           <button
  238:             type="button"
  239:             onClick={openUnpublishConfirmation}
  240:             className="inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-amber-700 transition hover:bg-amber-100 sm:w-auto"
  241:           >
  242:             <RotateCcw className="h-4 w-4" />
  243:             Return to draft
  244:           </button>
  245:         )}
  246: 
  247:         {success && (
  248:           <div
  249:             role="status"
  250:             className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 flex max-w-sm items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-200 bg-white p-4 text-emerald-800 shadow-2xl"
  251:           >
  252:             <CheckCircle2 className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0" />
  253: 
  254:             <div>
  255:               <p className="font-bold">Status updated</p>
  256: 
  257:               <p className="mt-1 text-sm">{success}</p>
  258:             </div>
  259: 
  260:             <button
  261:               type="button"
  262:               onClick={() => setSuccess(null)}
  263:               aria-label="Close notification"
  264:               className="ml-2 text-slate-400 transition hover:text-slate-700"
  265:             >
  266:               <X className="h-4 w-4" />
  267:             </button>
  268:           </div>
  269:         )}
  270:       </div>
  271: 
  272:       {confirmationAction && (
  273:         <div
  274:           className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/60 p-4 backdrop-blur-sm"
  275:           role="dialog"
  276:           aria-modal="true"
  277:           aria-labelledby="status-dialog-title"
  278:           onMouseDown={(event) => {
  279:             if (event.target === event.currentTarget) {
  280:               closeConfirmation();
  281:             }
  282:           }}
  283:         >
  284:           <div className="w-full max-w-lg overflow-hidden rounded-3xl bg-white shadow-2xl">
  285:             <header className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 p-6">
  286:               <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
  287:                 <div
  288:                   className={
  289:                     confirmationAction === "publish"
  290:                       ? "flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700"
  291:                       : "flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-amber-100 text-amber-700"
  292:                   }
  293:                 >
  294:                   {confirmationAction === "publish" ? (
  295:                     <FileCheck2 className="h-6 w-6" />
  296:                   ) : (
  297:                     <RotateCcw className="h-6 w-6" />
  298:                   )}
  299:                 </div>
  300: 
  301:                 <div>
  302:                   <h2
  303:                     id="status-dialog-title"
  304:                     className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-950"
  305:                   >
  306:                     {confirmationAction === "publish"
  307:                       ? "Publish resource?"
  308:                       : "Return resource to draft?"}
  309:                   </h2>
  310: 
  311:                   <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
  312:                     {confirmationAction === "publish"
  313:                       ? "Publishing marks this resource as reviewed and ready for future exporting or assignment."
  314:                       : "The resource will no longer be marked as published. Its content will not be deleted."}
  315:                   </p>
  316:                 </div>
  317:               </div>
  318: 
  319:               <button
  320:                 type="button"
  321:                 onClick={closeConfirmation}
  322:                 disabled={updating}
  323:                 aria-label="Close confirmation"
  324:                 className="inline-flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl text-slate-400 transition hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
  325:               >
  326:                 <X className="h-5 w-5" />
  327:               </button>
  328:             </header>
  329: 
  330:             <div className="p-6">
  331:               {confirmationAction === "publish" &&
  332:                 validationIssues.length === 0 && (
  333:                   <div className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 p-4">
  334:                     <p className="font-bold text-emerald-900">Resource ready</p>
  335: 
  336:                     <ul className="mt-3 space-y-2 text-sm text-emerald-800">
  337:                       <li className="flex items-center gap-2">
  338:                         <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
  339:                         Required lesson content is complete
  340:                       </li>
  341: 
  342:                       <li className="flex items-center gap-2">
  343:                         <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
  344:                         Learning objectives and success criteria are present
  345:                       </li>
  346: 
  347:                       <li className="flex items-center gap-2">
  348:                         <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
  349:                         Assessment questions include model answers
  350:                       </li>
  351:                     </ul>
  352:                   </div>
  353:                 )}
  354: 
  355:               {confirmationAction === "publish" &&
  356:                 validationIssues.length > 0 && (
  357:                   <div className="rounded-2xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4">
  358:                     <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
  359:                       <AlertCircle className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-red-600" />
  360: 
  361:                       <div>
  362:                         <p className="font-bold text-red-900">
  363:                           Resource not ready
  364:                         </p>
  365: 
  366:                         <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-red-800">
  367:                           Complete the following items before publishing:
  368:                         </p>
  369:                       </div>
  370:                     </div>
  371: 
  372:                     <ul className="mt-4 space-y-2 pl-8 text-sm text-red-800">
  373:                       {validationIssues.map((issue) => (
  374:                         <li key={issue} className="list-disc">
  375:                           {issue}
  376:                         </li>
  377:                       ))}
  378:                     </ul>
  379:                   </div>
  380:                 )}
  381: 
  382:               {error && (
  383:                 <div
  384:                   role="alert"
  385:                   className="mt-4 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-red-800"
  386:                 >
  387:                   <AlertCircle className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0" />
  388: 
  389:                   <div>
  390:                     <p className="font-bold">Status not updated</p>
  391: 
  392:                     <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6">{error}</p>
  393:                   </div>
  394:                 </div>
  395:               )}
  396:             </div>
  397: 
  398:             <footer className="flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 border-t border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-5 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
  399:               <button
  400:                 type="button"
  401:                 onClick={closeConfirmation}
  402:                 disabled={updating}
  403:                 className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
  404:               >
  405:                 Cancel
  406:               </button>
  407: 
  408:               <button
  409:                 type="button"
  410:                 onClick={() => void confirmStatusChange()}
  411:                 disabled={
  412:                   updating ||
  413:                   (confirmationAction === "publish" &&
  414:                     validationIssues.length > 0)
  415:                 }
  416:                 className={
  417:                   confirmationAction === "publish"
  418:                     ? "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-emerald-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-300"
  419:                     : "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-amber-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-300"
  420:                 }
  421:               >
  422:                 {updating ? (
  423:                   <>
  424:                     <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
  425:                     Updating status...
  426:                   </>
  427:                 ) : confirmationAction === "publish" ? (
  428:                   <>
  429:                     <Send className="h-4 w-4" />
  430:                     Publish resource
  431:                   </>
  432:                 ) : (
  433:                   <>
  434:                     <RotateCcw className="h-4 w-4" />
  435:                     Return to draft
  436:                   </>
  437:                 )}
  438:               </button>
  439:             </footer>
  440:           </div>
  441:         </div>
  442:       )}
  443:     </>
  444:   );
  445: }

FILE: services\teacherContentLibraryService.ts

    1: import {
    2:   addDoc,
    3:   collection,
    4:   deleteDoc,
    5:   doc,
    6:   getDocs,
    7:   query,
    8:   serverTimestamp,
    9:   Timestamp,
   10:   updateDoc,
   11:   where,
   12: } from "firebase/firestore";
   13: 
   14: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
   15: import {
   16:   deleteExamQuestionSet,
   17:   duplicateExamQuestionSet,
   18:   getTeacherExamQuestionSets,
   19:   updateExamQuestionSetStatus,
   20: } from "@/services/examQuestionService";
   21: import {
   22:   deleteTeacherResource,
   23:   duplicateTeacherResource,
   24:   getTeacherResources,
   25:   updateTeacherResourceStatus,
   26: } from "@/services/teacherResourceService";
   27: import type {
   28:   TeacherContentItem,
   29:   TeacherContentKind,
   30:   TeacherContentLifecycle,
   31: } from "@/types/teacherContent";
   32: 
   33: function timestampToDate(value: unknown): Date | null {
   34:   return value instanceof Timestamp ? value.toDate() : null;
   35: }
   36: 
   37: function normaliseLifecycle(value: unknown): TeacherContentLifecycle {
   38:   return value === "published" || value === "archived" ? value : "draft";
   39: }
   40: 
   41: export function teacherContentKindLabel(kind: TeacherContentKind): string {
   42:   if (kind === "teaching-resource") return "Teaching Resource";
   43:   if (kind === "ai-quiz") return "AI Quiz";
   44:   return "Exam Question Set";
   45: }
   46: 
   47: export async function getTeacherContentLibrary(
   48:   teacherId: string,
   49: ): Promise<TeacherContentItem[]> {
   50:   const id = teacherId.trim();
   51:   if (!id) return [];
   52: 
   53:   const [resources, examSets, quizSnapshot] = await Promise.all([
   54:     getTeacherResources(id),
   55:     getTeacherExamQuestionSets(id),
   56:     getDocs(query(collection(db, "generatedQuizzes"), where("teacherId", "==", id))),
   57:   ]);
   58: 
   59:   const resourceItems: TeacherContentItem[] = resources.map((resource) => ({
   60:     key: `teaching-resource:${resource.id}`,
   61:     id: resource.id,
   62:     teacherId: resource.teacherId,
   63:     kind: "teaching-resource",
   64:     title: resource.title,
   65:     description: resource.content.overview || "",
   66:     topic: resource.topic,
   67:     qualification:
   68:       resource.yearGroup === "A Level" ||
   69:       resource.yearGroup === "Year 12" ||
   70:       resource.yearGroup === "Year 13"
   71:         ? "A_LEVEL"
   72:         : "GCSE",
   73:     examBoard: resource.examBoard,
   74:     yearGroup: resource.yearGroup,
   75:     difficulty: resource.difficulty,
   76:     lifecycle: normaliseLifecycle(resource.status),
   77:     questionCount: resource.content.assessmentQuestions.length,
   78:     totalMarks: resource.content.assessmentQuestions.reduce(
   79:       (total, question) => total + (Number(question.marks) || 0),
   80:       0,
   81:     ),
   82:     createdAt: resource.createdAt,
   83:     updatedAt: resource.updatedAt,
   84:     openHref: `/teacher/resources/${resource.id}`,
   85:     assignHref:
   86:       resource.status === "published"
   87:         ? `/teacher/assignment-wizard?contentType=teaching-resource&contentId=${encodeURIComponent(resource.id)}`
   88:         : null,
   89:   }));
   90: 
   91:   const examItems: TeacherContentItem[] = examSets.map((set) => ({
   92:     key: `exam-paper:${set.id}`,
   93:     id: set.id,
   94:     teacherId: set.teacherId,
   95:     kind: "exam-paper",
   96:     title: set.title,
   97:     description: `${set.questionCount} original exam-style questions worth ${set.totalMarks} marks.`,
   98:     topic: set.topic,
   99:     qualification: set.qualification,
  100:     examBoard: set.examBoard,
  101:     yearGroup: "",
  102:     difficulty: set.difficulty,
  103:     lifecycle: normaliseLifecycle(set.status),
  104:     questionCount: set.questionCount,
  105:     totalMarks: set.totalMarks,
  106:     createdAt: set.createdAt,
  107:     updatedAt: set.updatedAt,
  108:     openHref: `/teacher/question-bank/${set.id}`,
  109:     assignHref:
  110:       set.status === "published"
  111:         ? `/teacher/assignment-wizard?contentType=exam-paper&contentId=${encodeURIComponent(set.id)}`
  112:         : null,
  113:   }));
  114: 
  115:   const quizItems: TeacherContentItem[] = quizSnapshot.docs.map((quizDocument) => {
  116:     const data = quizDocument.data();
  117:     const questions = Array.isArray(data.questions) ? data.questions : [];
  118:     const lifecycle = normaliseLifecycle(data.status);
  119: 
  120:     return {
  121:       key: `ai-quiz:${quizDocument.id}`,
  122:       id: quizDocument.id,
  123:       teacherId: typeof data.teacherId === "string" ? data.teacherId : id,
  124:       kind: "ai-quiz",
  125:       title: typeof data.title === "string" ? data.title : "Untitled Quiz",
  126:       description: typeof data.description === "string" ? data.description : "",
  127:       topic: typeof data.topicId === "string" ? data.topicId : "",
  128:       qualification: typeof data.qualification === "string" ? data.qualification : "GCSE",
  129:       examBoard: typeof data.examBoard === "string" ? data.examBoard : "",
  130:       yearGroup: "",
  131:       difficulty: typeof data.difficulty === "string" ? data.difficulty : "standard",
  132:       lifecycle,
  133:       questionCount:
  134:         typeof data.questionCount === "number" ? data.questionCount : questions.length,
  135:       totalMarks: null,
  136:       createdAt: timestampToDate(data.createdAt),
  137:       updatedAt: timestampToDate(data.updatedAt),
  138:       openHref: `/teacher/quiz-library/${quizDocument.id}`,
  139:       assignHref:
  140:         lifecycle === "published"
  141:           ? `/teacher/assignment-wizard?quizId=${encodeURIComponent(quizDocument.id)}`
  142:           : null,
  143:     };
  144:   });
  145: 
  146:   return [...resourceItems, ...quizItems, ...examItems].sort(
  147:     (a, b) =>
  148:       (b.updatedAt ?? b.createdAt ?? new Date(0)).getTime() -
  149:       (a.updatedAt ?? a.createdAt ?? new Date(0)).getTime(),
  150:   );
  151: }
  152: 
  153: export async function updateTeacherContentLifecycle(
  154:   item: TeacherContentItem,
  155:   lifecycle: TeacherContentLifecycle,
  156: ): Promise<void> {
  157:   if (item.kind === "teaching-resource") {
  158:     await updateTeacherResourceStatus(item.id, lifecycle);
  159:     return;
  160:   }
  161: 
  162:   if (item.kind === "exam-paper") {
  163:     await updateExamQuestionSetStatus(item.id, lifecycle);
  164:     return;
  165:   }
  166: 
  167:   await updateDoc(doc(db, "generatedQuizzes", item.id), {
  168:     status: lifecycle,
  169:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  170:   });
  171: }
  172: 
  173: export async function duplicateTeacherContent(
  174:   item: TeacherContentItem,
  175:   teacherId: string,
  176: ): Promise<string> {
  177:   const id = teacherId.trim();
  178:   if (!id) throw new Error("A teacher account is required.");
  179: 
  180:   if (item.kind === "teaching-resource") {
  181:     return duplicateTeacherResource(item.id, id);
  182:   }
  183: 
  184:   if (item.kind === "exam-paper") {
  185:     return duplicateExamQuestionSet(item.id, id);
  186:   }
  187: 
  188:   const snapshot = await getDocs(
  189:     query(collection(db, "generatedQuizzes"), where("teacherId", "==", id)),
  190:   );
  191:   const source = snapshot.docs.find((docItem) => docItem.id === item.id);
  192:   if (!source) throw new Error("The quiz could not be found in your library.");
  193: 
  194:   const data = source.data();
  195:   const reference = await addDoc(collection(db, "generatedQuizzes"), {
  196:     ...data,
  197:     title: `${item.title} (Copy)`,
  198:     status: "draft",
  199:     createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
  200:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  201:   });
  202:   return reference.id;
  203: }
  204: 
  205: export async function deleteTeacherContent(item: TeacherContentItem): Promise<void> {
  206:   if (item.lifecycle !== "archived") {
  207:     throw new Error("Archive content before deleting it permanently.");
  208:   }
  209: 
  210:   if (item.kind === "teaching-resource") {
  211:     await deleteTeacherResource(item.id);
  212:     return;
  213:   }
  214: 
  215:   if (item.kind === "exam-paper") {
  216:     await deleteExamQuestionSet(item.id);
  217:     return;
  218:   }
  219: 
  220:   await deleteDoc(doc(db, "generatedQuizzes", item.id));
  221: }

  108:   "teacherResources";
  109: 
  110: function timestampToDate(
  111:   value: unknown,
  112: ): Date | null {
  113:   if (
  114:     value instanceof Timestamp
  115:   ) {
  116:     return value.toDate();
  117:   }
  118: 
  119:   return null;
  120: }
  121: 
  122: function requireSignedInTeacherId(): string {
  123:   const teacherId =
  124:     auth.currentUser?.uid;
  125: 
  126:   if (!teacherId) {
  127:     throw new Error(
  128:       "A signed-in teacher account is required.",
  129:     );
  130:   }
  131: 
  132:   return teacherId;
  133: }
  134: 
  135: async function requireOwnedTeacherResource(
  136:   resourceDocumentId: string,
  137: ): Promise<{
  138:   teacherId: string;
  139:   reference: ReturnType<typeof doc>;
  140:   data: FirestoreTeacherResource;
  141: }> {
  142:   const cleanedResourceId =
  143:     resourceDocumentId.trim();
  144: 
  145:   if (!cleanedResourceId) {
  146:     throw new Error(
  147:       "A valid teaching resource is required.",
  148:     );
  149:   }
  150: 
  151:   const teacherId =
  152:     requireSignedInTeacherId();
  153: 
  154:   const reference = doc(
  155:     db,
  156:     COLLECTION_NAME,
  157:     cleanedResourceId,
  158:   );
  159: 
  160:   const snapshot =
  161:     await getDoc(reference);
  162: 
  163:   if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  164:     throw new Error(
  165:       "The teaching resource could not be found.",
  166:     );
  167:   }
  168: 
  169:   const data =
  170:     snapshot.data() as
  171:       FirestoreTeacherResource;
  172: 
  173:   if (
  174:     !data.teacherId ||
  175:     data.teacherId !== teacherId
  176:   ) {
  177:     throw new Error(
  178:       "You do not have permission to modify this teaching resource.",
  179:     );
  180:   }
  181: 
  182:   return {
  183:     teacherId,
  184:     reference,
  185:     data,
  186:   };
  187: }
  188: 
  189: export async function saveTeacherResource(
  190:   teacherId: string,
  191:   resource:
  192:     GeneratedTeachingResource,
  193: ): Promise<string> {
  194:   const signedInTeacherId =
  195:     requireSignedInTeacherId();
  196: 
  197:   const cleanedTeacherId =
  198:     teacherId.trim();
  199: 
  200:   if (!cleanedTeacherId) {
  201:     throw new Error(
  202:       "A signed-in teacher is required to save this resource.",
  203:     );
  204:   }
  205: 
  206:   /*
  207:    * Prevent a caller from saving a resource under
  208:    * another teacher's UID.
  209:    */
  210:   if (
  211:     cleanedTeacherId !==
  212:     signedInTeacherId
  213:   ) {
  214:     throw new Error(
  215:       "You cannot save a resource for another teacher account.",
  216:     );
  217:   }
  218: 
  219:   const existingQuery = query(
  220:     collection(
  221:       db,
  222:       COLLECTION_NAME,
  223:     ),
  224:     where(
  225:       "teacherId",
  226:       "==",
  227:       signedInTeacherId,
  228:     ),
  229:     where(
  230:       "sourceResourceId",
  231:       "==",
  232:       resource.id,
  233:     ),
  234:   );
  235: 
  236:   const existingSnapshot =
  237:     await getDocs(
  238:       existingQuery,
  239:     );
  240: 
  241:   if (
  242:     !existingSnapshot.empty
  243:   ) {
  244:     throw new Error(
  245:       "This resource has already been saved to your library.",
  246:     );
  247:   }
  248: 
  249:   const documentReference =
  250:     await addDoc(
  251:       collection(
  252:         db,
  253:         COLLECTION_NAME,
  254:       ),
  255:       {
  256:         teacherId:
  257:           signedInTeacherId,
  258: 
  259:         sourceResourceId:
  260:           resource.id,
  261: 
  262:         title:
  263:           resource.title,
  264: 
  265:         topic:
  266:           resource.topic,
  267: 
  268:         resourceType:
  269:           resource.resourceType,
  270: 
  271:         yearGroup:
  272:           resource.yearGroup,
  273: 
  274:         examBoard:
  275:           resource.examBoard,
  276: 
  277:         duration:
  278:           resource.duration,
  279: 
  280:         difficulty:
  281:           resource.difficulty,
  282: 
  283:         content:
  284:           resource,
  285: 
  286:         status:
  287:           "draft",
  288: 
  289:         createdAt:
  290:           serverTimestamp(),
  291: 
  292:         updatedAt:
  293:           serverTimestamp(),
  294:       },
  295:     );
  296: 
  297:   return documentReference.id;
  298: }
  299: 
  300: export async function getTeacherResources(
  301:   teacherId: string,
  302: ): Promise<
  303:   SavedTeacherResource[]
  304: > {
  305:   const signedInTeacherId =
  306:     requireSignedInTeacherId();
  307: 
  308:   const cleanedTeacherId =
  309:     teacherId.trim();
  310: 
  311:   if (!cleanedTeacherId) {
  312:     return [];
  313:   }
  314: 
  315:   /*
  316:    * Do not allow this client-side service to become
  317:    * a directory for another teacher's private content.
  318:    */
  319:   if (
  320:     cleanedTeacherId !==
  321:     signedInTeacherId
  322:   ) {
  323:     throw new Error(
  324:       "You cannot access another teacher's resource library.",
  325:     );
  326:   }
  327: 
  328:   const resourcesQuery =
  329:     query(
  330:       collection(
  331:         db,
  332:         COLLECTION_NAME,
  333:       ),
  334:       where(
  335:         "teacherId",
  336:         "==",
  337:         signedInTeacherId,
  338:       ),
  339:       orderBy(
  340:         "createdAt",
  341:         "desc",
  342:       ),
  343:     );
  344: 
  345:   const snapshot =
  346:     await getDocs(
  347:       resourcesQuery,
  348:     );
  349: 
  350:   return snapshot.docs.map(
  351:     (
  352:       resourceDocument,
  353:     ) => {
  354:       const data =
  355:         resourceDocument.data() as
  356:           FirestoreTeacherResource;
  357: 
  358:       /*
  359:        * Defensive validation.
  360:        *
  361:        * The Firestore query should already guarantee
  362:        * this, but we do not return a malformed record
  363:        * whose owner does not match the current user.
  364:        */
  365:       if (
  366:         data.teacherId !==
  367:         signedInTeacherId
  368:       ) {
  369:         throw new Error(
  370:           "A resource ownership mismatch was detected.",
  371:         );
  372:       }
  373: 
  374:       return {
  375:         id:
  376:           resourceDocument.id,
  377: 
  378:         teacherId:
  379:           data.teacherId,
  380: 
  381:         sourceResourceId:
  382:           data.sourceResourceId,
  383: 
  384:         title:
  385:           data.title,
  386: 
  387:         topic:
  388:           data.topic,
  389: 
  390:         resourceType:
  391:           data.resourceType,
  392: 
  393:         yearGroup:
  394:           data.yearGroup,
  395: 
  396:         examBoard:
  397:           data.examBoard,
  398: 
  399:         duration:
  400:           data.duration,
  401: 
  402:         difficulty:
  403:           data.difficulty,
  404: 
  405:         content:
  406:           data.content,
  407: 
  408:         status:
  409:           data.status,
  410: 
  411:         createdAt:
  412:           timestampToDate(
  413:             data.createdAt,
  414:           ),
  415: 
  416:         updatedAt:
  417:           timestampToDate(
  418:             data.updatedAt,
  419:           ),
  420:       };
  421:     },
  422:   );
  423: }
  424: 
  425: export async function updateTeacherResourceStatus(
  426:   resourceDocumentId: string,
  427:   status:
  428:     TeacherResourceStatus,
  429: ): Promise<void> {
  430:   const {
  431:     reference,
  432:   } =
  433:     await requireOwnedTeacherResource(
  434:       resourceDocumentId,
  435:     );
  436: 
  437:   await updateDoc(
  438:     reference,
  439:     {
  440:       status,
  441:       updatedAt:
  442:         serverTimestamp(),
  443:     },
  444:   );
  445: }

  492: export async function duplicateTeacherResource(
  493:   resourceDocumentId: string,
  494:   teacherId: string,
  495: ): Promise<string> {
  496:   const signedInTeacherId =
  497:     requireSignedInTeacherId();
  498: 
  499:   const cleanedTeacherId =
  500:     teacherId.trim();
  501: 
  502:   if (
  503:     !cleanedTeacherId ||
  504:     cleanedTeacherId !==
  505:       signedInTeacherId
  506:   ) {
  507:     throw new Error(
  508:       "You cannot duplicate content into another teacher's library.",
  509:     );
  510:   }
  511: 
  512:   const {
  513:     data: source,
  514:   } =
  515:     await requireOwnedTeacherResource(
  516:       resourceDocumentId,
  517:     );
  518: 
  519:   const stamp =
  520:     Date.now();
  521: 
  522:   const duplicatedTitle =
  523:     `${source.title} (Copy)`;
  524: 
  525:   const reference =
  526:     await addDoc(
  527:       collection(
  528:         db,
  529:         COLLECTION_NAME,
  530:       ),
  531:       {
  532:         teacherId:
  533:           signedInTeacherId,
  534: 
  535:         sourceResourceId:
  536:           `${source.sourceResourceId}-copy-${stamp}`,
  537: 
  538:         title:
  539:           duplicatedTitle,
  540: 
  541:         topic:
  542:           source.topic,
  543: 
  544:         resourceType:
  545:           source.resourceType,
  546: 
  547:         yearGroup:
  548:           source.yearGroup,
  549: 
  550:         examBoard:
  551:           source.examBoard,
  552: 
  553:         duration:
  554:           source.duration,
  555: 
  556:         difficulty:
  557:           source.difficulty,
  558: 
  559:         content: {
  560:           ...source.content,
  561: 
  562:           id:
  563:             `${source.content.id}-copy-${stamp}`,
  564: 
  565:           title:
  566:             duplicatedTitle,
  567:         },
  568: 
  569:         status:
  570:           "draft",
  571: 
  572:         createdAt:
  573:           serverTimestamp(),
  574: 
  575:         updatedAt:
  576:           serverTimestamp(),
  577:       },
  578:     );
  579: 
  580:   return reference.id;
  581: }
  582: 
  583: export async function deleteTeacherResource(
  584:   resourceDocumentId: string,
  585: ): Promise<void> {
  586:   const {
  587:     reference,
  588:     data,
  589:   } =
  590:     await requireOwnedTeacherResource(
  591:       resourceDocumentId,
  592:     );
  593: 
  594:   /*
  595:    * Permanent deletion is intentionally restricted
  596:    * to archived resources.
  597:    */
  598:   if (
  599:     data.status !==
  600:     "archived"
  601:   ) {
  602:     throw new Error(
  603:       "Archive this resource before deleting it permanently.",
  604:     );
  605:   }
  606: 
  607:   await deleteDoc(
  608:     reference,
  609:   );
  610: }
